We then connect these ideal collective dynamics to microscopic interactions and realistic solid-state systems, discussing how interaction strength, disorder, decoherence, and experimental control affect sensing performance. Different platforms and experiments are compared to highlight key material properties, advantages, and limitations. The tutorial focuses on a materials-oriented understanding of nonlinear collective-spin sensing, rather than a comprehensive comparison of experimental platforms or a practical guide to sensing experiments.

</details>


### [105] [Fermionic Gaussian Scrooge Ensembles in Deep Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01209)
*Ning Sun,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"费米子高斯Scrooge系综"，用以描述自由费米子系统中深度热化所生成的投影系综，并在SYK$_2$模型与具电荷守恒的随机高斯电路中验证其普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对多体波函数的部分测量会产生未测子系统的纯态投影系综。已有研究表明，混沌系统中该系综普适收敛为与平均密度矩阵相容的最大随机系综（Scrooge系综）；而自由费米子系统是非混沌的，其态被约束为高斯态。为刻画这类系统中的深度热化，需要构建一个与之匹配的系综描述。

Method: 引入费米子高斯Scrooge系综，将其定义为对高斯Haar系综按平均密度矩阵所作的畸变，并通过其矩/replica结构揭示高斯态在复制的Hilbert空间中的扩大对称性。验证分两部分：(1) 解析证明SYK$_2$模型的投影系综在任意演化时刻都服从该系综；(2) 数值给出具电荷守恒的随机高斯电路中长时间极限下该系综出现的证据。

Result: 解析上证明SYK$_2$模型投影系综在任意时刻满足费米子高斯Scrooge系综；数值上表明其在具电荷守恒的随机高斯电路足够长时间后涌现；其矩结构显示出高斯态在复制空间中的扩大对称性。

Conclusion: 费米子高斯Scrooge系综可作为自由费米子系统中深度热化的普适描述。

Abstract: Measuring part of a many-body wave function generates a projected ensemble of pure quantum states on the unmeasured subsystem. Recent advances in deep thermalization have shown that, in chaotic systems, this ensemble universally converges to the maximally random ensemble compatible with its average density matrix, known as the Scrooge ensemble. By contrast, free-fermion systems are nonchaotic, and their quantum states are constrained to remain Gaussian. Motivated by this distinction, we introduce the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble to describe deep thermalization in generic free-fermion systems. This ensemble is defined as a distortion of the Gaussian Haar ensemble by the average density matrix, and its moments reveal an enlarged symmetry of Gaussian states in the replicated Hilbert space. We demonstrate the emergence of the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble in two complementary settings: (1) we analytically prove that the projected ensemble of the SYK$_2$ model follows the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble at arbitrary evolution times; (2) we provide numerical evidence that it emerges at sufficiently long times in random Gaussian circuits with charge conservation. Our results establish the fermionic Gaussian Scrooge ensemble as a universal description of deep thermalization in free-fermion systems.

</details>


### [106] [Conditioning on Subalgebras - Entropy Duality and Generalized Quantum Stein's Lemma in von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00772)
*Bjarne Bergh,Li Gao,Mizanur Rahaman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为任意冯诺依曼代数的子代数建立了条件熵的对偶关系，涵盖Umegaki相对熵、夹层Rényi散度与Petz-Rényi散度，并将其推广至完全无限维情形，进而给出有限指数子代数假设下的量子Stein引理无限维推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件熵对偶关系主要局限于有限维张量积子系统，缺乏对一般子代数及无限维冯诺依曼代数的统一处理，且相对熵到子代数的操作解释也仅有有限维结果。

Method: 在任意冯诺依曼代数的子代数框架下，对Umegaki相对熵、夹层Rényi散度与Petz-Rényi散度建立对偶关系；将有限维张量积子系统的对偶性推广到一般子代数并提升至无限维；以该对偶性为工具，在备择假设为有限指数子代数状态空间时证明量子Stein引理的无限维版本。

Result: 成功建立了上述相对熵与Rényi散度相对子代数的对偶关系；证明了对子代数的相对熵可作为复合量子假设检验中最优渐近误差指数，即量子Stein引理在有限指数子代数情形下的无限维推广，这是首个超越有限维的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了条件熵对偶理论至一般子代数与无限维冯诺依曼代数，并首次给出子代数相对熵的无限维操作解释，为复合量子假设检验提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We establish duality relations for conditional entropies relative to subalgebras of arbitrary von Neumann algebras, including the Umegaki relative entropy as well as the sandwiched- and Petz-Rényi divergences. Even in finite dimensions, these results extend the familiar dualities for conditional entropies from tensor-product subsystems to general subalgebras, while also lifting the framework to the fully infinite-dimensional setting. As an application, we establish an infinite-dimensional extension of quantum Stein's lemma when the alternative hypothesis is the state space of a finite-index subalgebra, which gives an operational interpretation of the relative entropy to a subalgebra as the optimal asymptotic error exponent in composite quantum hypothesis testing-the first beyond finite dimensions.

</details>


### [107] [Causal Trade-Offs in Superluminal Extensions of Relativity. A no-go theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00494)
*Amrapali Sen,Matthias Salzger,V. Vilasini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立因果建模框架研究Dragan-Ekert超光速参照系提案，证明一个no-go定理：允许超光速参照系的理论若要避免超光速信号，必须严格限制可访问时空区域、使参照系选择具有可观测后果，或令自由变量不存在或其存在依赖于参照系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dragan和Ekert近期提出通过引入"客观不确定性"来调和相对论对称性与量子不确定性，允许存在超光速参照系。本文旨在形式化检验该提案，厘清超光速扩展相对论是否真能避免超光速信号这一根本冲突。

Method: 采用因果建模（causal modelling）框架对超光速参照系提案进行形式化分析，并证明一个no-go定理。

Result: 证明该提案若允许超光速参照系，则只有在满足以下至少一项时才能避免超光速信号：（1）严重限制操作协议可访问的时空区域；（2）参照系选择产生可观测后果；（3）自由变量不存在，或其存在依赖于参照系。结果表明Dragan-Ekert的客观不确定性方案本身并不足以消除该冲突。

Conclusion: 该no-go结果超越了"超光速变换可能导致超光速信号"的直觉；由于可通过放松其他基本假设来避免超光速信号，所提出的客观不确定性可能根本不必要。研究揭示了基于不确定性的解决方案的局限以及超光速相对论扩展面临的因果权衡。

Abstract: Extensions of relativity that include superluminal reference frames have recently been proposed by Dragan and Ekert as a way to reconcile relativistic symmetry with quantum uncertainty. In this work, we develop a causal modelling framework to formally study this proposal and prove a no-go theorem demonstrating that a theory permitting superluminal reference frames can avoid superluminal signalling only if it severely constrains which spacetime regions are accessible to operational protocols, if the choice of reference frame has observable consequences or if free variables do not exist or their existence depends on the reference frame. Crucially, our result goes beyond the familiar intuition that superluminal transformations may lead to superluminal signalling; our no-go result shows that Dragan and Ekert's proposal of introducing objective uncertainty does not, by itself, eliminate this conflict. In fact, because superluminal signalling might instead be avoided by relaxing other fundamental assumptions in the presence of superluminal reference frames, the proposed objective uncertainty may not be necessary at all. Our results clarify the limitations of uncertainty-based resolutions and the causal trade-offs faced by superluminal extensions of relativity.

</details>


### [108] [Achieving Identical Stored Energy in Cascaded Collisional Quantum Battery Charging: Analytical Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01332)
*Longyan Gong,Jing Zhang,Yongtao Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在级联碰撞模型框架下研究双量子比特量子电池，通过对充电器施加自适应测量获得两个期望储能量相等的条件电池态：充电器处于激发态时可完全抑制轨迹依赖的储能涨落；处于一般叠加态时，因依赖电池相位的递归动力学而无法完全抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同试验间充电均匀性是可靠量子电池的基本要求，但量子内禀随机性从根本上威胁这一目标。

Method: 在级联碰撞模型框架下构建双量子比特量子电池，在每次碰撞后对充电器实施自适应测量，得到期望储能量相等的两个条件电池态，并解析分析激发态与一般叠加态充电器下的涨落抑制行为，辅以数值模拟验证。

Result: 对激发态充电器，解析证明可完全抑制轨迹依赖的储能涨落；对一般叠加态充电器，理论揭示由于依赖电池相位的递归动力学会从根本上禁止完全抑制。数值模拟与理论预测在定量上高度吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了实现高均匀性量子电池充电的可行途径。

Abstract: Charging uniformity across distinct trials is an essential requirement for reliable quantum batteries. Nevertheless, intrinsic quantum stochasticity poses a fundamental challenge to this goal. This work investigates a two-qubit quantum battery within the framework of cascaded collision models. We propose an adaptive measurement on the charger after each collision, yielding two conditional battery states of equal expected stored energy. For chargers prepared in the excited state, we analytically prove that complete suppression of trajectory-dependent stored-energy fluctuations is attainable; for chargers prepared in general superposition states, our theory reveals that full suppression is fundamentally prohibited owing to battery-phase-dependent recursive dynamics. Numerical simulations are in excellent quantitative agreement with theoretical predictions. These findings provide a feasible pathway toward high-uniformity quantum-battery charging.

</details>


### [109] [Hidden-State Updates and observable-record composition in retrocausal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00357)
*D. M. Theshan N. Weerasinghe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将隐变量模型的可复合性问题分解为两个组合性要求，用归一化缺陷生成的**不变空间**刻画自适应策略下的单位连续质量，用**观测空间**刻画联合读出下隐制备的不可区分性，并给出有限状态与Wrapped-Cauchy源的具体反例及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐变量模型虽可重现单个上下文无关的读数，但难以在装置连接或重复使用时保持可观测记录的一致性。需要区分并形式化"组合"究竟在哪些意义上失败，从而给出可检验的判据。

Method: 对有限族非负instrument矩阵，构造由归一化缺陷张成的不变空间，其零化子定义在结果条件更新下封闭的极大容许正锥，刻画任意有限自适应策略下的单位延续质量；另设观测空间刻画联合读出下上下文相关隐制备的不可区分性。结合精确有理数计算、穷举有限策略实验、连续源采样与求积检验所述例子。

Result: 给出一个有限状态例子：通过所有固定序列，但在结果依赖的停止策略下失败；对具有明确"源不变被动延拓"的wrapped-Cauchy自旋源，两个各自公平的探针产生设置依赖的一致性概率，对比度为 ε²·sech(2g)/4。更一般地，在单次与两次相同使用的保护下，连续二元被动响应必为常数，但可测的二值例外仍可能存在。

Conclusion: 该判据适用于指定的instrument族与可访问记录，不构造微观探测器，也不确立在无限制网络复合下的一致性。

Abstract: A hidden-variable model can reproduce context-independent individual readouts while failing to preserve observable records when devices are connected or reused. We distinguish two composition requirements. For a finite family of nonnegative instrument matrices, an invariant space generated by normalization defects characterizes unit continuation mass under every finite adaptive policy. Its annihilator defines the maximal admissible positive cone closed under outcome-conditioned updates. A separate observation space characterizes indistinguishability of context-dependent hidden preparations under joint readout. A finite-state example passes every fixed sequence but fails under outcome-dependent stopping. For a wrapped-Cauchy spin source with an explicitly stipulated unchanged-source passive extension, two individually fair probes acquire a setting-dependent agreement probability with contrast $ε^2\operatorname{sech}(2g)/4$. More generally, protection under one and two identical uses forces a continuous binary passive response to be constant; measurable two-valued exceptions remain possible. Exact rational calculations, exhaustive finite-policy experiments, continuous source sampling, and quadrature test the stated examples. The criteria apply to specified instrument families and accessible records; they do not construct a microscopic detector or establish consistency under unrestricted network composition.

</details>


### [110] [Exact first-detection probability in a locally monitored solvable quantum circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01703)
*Cecilia De Fazio,Igor Lesanovsky,Gabriele Perfetto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究确定性Floquet量子East模型中局域频闪测量与酉量子线路动力学的相互作用，通过更新方程将首次探测概率与子系统Loschmidt echo联系起来，给出了首次探测概率的精确解析结果，并揭示出由测量时间与弛豫时间竞争所决定的两种统计区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局域测量反作用如何重塑相互作用量子多体系统的探测统计，是一个核心但缺乏精确刻画的问题；作者希望在可解的Floquet量子East模型中给出严格的解析描述。

Method: 针对确定性Floquet量子East模型，研究局域频闪测量与酉线路动力学的相互作用；建立连接首次探测概率与子系统Loschmidt echo的更新方程，后者编码被监测量子比特的有效开放动力学，从而精确解析求解首次探测到某子集量子比特回到初态的概率。

Result: 统计呈现两种由测量探测时间与酉线路弛豫时间尺度竞争决定的区域：稀疏监测导致几何型首次探测统计；频繁监测则诱发关联，表现为偏离几何行为。局域协议中返回概率受被监测子区域大小控制，可通过局域中电路测量在数字量子模拟器上获取。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了局域测量反作用重塑相互作用量子多体系统探测统计的罕见精确刻画，并指出其局域协议可在数字量子模拟器上通过中电路测量实现。

Abstract: We study the interplay between local stroboscopic measurements and the unitary quantum circuit dynamics of the deterministic Floquet-quantum East model. We derive exact analytical results for the probability of first detecting a subset of qubits back in their initial state. This is achieved via a renewal equation linking the first-detection probability to the subsystem Loschmidt echo, which encodes the effective open dynamics of the monitored qubits. The resulting statistics exhibit two regimes set by the competition between the measurement probing time and the relaxation timescale of the unitary circuit dynamics: while infrequent monitoring leads to geometric first-detection statistics, frequent monitoring induces correlations that manifest as deviations from this geometric behavior. These results provide a rare exact characterization of how local measurement backaction reshapes detection statistics in an interacting quantum many-body system. In our local protocol, return probabilities are controlled by the size of the monitored subregion and can therefore be accessed on digital quantum simulators via local mid-circuit measurements.

</details>


### [111] [A Quantum Phase Neural Network with Multi-Inputs/Single-Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00868)
*Shuang Cong,Jinmin Yang,Sajede Harraz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出35输入/单输出量子相位神经网络(QPNN)用于识别7×5像素的字母A和B，通过量子相位编码、旋转门与受控NOT门构建网络，推导含自适应学习率的相位权重训练算法，并在Qiskit上验证零误差识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子神经网络需生成全部2^35个复指数振幅，面临"指数墙"瓶颈，难以支撑多输入识别任务的实用化，因此需要一种避免穷举振幅的相位驱动实现方案。

Method: 网络每节点输入为0或1；经归一化与量子相位编码，将每组35位数字输入映射为由35个量子相位描述的量子叠加态；用量子旋转门引入可调相位，用受控NOT门纠缠两输入间的相对相位；推导输入-输出关系，并给出带自适应学习率的相位权重学习训练算法。

Result: 设计了识别概率为1的解，给出所有零误差可调相位解的解析表达式（解不唯一）；在Qiskit平台上完成实验验证，性能得到确认。

Conclusion: 相位驱动将概率计算转化为含可调旋转角的解析多项式函数，规避了生成全部2^35复指数振幅的瓶颈，为"指数墙"问题提供了有效解法，并为多输入识别问题的实际应用提供了新的实现路径。

Abstract: A 35 input/single output quantum phase neural network (QPNN) is proposed to recognize the English letters' A 'and' B 'composed 7*5 of pixels. Each node of the proposed QPNN has the input with 0 or 1. Through normalization and quantum phase encoding, each set of 35 with 0 or 1 digital inputs is encoded into a quantum superposition state described by 35 quantum phases. Then, a quantum rotation gate is used to introduce adjustable phase, and a controlled NOT gate is used to entangle the relative phase between the two inputs. The relationship between the input and output of the network is derived. This paper also derived the phase weight learning training algorithm with adaptive learning rate. The solutions with the recognition probability of 1 is designed, and the analytical expressions for all adjustable phase solutions with zero errors are not unique. The experimental performance is verified on the Qiskit platform. This paper uses phase drive to convert the probability calculation into an analytical polynomial function with adjustable rotation angles in the network, avoiding the bottleneck of generating all 2^35 complex exponential amplitudes and providing an effective way to solve the "exponential wall" problem, which provides a new implementation solution for the practical application of multi-input recognition problems.

</details>


### [112] [Finite-blocklength classical communication over the quantum erasure channel with and without classical feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00440)
*Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文确定了无共享纠缠、带无噪经典反馈时，量子擦除信道传输固定数目经典消息的最优成功概率为 E[min{1,d^K/M}]，其中 K~Binomial(n,1-p)，并给出可达协议、无反馈的假设检验逆定理、指数分析及相关比较结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子擦除信道在有限码长、固定消息数场景下的精确通信性能，特别是经典反馈是否能提升成功概率，以及反馈辅助与无反馈情形之间的差距。

Method: 利用基于经典控制固定技巧证明的无噪量子通信维度界，建立允许自适应量子编码器、量子存储和接收仪器的逆定理；构造重复数字并等待确认的经典协议达到上界；通过擦除信道输出的相对优超性质与假设检验逆定理得到无反馈界；并进行数值比较（如四次qubit擦除信道、四条消息的严格差距，以及四面体qubit态乘积码与经典二进制擦除码比较）。

Result: 最优成功概率精确等于 E[min{1,d^K/M}]，K 为参数 (n,1-p) 的二项随机变量；该界对任意整数 M 可由经典协议达到。无反馈时相同数值界可由假设检验逆定理得到，但不一定可达，四次qubit擦除信道和四条消息存在严格差距；采用四面体qubit态的乘积态码优于同参数下所有经典二进制擦除码。给出显式消息数公式，并表明有界余项正态近似与平均成功强逆指数在无反馈时不变；确定容量以下的反馈辅助错误指数，证明其在临界速率以上与无反馈指数一致，并给出低速率下的无反馈界。

Conclusion: 经典反馈可为量子擦除信道上的有限长度经典消息传输带来精确的最优成功概率，且在某些参数下严格优于无反馈方案；无反馈情形虽具有相同数值的逆定理界，但未必可达，而量子编码可显著超越经典编码。反馈辅助错误指数在临界速率以上与无反馈情形一致。

Abstract: We determine the optimal success probability for transmitting a fixed number of classical messages through a finite number of uses of the quantum erasure channel, assisted by noiseless classical feedback and without initial shared entanglement. For an input dimension $d$, an erasure probability $p$, a blocklength $n$, and $M$ equiprobable messages, the optimal success probability is equal to $E[\min\{1,d^K/M\}]$, where $K$ is binomial with parameters $n$ and $1-p$. The converse allows arbitrary adaptive quantum encoders, quantum memories, and receiver instruments. Its main ingredient is an elementary dimension bound for noiseless quantum communication with classical feedback, proved by fixing the classical controls without conditioning the sender's state on the observed transcript. A classical protocol that transmits the base-$d$ digits of an integer representing the message, repeating each digit until the receiver acknowledges its reception or the prescribed blocklength is reached, attains the bound for every integer $M$. We also establish a relative-majorization property of erasure-channel outputs and exactly evaluate a hypothesis-testing converse, recovering the same numerical bound without feedback. That converse need not be achievable without feedback: four uses of the qubit erasure channel and four messages give a strict gap. A product-state code employing tetrahedral qubit states nevertheless outperforms every classical binary erasure code with these parameters. We give an explicit message-size formula and show that the bounded-remainder normal approximation and the average-success strong-converse exponent are unchanged without feedback. We also determine the feedback-assisted error exponent below capacity, prove that it agrees with the no-feedback exponent above a critical rate, and give no-feedback bounds at lower rates.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum Secret Sharing and Error Correction vs No-Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00441)
*Steven Chien,Ishani Mukherjee,Mark Zhandry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出多拷贝量子秘密分享（k-copy QSS）变体，突破单拷贝no-cloning障碍，给出t>(n-k+1)/2的构造与t≤(n-1)/k的Clique Path障碍，二者在k=2时精确匹配，并推广至擦除信道量子纠错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典秘密分享可实现任意访问结构且门限方案高效，但量子情形下no-cloning定理严重限制可行性：门限QSS当且仅当t>n/2可行。作者希望放宽输入（提供k份相同拷贝、只需恢复一份）来绕过单拷贝no-cloning障碍，并探究no-cloning是否仍精确刻画多拷贝可行性。

Method: 定义k-copy QSS：输入k份相同态，输出仅需恢复一份。对门限情形，利用no-cloning得到t≤n/(k+1)不可行的下界；构造性证明t>(n-k+1)/2可行；提出适用于任意访问结构的新障碍Clique Path obstruction，对门限给出t≤(n-1)/k不可行。核心工具是访问结构导出图的k-可着色性。

Result: 得到多拷贝QSS的上下界：可行条件t>(n-k+1)/2，不可行条件t≤(n-1)/k，且k=2时两界精确吻合。发现no-cloning不再精确刻画多拷贝情形（存在略微超越单拷贝障碍的可行区域）。该结果可平移至擦除信道下的量子纠错，给出类似障碍。

Conclusion: 多拷贝可略微超越单拷贝no-cloning障碍，但no-cloning不再精确刻画可行性。k=2时上下界完全匹配，k≥3的间隙闭合是未来有吸引力的方向；结果同时为擦除信道量子纠错提供障碍。

Abstract: Secret sharing is ubiquitous throughout cryptography. All possible access structures are classically feasible, and in the case of threshold access structures, the protocols are even very efficient. However, when moving to the quantum setting, the no-cloning theorem shows that many access structures are impossible. In fact, no-cloning exactly characterizes feasibility: for thresholds, quantum secret sharing is possible if and only if the threshold $t$ is strictly more than $n/2$.
  In this work, we propose a variant of quantum secret sharing (QSS) where two or more identical copies of the input state are provided, but the output is only required to recover one copy. This notion circumvents the simple one-copy no-cloning obstruction, though the natural $k$-copy generalization still gives much milder obstructions. For thresholds using $k$ copies, no-cloning implies that QSS is impossible whenever $t\leq n/(k+1)$.
  It is tempting to hypothesize that no-cloning continues to exactly characterize the many-copy case. However, we show that this is not the case. We give positive results showing that multiple copies allow for going slightly beyond the single-copy obstruction: for thresholds, we construct QSS whenever $t>(n-k+1)/2$. On the other hand, we give a novel obstruction we call the Clique Path obstruction, which applies to arbitrary access structures. For thresholds, it shows that QSS is impossible whenever $t\leq (n-1)/k$. Our upper and lower bounds exactly match for $k=2$. Both our results leverage connections to the $k$-colorability of certain graphs derived from the access structure. We leave closing the gap for $k\geq 3$ copies as a fascinating direction for future work.
  Secret sharing is closely related to error correction, which can also be considered in the many-copy setting. Our results imply similar obstructions for quantum error correction for erasure channels.

</details>


### [114] [Robust classical and quantum communication over almost-iid channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00442)
*Liuhang Ye,Bjarne Bergh,Nilanjana Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究沿固定量子信道 Φ 的 Choi-近似-iid 信道序列的渐近容量，证明其通用无辅助经典与量子容量等于 Φ 本身的容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渐近通信理论用 Φ^{⊗n} 描述信道的 n 次使用，即 iid 范式，但精确张量幂行为是理想化假设。作者希望考察当信道序列“几乎是 iid”时，Φ 的渐近容量是否仍可实现，从而放宽对严格张量幂结构的要求。

Method: 定义沿 Φ 的 Choi-近似-iid 信道序列：其归一化 Choi 态在 Mazzola-Sutter-Renner (MSR) 意义下沿 Φ 的归一化 Choi 态近似-iid，缺陷参数 r_n=o(n)。该定义与量子信道指数 de Finetti 定理中出现的近似-iid 类相对应；作者在该类上构造通用编码方案。

Result: 证明沿 Φ 的 Choi-近似-iid 信道类，其通用无辅助经典容量与量子容量均等于信道 Φ 的相应容量。通用性体现在类内：对低于相关容量的每个速率，存在仅依赖 Φ 和该速率、而不依赖具体信道序列的码序列，使该类中每个序列都达到错误概率消失。

Conclusion: 精确 iid（张量幂）假设可被放宽为 Choi-近似-iid 结构而不损失无辅助经典与量子容量；MSR 近似-iid 类为量子信道提供了与 de Finetti 定理相容的通用容量可达性框架。

Abstract: The independent and identically distributed (iid) paradigm for asymptotic communication through quantum channels describes $n$ uses of a channel $Φ$ by $Φ^{\otimes n}$. Since exact tensor-power behaviour is an idealization, we ask whether the asymptotic capacities of $Φ$ remain achievable for channel sequences that are almost-iid along $Φ$. We consider sequences of Choi-almost-iid channels whose normalized Choi states are Mazzola-Sutter-Renner (MSR) almost-iid along the normalized Choi state of $Φ$, with defect parameters $r_n=o(n)$. In Renner's exponential de Finetti theorem for quantum states, almost-iid states appear in the approximating mixture and motivate the MSR almost-iid class. Choi-almost-iid channels play the corresponding role in an exponential de Finetti theorem for quantum channels established in a companion paper. In this paper, we prove that the universal unassisted classical and quantum capacities of the class of Choi-almost-iid channels along $Φ$ equal those of the channel $Φ$. Here universality is within this class: for each rate below the relevant capacity, a code sequence depending on $Φ$ and the rate, but not on the particular channel sequence, achieves vanishing error for every sequence in the class.

</details>


### [115] [Approximation theorems for fermionic Gaussian states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01860)
*Amir-Reza Negari,Farzin Salek,Zoltán Zimborás,Aram Harrow,Patrick Hayden,Jens Eisert*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在费米子高斯态的Majorana协方差矩阵层面，用量化的两点关联"一夫一妻"约束，统一导出高次图哈密顿量的乘积态近似、有限费米高斯de Finetti定理以及空间态的恢复性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体物理中"足够分散的相互作用或关联导致平均场描述"是反复出现的原理，但对费米子高斯态缺乏定量刻画。作者希望直接在协方差矩阵层面给出该原理的定量版本。

Method: 核心观察：费米子协方差矩阵的可容许条件对两点关联施加定量monogamy约束——固定区域对众多不相交区域的协方差"预算"有界。据此分三条路线展开：高次正则图上二次费米哈密顿量的基态能量与有限温自由能乘积态近似（误差O(D^{-1/2})）；分次费米拷贝置换下k-copy高斯态的n-可交换性与单拷贝边缘乘积的迹范数接近度O(k/n)；互信息指数聚类的空间费米高斯态中，跨缓冲区的条件互信息衰减O(r^{-1})及其经可恢复性的O(r^{-1/2})迹范数近似。

Result: (1) D-正则图上二次费米哈密顿量基态能量与自由能的乘积态近似误差为O(D^{-1/2})；(2) 有限费米高斯de Finetti定理：n-可交换k-copy高斯态在迹范数下O(k/n)接近单拷贝边缘乘积，无限可交换蕴含精确乘积结构；(3) 互信息指数聚类的空间态条件互信息按O(r^{-1})衰减，恢复态给出O(r^{-1/2})迹范数近似。

Conclusion: 乘积近似、de Finetti近似与可恢复性估计对费米高斯系统均可直接从协方差层面的结构导出，表明协方差矩阵的monogamy约束是统一机制。

Abstract: A recurring principle in many-body physics is that sufficiently distributed interactions or correlations lead to an effectively mean-field description. For fermionic Gaussian states, we establish a quantitative version of this principle directly at the level of Majorana covariance matrices. The main observation is that the admissibility condition for fermionic covariance matrices imposes a quantitative monogamy constraint on two-point correlations: a fixed region has only a bounded covariance budget to distribute among many disjoint regions. We use this principle in three directions. First, for quadratic fermionic Hamiltonians on finite simple graphs of high degree, we obtain product-state approximations to both the ground-state energy and the finite-temperature free energy, with error of order \(D^{-1/2}\) for \(D\)-regular graphs. Second, we prove a finite fermionic Gaussian de Finetti theorem: a \(k\)-copy Gaussian state that is \(n\)-exchangeable under graded fermionic copy permutations is \(O(k/n)\)-close in trace norm to the product of its one-copy marginal. In particular, infinite exchangeability within the Gaussian class implies exact product structure. Third, for spatial fermionic Gaussian states with exponentially clustering mutual information, we prove that the conditional mutual information across a separating buffer decays as \(O(r^{-1})\), and hence, by recoverability, the state admits an \(O(r^{-1/2})\) trace-norm approximation by a recovered state. These results show that product and de Finetti approximations and recoverability estimates for fermionic Gaussian systems can all be derived directly from covariance-level structure.

</details>


### [116] [Dissipation-Enabled Operator-Norm Locality Bounds for Bose-Hubbard Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01669)
*Marius Lemm,Tim Möbus*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 局域耗散可恢复玻色格点系统的算符范数Lieb–Robinson界，并对耗散Bose–Hubbard模型（ℓ>2光子损耗）给出近乎弹道的LR界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭合玻色格点系统一般不存在对初态一致的算符范数Lieb–Robinson界，因为信息传播速度随局域玻色占据数增长，可宏观巨大；希望像量子自旋系统那样获得与输入态一致的信息传播约束。

Method: 考虑由Lindbladian描述的耗散Bose–Hubbard模型，含在位ℓ光子损耗（ℓ>2）；利用耗散快速耗尽高占据格点，在局域粒子矩的Sobolev型尺度上正则化状态，并推导矩界（时间趋零时发散但在低阶可积）。进一步推广至cat-code耗散，并在码空间初态下证明体积一致的局域绝热近似。

Result: 证明耗散系统存在算符范数Lieb–Robinson界，主要结果给出近乎弹道的LR界；矩界在t→0发散但低阶可积；对cat-code耗散，码空间初态得到体积一致局域绝热近似。

Conclusion: 由该LR界可得局域信道近似、热力学极限与高效数字量子模拟，这些应用均可对输入态一致成立，类似量子自旋系统，而闭合Bose–Hubbard系统不具备此性质。

Abstract: Closed bosonic lattice systems do not generally admit operator norm Lieb--Robinson bounds uniformly in the initial state. The reason is that the information propagation velocity can grow with the local boson occupancy, which can be macroscopically large. Here, we show that local dissipation restores an operator-norm Lieb--Robinson bound. We consider the dissipative Bose--Hubbard model described by a Lindbladian operator with on-site $\ell$-photon loss, $\ell>2$ and, for our main result, we establish an almost-ballistic Lieb-Robinson bound. Dissipation rapidly depletes highly occupied sites, thus regularizing the state on the Sobolev-type scale of local particle moments. The resulting moment bounds diverge as time approaches zero but remain integrable near zero at sufficiently low orders. We extend these ideas to treat cat-code dissipation and, for initial states in the code space, we prove a local adiabatic approximation uniform in the total volume. Further consequences include local channel approximation, a thermodynamic limit, and efficient digital quantum simulation. The point is that these applications are now available \textit{uniformly in the input state}, as for quantum spin systems, but in contrast to closed Bose--Hubbard systems.

</details>


### [117] [Vacuum entanglement in a time-dependent electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01394)
*Álvaro Álvarez-Domínguez,Luis J. Garay,Mercedes Martín-Benito,Iker Sanz-González*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过构造两个不相交区域中的局域可观测量，分析带电标量场真空态关联，并将其时间演化与Schwinger效应中的粒子-反粒子产生联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究外部电场背景下量子真空态关联的演化，理解电场如何影响不同区域间的关联结构及其与粒子产生的关系。

Method: 在两个不相交区域构造局域可观测量，考虑时间依赖的均匀电场，将关联的时间演化与Schwinger效应导致的粒子-反粒子产生相关联。

Result: 电场增加态的混合度并增强总关联，但区域间可提纯纠缠减少；在非微扰对产生机制下，足够强脉冲时纠缠在有限时间内消失。外部背景诱导的各向异性使关联分布呈现非平凡的方向依赖性。

Conclusion: 外电场显著改变量子真空的关联与纠缠结构，强脉冲可导致区域间可提纯纠缠在有限时间内完全消失，且关联分布具有方向依赖性。

Abstract: We analyze the correlations of the vacuum state of a charged scalar field by constructing localized observables in two disjoint regions. We consider a time-dependent homogeneous electric field and examine the time evolution of correlations by relating it with the particle-antiparticle creation through the Schwinger effect. Our results show that the electric field increases the mixedness of the states and enhances total correlations, while the distillable entanglement among the regions decreases, vanishing at a finite time for sufficiently intense pulses within the non-perturbative pair-production regime. We also discuss the effect of anisotropy induced by the external background, showing that the distribution of correlations acquires a nontrivial directional dependence.

</details>


### [118] [Flexible rigidity: Compiling generic trusted-preparation protocols into sequential device-independent protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00444)
*Amir Arqand,Srijita Kundu,Ernest Y. -Z. Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出通用编译器，将一类可信态制备的器件相关协议转化为顺序器件无关协议，并基于Rényi散度构造新型刚性定理，实现恒定速率与恒定噪声容忍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的刚性定理基于迹距离或保真度，存在根本性缩放障碍，难以在器件重复使用场景下同时保持恒定速率与恒定噪声容忍。本文旨在通过新的散度工具克服该障碍，推动可信态制备协议向器件无关协议的高效转化。

Method: 构造通用编译器：要求可信制备协议具备纠缠实现，且该实现对应满足弱刚性条件的非局域博弈最优策略。技术核心是建立基于Rényi散度的新一类刚性定理；顺序通用情形下证明依赖Fawzi与Fawzi(2021)的sharp-divergence链式法则；无记忆器件特殊情形下，通过推导修正的channel sandwiched散度次可加性提升速率；并证明Brown、Fawzi与Fawzi(2021)迭代均值散度的链式法则。

Result: 编译器应用于不可克隆加密、点函数防复制及盲随机性扩展，给出相应的顺序器件无关协议。新刚性定理达到恒定速率（可信制备态与器件使用次数之比为常数），并容忍每次器件使用的恒定噪声水平。无记忆情形下速率进一步改进。

Conclusion: 基于Rényi散度的刚性定理为器件无关协议提供了灵活密码学应用与恒定速率、恒定噪声容忍的保障，克服了传统迹距离/保真度刚性定理的缩放障碍；相关散度链式法则与次可加性结果具有独立价值。

Abstract: We give a generic compiler for turning a wide class of device-dependent protocols with trusted state preparation into sequential device-independent protocols. In order for our compiler to apply, the trusted-preparation protocol needs to satisfy certain minimal conditions: having an entanglement-based realization in the first place, and the entanglement-based realization corresponding to the optimal strategy of any non-local game that satisfies a fairly weak rigidity condition. We apply our generic compiler to give sequential device-independent protocols for uncloneable encryption, copy-protection of point functions, and blind randomness expansion.
  Our technical contribution is a new class of rigidity theorems based on Rényi divergences, with flexible cryptographic applications. They allow our compiler to achieve a constant rate (i.e., the ratio of trusted states prepared to device uses), while tolerating a constant level of noise per device usage. This overcomes a fundamental scaling obstacle faced by previous rigidity theorems for trace distance or fidelity. For the generic sequential case, our proof is based on the sharp-divergence chain rule by Fawzi and Fawzi (2021), which suffices for a constant but fairly low rate. In the special case when the devices retain no memory, we improve the rates by deriving a novel subadditivity property for a modified channel sandwiched divergence, which may be of independent interest. Finally, as a prospect for future improvements, we also prove a chain rule for the iterated-mean divergences from Brown, Fawzi and Fawzi (2021).

</details>


### [119] [Learning Local Fermionic Lindbladians under Parity Superselection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00448)
*Tim Möbus,Daniel Stilck França,Cambyse Rouzé*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究在宇称超选择约束下，通过纯偶制备与测量及短时连续演化，学习多模Lindblad生成元（马约拉纳耗散动力学）的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇称超选择规则禁止直接测量奇马约拉纳可观测量，因此无法直接刻画含奇算符的Lindblad生成元。作者希望在仅使用偶宇称制备与测量、无外部辅助模式、且不需要已知非零系数位置的前提下，仍能学习到含奇项的Lindblad动力学，并给出样本复杂度保证。

Method: 利用内部标记（markers）将奇探针转为偶可观测量，通过对三个分离标记进行配对测量来标定其动力学贡献；采用带符号Fierz反演恢复标准系数，并用半定规划拟合保证生成元合法（完全正、迹保持）。针对有限程模型在已知有界度图上的情形，以及在无已知图、每模一个惰性辅助模与可能非局域配对操作的情形，分别给出样本复杂度与误差界。

Result: 在已知有界度图、合适标记接入并给定加权强度界的情况下，达到逐元素误差ε需Õ(ᾱ²ε⁻²log(m/δ))个样本；将误差提升为diamond范数误差ε需额外m²因子，与短时新鲜系统实验的下界在常数与对数因子内匹配。无已知图时，总量子绝对系数误差至多每模ε，需Õ_k(ᾱ²d²m^{⌊k/2⌋}ε⁻²log(m/δ))个样本，前提是给定近似系数度界d与受控的弱系数尾部。所有保证以至少1-δ概率成立。对几何模型，固定支撑偶可观测量的预测具有关于系统规模对数的样本复杂度；并给出有限体积与指数尾部扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了在宇称超选择约束下学习含奇马约拉纳项的Lindblad生成元的严格样本复杂度上下界，表明短时偶制备-测量实验可达到近最优效率，且在不依赖外部辅助模式与已知非零系数位置的条件下依然可行，为量子耗散过程层析提供了理论与算法框架。

Abstract: Parity superselection forbids direct measurement of odd Majorana observables. We learn time-independent, parity-covariant, $k$-mode-local Lindbladians on $m$ modes using even preparations and measurements with uninterrupted short-time evolution. Internal markers turn odd probes into even observables, and pair measurements among three separated markers calibrate their dynamical contributions. Signed Fierz inversion recovers canonical coefficients, while semidefinite fitting yields a valid generator. For finite-range models on known bounded-degree graphs with suitable marker access and a supplied weighted-strength bound $\barα$, entrywise error $\varepsilon$ is achieved using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\barα^2\varepsilon^{-2}\log(m/δ))$ samples, without external modes or known nonzero coefficient locations. Recovery to diamond-norm error $\varepsilon$ costs an additional factor $m^2$, matching lower bounds for short-time experiments on fresh systems up to logarithmic and fixed geometric factors. Without a supplied graph, one idle ancillary mode per system mode and potentially nonlocal pair operations give total absolute coefficient error at most $\varepsilon$ per mode using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}_k(\barα^2\mathsf d^2m^{\lfloor k/2\rfloor}\varepsilon^{-2}\log(m/δ))$ samples, under a supplied approximate coefficient-degree bound $\mathsf d$ and controlled weak-coefficient tails. All guarantees hold with probability at least $1-δ$. For geometric models, fixed-support even observables can be predicted with logarithmic system-size sample complexity at fixed time and accuracy. We also give finite-volume and exponential-tail extensions.

</details>


### [120] [Universal Bound and Phase Transition in Many-Body Fermionic Non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02075)
*Masahiro Hoshino,Ryota Matsuda,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 以magic Rényi熵(MRE)度量费米子非高斯性，证明其普适上界与Haar均值(最大密度ln(4/3)/Majorana)，并揭示SYK演化中MRE密度的拐点型一级转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子非高斯性是通用量子计算的重要资源，可由量子多体相互作用产生，但缺乏对其上界、典型态值及动力学增长机制的系统刻画。

Method: 以MRE作为非高斯性度量，解析推导其普适上界与Haar随机态均值，并比较具有相同协方差矩阵的态的MRE差异；在SYK哈密顿量下对高斯态做虚时与实时演化，通过改变时长扫描MRE密度。

Result: 典型Haar随机态在大系统极限达到最大MRE密度ln(4/3)/Majorana；相同协方差矩阵的态之间MRE可呈广泛差异；SYK演化中出现MRE密度拐点及四副本置换对称性自发破缺所标记的一级转变。

Conclusion: 该非高斯性转变是态的定性变化，不能由热自由能反映，表明非高斯性是独立于热力学量的量子资源维度。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a resource for universal quantum computation that can be generated by interactions in quantum many-body systems. Using the magic Rényi entropy (MRE) as a measure of non-Gaussianity, we derive its universal upper bound and Haar mean, proving that typical Haar-random states attain the maximal MRE density of $\ln(4/3)$ per Majorana in the large-system limit. We also show that the MRE can differ extensively between states with identical covariance matrices. To investigate how non-Gaussianity grows toward this maximal density, we evolve a Gaussian state in imaginary and real time under the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev Hamiltonian. By varying the imaginary- and real-time durations, we identify a first-order transition marked by a kink in the MRE density and spontaneous breaking of the permutation symmetry among the four copies used to evaluate the MRE. This transition represents a qualitative change in the non-Gaussianity of the state that is not reflected in the thermal free energy.

</details>


### [121] [Floquet-Universal Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01878)
*Emilio Onorati,Harriet Apel,Michael M. Wolf,Toby Cubitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了 Floquet 模拟理论，用周期性驱动哈密顿量合成时间无关哈密顿量，并给出 Floquet-通用哈密顿量的完整构造性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟与数字哈密顿量模拟分别依赖时间无关哈密顿量与完全可控的时间相关量子线路两种不同动力学机制；时间周期哈密顿量代表二者的中间机制，但此前缺乏系统的 Floquet 模拟理论。

Method: 通过周期性驱动哈密顿量实现 Floquet 模拟，证明相互作用集合 S 可 Floquet-模拟 Lie(S) 中的每个哈密顿量，并据此构造性地刻画 Floquet-通用哈密顿量；构造仅使用 O(1) 的局域相互作用强度及其比值。

Result: 给出 Floquet-通用哈密顿量的完整构造性刻画；避免模拟模拟中常见的多尺度局域相互作用强度，仅需多项式增长的驱动频率范围（对 k-local 晶格哈密顿量等重要类）；导出若干 BQP-完全性与 QMA-困难性结果。

Conclusion: 周期驱动哈密顿量可在中间机制下高效合成时间无关哈密顿量，为 Floquet 物理中的自然物理性质与量提供复杂度理论意义。

Abstract: Analogue and digital Hamiltonian simulation represent two distinct approaches to making one quantum system replicate the physical properties of another, with consequences ranging from practical applications to complexity theory and quantum gravity. They are rooted in two distinct regimes of quantum dynamics: time-independent Hamiltonians for the former; fully controllable time-dependent quantum circuits in the latter. Time-periodic Hamiltonians represent an intermediate regime. In this work, we establish a theory of Floquet simulation where we use periodically driven Hamiltonians to synthesise time-independent ones.
  We show that a set of interactions $S$ can Floquet-simulate every Hamiltonian in the Lie algebra $\mathrm{Lie}(S)$, and consequently we give a complete and constructive characterisation of Floquet-universal Hamiltonians that are able to produce any target Hamiltonian. Notably, our construction uses only $O(1)$ local interaction strengths and ratios thereof, avoiding the impractical multi-scale local interaction strengths often required in analogue simulation via time-independent Hamiltonians. Instead, the construction requires a range of driving frequencies which scales polynomially with system size for important classes such as $k$-local lattice Hamiltonians. This has immediate complexity-theoretic implications, including numerous BQP-completeness and QMA-hardness results for natural physical properties and quantities studied in Floquet physics.

</details>


### [122] [Strong Simulation of 1D Quantum Circuits via Reduced Transition Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02082)
*Matilde Grassi,Stefano Carignano,Luca Tagliacozzo,Jacopo De Nardis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Sweeping RTM算法，用横向张量网络收缩近似强模拟一维混沌砖墙电路在有限相对精度下的指定输出概率，数值发现所需键维随演化时间呈亚指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态在混沌幺正电路下直接演化时，空间纠缠的快速增长严重限制了张量网络模拟。作者转而针对一个更聚焦的任务：以有限相对精度近似强模拟一维混沌砖墙电路的指定输出概率，从而绕开纠缠增长带来的瓶颈。

Method: 给定输入与输出比特串y和x，计算p(x|y)=|⟨x|U(T)|y⟩|²。采用Sweeping RTM算法，即基于约化转移矩阵（RTM）的横向张量网络收缩，联合压缩左、右时间边界态，寻求在不同空间切割处及键维增大时均收敛的输出概率。

Result: 在一维混沌砖墙电路、固定相对目标精度下，数值证据表明获得稳定估计所需键维在可及时间窗口内呈亚指数增长。

Conclusion: 该工作为混沌量子电路的经典概率查询开辟了直接路径，并有望应用于基准测试与学习任务。

Abstract: Tensor networks are powerful tools for simulating quantum many-body systems, but the growth of spatial entanglement severely limits the direct evolution of pure states under chaotic unitary circuits. Here we consider a more targeted task: the approximate strong simulation of a specified output probability of a 1D chaotic brick-wall circuit at finite relative precision. Given input and output bit strings $\by$ and $\bx$, we evaluate $p(\bx|\by)=|\bra{\bx}U(T)\ket{\by}|^2$ using the Sweeping RTM algorithm, a transverse tensor-network contraction based on reduced transition matrices (RTMs). The algorithm compresses the left and right temporal boundary states jointly, seeking an output probability that converges across spatial cuts and as the bond dimension is increased. For chaotic one-dimensional brick-wall circuits at a fixed relative target precision, we find numerical evidence that the bond dimension required to obtain stable estimates grows subexponentially over the accessible time window. Our findings open a direct route to classical probability queries for chaotic quantum circuits, with potential applications to benchmarking and learning tasks.

</details>


### [123] [Avoiding Exponentially Large Groups with Open Quantum System Technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01932)
*Jihong Cai,Advith Govindarajan,Marius Junge*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过引入开放量子系统技术，论文识别出O(n^3)维的小李群及单一相互作用哈密顿量，在poly(n)复杂度下实现态制备、密度矩阵及量子信道的通用逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统态制备与量子信道实现需要在整个n量子比特酉群上操作，成本高昂。论文旨在利用开放量子系统的全部能力（部分迹、环境态制备、群操作），用更小的代数结构替代庞大的酉群，从而降低态、密度与信道操控的复杂度。

Method: 在(n+3)量子比特上构造由局部Pauli串李代数导出的小李群G，其李代数维数为poly(n)。通过重复使用部分迹、环境中的态制备及群操作，从任意固定输入态逼近任意态；进一步识别单个开放系统魔相互作用哈密顿量，其酉群与上述开放系统技术结合可在所有密度矩阵空间上实现传递性；再用更大环境逼近n量子比特上的所有信道。

Result: 证明了任意态均可由固定输入态经部分迹与群操作逼近；单个相互作用哈密顿量结合开放系统技术实现密度空间传递性；所有n量子比特信道均可由更大环境逼近，实现信道通用性。识别出由局部Pauli串导出的、维数为poly(n)的小李群。

Conclusion: 开放量子系统框架下的小群与单一哈密顿量足以实现态、密度与信道的通用逼近，并由此引出一个关于“廉价态/密度/信道”与“昂贵态/密度/信道”的新分类图景，为低成本量子控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to state preparation by embracing the full power of open quantum systems. Instead of working with the whole unitary group in $n$ qubits, we identify a small Lie group $G$ in $(n+3)$ qubits. The dimension of its Lie algebra is $\text{poly}(n)$. Every state can be approximated starting from any fixed input state by repeatedly using partial trace and state preparation in the environment alongside group operations. Moreover, we also identify a single (open system magic) interaction Hamiltonian whose unitary group can be combined with open system quantum technology mentioned above to achieve transitivity on the space of all densities. Similarly, we show that all channels on $n$ qubits can be approximated by using a larger environment, achieving channel universality. The interesting small groups we identify are derived from Lie algebras of local Pauli strings and lead to a new landscape of cheap and expensive states, densities, and channels.

</details>


### [124] [Factor Code Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00469)
*Abhisek Sahu,Jeremy van der Heijden,Mark Van Raamsdonk,Rana Zibakhsh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"因子码网络"概念，研究量子码等距与单射算子映射构成的网络何时能被提升，并给出树形网络与菱形网络的判定条件及相关交换性、生成性与交性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子码由等距T定义，研究其算子映射φ构成的*-同态，可将逻辑算子系统嵌入物理算子系统。作者关心多个码分层嵌套（按偏序集索引）时，能否在保持等距复合关系的同时为每层配上单射算子映射，使整体形成一致的代数结构，从而推广单码情形并刻画网络提升的可能性。

Method: 给出算子映射φ为*-同态且满足φ(O)T=T O，并指出单射性等价于dim H_code整除dim H_phys；定义因子码为(T,φ)且φ(B(H_code))为von Neumann因子子代数。对偏序集索引的码网络{T_ji}定义因子码网络要求单射映射满足φ_kj φ_ji=φ_ki。随后按网络拓扑分类讨论：树结构利用Hilbert空间维数整除条件构造；菱形网络用T_42 H_2与T_43 H_3的主夹角及其重数条件判定。对不可比情形考察两子代数交换性、联合生成B(H_j)及交为标量倍；对i<j_1,j_2<k考察交等于φ_ki(B(H_i))及与B(H_k)构成非退化交换方的条件。

Result: 证明：具有树结构的任意码网络，只要满足Hilbert空间维数的整除条件即可提升为因子码网络；但一般网络不成立。对菱形网络，提升可能当且仅当T_42 H_2与T_43 H_3之间的所有主夹角的重数都被H_1的维数整除。进一步刻画了不可比子代数何时交换、何时生成整个B(H_j)、何时仅交于恒等倍数，以及在链式条件下两子代数交等于φ_ki(B(H_i))并可与B(H_k)构成非退化交换方的充要条件。

Conclusion: 整除性条件是树形码网络提升的充分条件，但对一般拓扑（如菱形）需额外的主夹角重数整除条件。该工作将单码的因子码概念推广到网络，给出提升可行性及子代数相对位置的代数判据，为量子码网络的算子代数结构提供系统刻画。

Abstract: For a quantum code defined by an isometry $T:H_{\rm code} \to H_{\rm phys}$, an operator map is a *-homomorphism $\varphi: B(H_{\rm code}) \to B(H_{\rm phys})$ from the space of operators on the logical Hilbert space to the space of operators on the physical Hilbert space satisfying $\varphi({\cal O}) T = T {\cal O}$ for every operator ${\cal O}$ on $H_{\rm code}$. This can additionally be taken to be unital $\varphi(I_{code}) = I_{phys}$ if and only if $\dim H_{\rm code}$ divides $\dim H_{\rm phys}$. In this case, $\varphi(B(H_{\rm code})$ is a von Neumann factor subalgebra of $B(H_{\rm phys})$ and we call the pair $(T,\varphi)$ a {\it factor code}.
  A code network is a family of Hilbert spaces $\{{\cal H}_i\}$ indexed by a partially ordered set with isometries $T_{ji}:{\cal H}_i \to {\cal H}_j$ for $i < j$ satisfying $T_{kj} T_{ji} = T_{ki}$ for $i < j < k$. In this paper, we ask when this can be promoted to a {\it factor code network} by defining unital operator maps $\varphi_{ji}$ so that $(T_{ji}, \varphi_{ji})$ is a factor code and $\varphi_{kj} \varphi_{ji} = \varphi_{ki}$. We show that the divisibility conditions on Hilbert space dimensions are sufficient for any code network with a tree structure but not in general. For a diamond-shaped network, we show that promotion is possible if and only if the principle angles between $T_{42} H_2$ and $T_{43} H_3$ all have multiplicity divisible by $H_1$.
  For incomparable $i_1,i_2 < j$, we characterize when $\varphi_{j i_1} (B(H_{i_1}))$ and $\varphi_{j i_2} (B(H_{i_2}))$ commute, when they generate all of $B(H_j)$, and when they intersect only on multiples of the identity. For $i < j_1,j_2 < k$, we characterize when $\varphi_{k j_1} (B(H_{j_1})) \cap \varphi_{k j_2} (B(H_{j_2})) = \varphi_{k i} (B(H_{i}))$ and when these algebras together with $B(H_k)$ form a nondegenerate commuting square.

</details>


### [125] [Stationarity as a One-Mode Constraint on Quantum Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02090)
*Itai Panas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文论证定态量子演化在能量标量性质上的"单一性"，提出reg-HF框架下的一模排除公理，并以氦原子为最小检验：参数自由的库仑载体$-δ/e$与有限Z插入$8δ/9$重现类氦系数和能量，同时展示宽非单分母谱与标量能量可精确矩压缩的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定态量子态虽有单一动力学相位因子与标量能量，其内部响应却可跨越巨大希尔伯特空间。作者关注的不是绝对相位的数值或可观测性，而是定态时间演化的"单一性"——即为何微观谱的复杂性不破坏能量层面的单模充分性。

Method: 在正则化Hartree--Fock（reg-HF）中，先建立与定态平均场的联系再做单模约化：由高斯HF表示诱导的原始积相对坐标高斯测度确定协方差，该协方差下同时为最小Fisher信息与最大Shannon微分熵分布、且是Stam界的等号情形；库仑算符容许相容的高斯尺度分辨，位力完备化将正则化对位移转化为定态相容响应；进而由定态性引出对末端标量关联响应的一模约束，并设定一模排除公理：正交于保留对模的响应分量所携带协方差不计入末端标量能量性质；用氦原子作最小非平凡检验，并做显式Bethe--Goldstone计算。

Result: 参数自由的保留库仑载体$-δ/e$与有限Z插入$8δ/9$密切重现独立的类氦系数与能量；显式Bethe--Goldstone计算显示出宽的非单分母返回谱，尽管其标量能量可被精确矩压缩。

Conclusion: 结果将微观谱复杂性与一模能量充分性区分开来：定态能量的标量简洁性不要求底层响应是一维的，而是可由一模排除公理在能量层面实现，氦原子验证了该分离的合理性。

Abstract: A stationary quantum state has one dynamical phase factor and one scalar energy even when its internal response spans a large Hilbert space. Our argument concerns not the value or observability of an absolute phase, but the one-ness of stationary time evolution. In regularized Hartree--Fock (reg-HF), the connection to the stationary mean field is established before any one-mode reduction: the primitive-product relative-coordinate Gaussian measure induced by the Gaussian HF representation fixes a covariance and, at that covariance, is simultaneously the minimum-Fisher-information and maximum-Shannon-differential-entropy distribution and the equality case of the Stam bound. The Coulomb operator admits a compatible Gaussian scale resolution, and virial completion converts the resulting regularized pair displacement into a stationary-compatible response. Stationarity motivates a one-mode constraint on the terminal scalar correlation response. The decisive reg-HF hypothesis is a one-mode exclusion axiom: covariance carried by response components orthogonal to the retained pair mode is excluded from the terminal scalar energy property, without asserting that the unrestricted response is one dimensional. Helium provides the minimal nontrivial test. The parameter-free retained Coulomb carrier $-δ/e$ and finite-$Z$ insertion $8δ/9$ closely reproduce independent He-like coefficients and energies, while an explicit Bethe--Goldstone calculation displays a broad non-single-denominator return spectrum even though its scalar energy is exactly moment-compressible. The result separates microscopic spectral complexity from one-mode energetic sufficiency.

</details>


### [126] [A No-Go Theorem for Order-Two Clifford Electric-Magnetic Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02097)
*Shunta Takahashi,Zhi Li,Beni Yoshida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了环面码中电磁对偶（电-磁交换）能否在微观上实现为二阶Clifford操作，证明Z2环面码不可能存在二阶的保局域Clifford实现，而ZN环面码在奇数N时可实现、偶数N时不可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电磁对偶在任意子类型层面是二阶的，并对应Hadamard型逻辑变换，但这一对偶的群律是否忠实地"提升"到微观Clifford实现尚不清楚，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 通过几何论证，将证明本地化为电弦与磁弦不可避免的交叉（类似不可定向流形中的交叉帽），对Z2环面码给出任意保局域Clifford实现不可能为二阶的普适证明；并将问题推广到ZN环面码进行分析。证明不依赖平移对称性、受限系统尺寸或顶点-面稳定子之间的固定配对。

Result: 对Z2环面码，任何保局域Clifford实现电-磁交换的操作都不可能是二阶的；对ZN环面码，奇数N存在二阶Clifford实现，而偶数N则不可能。

Conclusion: 涌现电磁对偶的群律不必忠实地提升到其微观Clifford实现。

Abstract: Electromagnetic duality in the toric code has order two at the level of anyon types and acts as a Hadamard-type logical transformation on the encoded quantum information. Here, we ask whether it can likewise be realized microscopically as an order-two Clifford operation. For the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ toric code, we prove that any locality-preserving Clifford realization of electric-magnetic exchange cannot have order two. Our proof is fully general and requires neither translation symmetry with restricted families of system sizes nor a fixed pairing between vertex and plaquette stabilizers. Geometrically, the proof locally emulates the unavoidable crossing between electric and magnetic strings, similar to a cross-cap in a non-orientable manifold. Extending the problem to the $\mathbb{Z}_N$ toric code, we find that an order-two Clifford realization exists for odd $N$, whereas it is impossible for even $N$. Our results show that the group law of an emergent electromagnetic duality need not lift faithfully to its microscopic Clifford realization.

</details>


### [127] [Unitary Schur Sampling of Qudits via Random SWAP Tests: Hunt for Antisymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02103)
*Shrigyan Brahmachari,David Jakab,Henry D. Pfister,Iman Marvian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种仅使用两两SWAP测试（即两量子比特投影测量到对称/反对称子空间）来实现酉Schur采样的协议，将Schur采样转化为对反对称性的搜索，并应用于量子纯度放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于电路的Schur采样依赖相干表示论操作（如Clebsch-Gordan变换、广义相位估计、对称群上的量子傅里叶变换），实现复杂且资源开销大。论文旨在寻找一种更简单、仅需两体测量的替代方案，以提取多体量子系统中的置换不变信息。

Method: 协议反复识别并提取最大反对称子系统，仅用两量子比特的SWAP测试（投影到对称与反对称子空间）实现任意置换不变混合态在n个d维qudit上的酉Schur采样。该过程被解释为对Young图的随机遍历，并保持SU(d)不可约表示状态，同时在重数子系统中制备规范纯态。

Result: 使用O(n·min{n,d}^3·log^2(n/ε))次随机SWAP测试，可达到金刚石距离误差ε。在量子纯度放大（QPA）应用中，最优纯化态可通过保留单个指定输出qudit并丢弃其余qudit获得。

Conclusion: 酉Schur采样可完全由两两SWAP测试实现，避免复杂的表示论相干操作；该算法具有Young图随机遍历的自然解释，并在量子纯度放大中给出简洁的最优输出方案。

Abstract: Schur sampling is a fundamental primitive for extracting permutation-invariant information from many-body quantum systems and has broad applications in quantum information science. Circuit-based implementations typically rely on coherent representation-theoretic operations such as Clebsch-Gordan transforms, generalized phase estimation, or quantum Fourier transforms over the symmetric group. We show that unitary Schur sampling of arbitrary permutation-invariant mixed states on $n$ $d$-dimensional qudits can instead be implemented using only pairwise SWAP tests, namely, two-qudit projective measurements onto the symmetric and antisymmetric subspaces. Our protocol achieves error $ε$ in diamond distance using $O(n\min\{n,d\}^{3}\log^2(n/ε))$ random SWAP tests. The central idea is to repeatedly identify and extract the largest antisymmetric subsystem. This turns unitary Schur sampling into a search for antisymmetry and gives the algorithm a natural interpretation as a stochastic traversal of a Young diagram. The protocol preserves the $\mathrm{SU}(d)$-irrep state while preparing a canonical pure state in the multiplicity subsystem. As an application, we consider quantum purity amplification (QPA), in which multiple noisy copies of a pure quantum state are combined to produce a state of higher purity. We show that the optimal purified state can be obtained by retaining a single designated output qudit and discarding the rest.

</details>


### [128] [On the Metastability of the Mean-Field Interchange Model for Local Dimension $\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01987)
*Sergio Escobar,Lin Lin,Michael Ragone,Kevin D. Stubbs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明平均场互换模型（局域维度 d≥3，单格点广义泡利耦合）的 Davies 动力学存在两个特征逆温度 β_min(d) 与 β=d：在区间外谱隙以 n 的逆多项式为下界，区间内谱隙随 n 指数消失，说明亚稳性导致的指数慢化完全来自 Young 图链。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多量子系统在低温下因亚稳相的存在而缓慢热化。但存在某些情形，进一步降温反而能恢复多项式时间热化。作者希望刻画这种"再降温恢复快速热化"现象的动力学相图并给出严格证明。

Method: 以平均场互换模型的 Davies 生成为对象，利用 Schur-Weyl 对偶将其分解为作用在 Young 图上的经典马尔可夫生成元，并分析其余对称性扇区（其谱隙为 Ω(n^{-1})），从而将谱隙问题归约为 Young 图链的行为。

Result: 动力学相图由两个特征逆温度 β_min(d) 与 β=d 界定，静态一阶相变位于二者之间。在区间 (β_min(d), d) 之外（高温与低温），Davies 生成元的谱隙以系统尺度 n 的逆多项式为下界；在区间内部，谱隙随 n 指数消失，表现为亚稳性。其余对称性扇区的谱隙为 Ω(n^{-1})。

Conclusion: Davies 动力学的指数慢化完全源自 Young 图链，验证了进一步降温可恢复多项式时间热化的机制，并给出了该模型动力学相变的定量刻画。

Abstract: Many quantum systems are believed to thermalize slowly at low temperatures due to the existence of metastable phases. However, there are cases where further cooling can restore polynomial-time thermalization: we demonstrate that the Davies dynamics of the mean-field interchange model with local dimension $d\geq 3$ and single-site generalized Pauli couplings exhibits this behavior. The dynamical phase diagram is beholden to two distinguished inverse temperatures, $β_{\mathrm{min}}(d)$ and $β=d$, with the static first-order phase transition lying between them. At low and high temperatures surrounding the interval $(β_{\mathrm{min}}(d),d)$, the spectral gap of the Davies generator is bounded below by an inverse polynomial in system size $n$. Meanwhile, in this interval, the gap vanishes exponentially in $n$, indicating metastability. The proof uses Schur-Weyl duality to decompose the Davies generator into a classical Markov generator on Young diagrams, and the restriction to the remaining symmetry sectors whose gap is $Ω(n^{-1})$. Thus, exponential slowdown arises entirely from the Young diagram chain.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum Algorithms for OPI Variants Beyond Locality and Classical Decodability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00502)
*Seyoon Ragavan,Noah Shutty*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Regev归约框架下突破两个限制：一是用非交换/多方量子解码器求解 $\mathbf{By}=0$ 的非零解，适用于具有"两重乘法性质"的码；二是在保留经典解码的前提下引入"直方图局部"全局频率约束，证明其重采样稳定性，从而给出最优多项式交集(OPI)变体的高效量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Regev归约是寻找满足非线性约束码字的量子算法框架，迄今未被去量子化的应用依赖两个限制：(1) 需要高效经典解码器；(2) 约束是逐坐标的（每坐标各指定允许值集合）。作者希望分别突破这两个限制，扩展框架的适用范围。

Method: 贡献一：采用量子解码器求解 $\mathbf{y}\in(\mathbb{F}_q\setminus\{0\})^m$ 满足 $\mathbf{By}=0$。对固定素数 $q>2$，Chen-Liu-Zhandry (Eurocrypt 2022) 针对随机矩阵、$m=\Omega(n^2)$ 求解，该体制已被经典算法覆盖；本文将其模板推广到有"两重乘法性质"的码（由 $\mathbf{B}$ 行张成）：任意两码字的逐坐标乘积所张成空间的维数远小于 $m$。贡献二：保留经典解码，允许符号频率的全局约束，研究"直方图局部"约束（规定每个符号允许出现次数）。对广泛约束族证明：均匀随机满足向量在均匀随机重采样一个坐标后仍满足的概率为常数；该稳定性在可解码 Hamming 重量处提供充分 Fourier 质量。

Result: 第一项贡献得到新的量子解码框架，覆盖具备两重乘法性质的码，超越原先依赖随机矩阵与经典解码器的体制。第二项贡献借助重采样稳定性在可解码 Hamming 重量处给出充分 Fourier 质量，从而对最优多项式交集(OPI)的若干变体给出高效量子算法。

Conclusion: 本文分别从解码器量子化与约束全局化两个方向扩展了Regev归约：量子解码器版适用于具有两重乘法性质的码，而直方图局部约束的稳定性分析为OPI变体提供高效量子算法，说明该框架可容纳更强的量子子程序与更一般的全局约束。

Abstract: Regev's reduction is a quantum algorithmic framework for finding codewords satisfying nonlinear constraints by decoding the dual code. To date, applications that have not been dequantized have relied on efficient classical decoders and coordinate-wise constraints specifying a set of allowed values for each coordinate. We push past these two restrictions separately via two separate contributions.
  Our first contribution uses a quantum decoder to find solutions $\mathbf{y}\in(\mathbb{F}_q\setminus\{0\})^m$ to $\mathbf{B}\mathbf{y}=0$, where $\mathbf{B}\in\mathbb{F}_q^{n\times m}$. For fixed prime $q>2$, Chen, Liu, and Zhandry (Eurocrypt 2022) solve this problem for random matrices with $m=Ω(n^2)$, a regime now covered by classical algorithms. We adapt their template to codes (spanned by the rows of $\mathbf{B}$) with a ``two-fold multiplication property'': the coordinate-wise products of pairs of codewords span a space of dimension much smaller than $m$.
  Our second contribution retains classical decoding but allows global constraints on symbol frequencies. We study ``histogram-local'' constraints, which specify the allowed numbers of occurrences of each symbol. For broad families of these constraints, we show that a uniformly random satisfying vector remains satisfying with constant probability after resampling a uniformly random coordinate. This stability provides sufficient Fourier mass at decodable Hamming weights, yielding efficient quantum algorithms for variants of optimal polynomial intersection (OPI).

</details>


### [130] [Local random quantum circuits converge to the Porter-Thomas distribution in polynomial depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02125)
*Aniruddha Sen,Nicholas Hunter-Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了多项式深度砖墙随机电路的输出分布以逆多项式速率在全变差距离下收敛到Porter-Thomas分布：对于由最近邻Haar随机门构成的局部随机量子电路，深度为\(O(n^{2m+1}\log n)\)时，其固定比特串输出分布与Porter-Thomas分布的全变差距离至多为\(O(1/n^m)\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Porter-Thomas统计是随机量子态及混沌量子多体系统输出分布的特征性质，其收敛性在随机电路采样和量子优势实验论证中居核心地位。尽管人们预期低深度随机量子电路的输出统计近似服从Porter-Thomas分布，但此前缺乏严格的收敛性证明，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 证明结合了三类工具：其一，利用近似设计（approximate designs）给出的矩界；其二，针对特征函数的解析估计；其三，用于逆矩的局域反集中（local anticoncentration）性质。研究对象为最近邻Haar随机门构成的砖墙式局部随机量子电路在固定比特串上的输出概率分布。

Result: 对任意\(m \geq 0\)，深度为\(O(n^{2m+1}\log(n))\)的电路的输出分布与Porter-Thomas分布之间的全变差距离至多为\(O(1/n^m)\)，即随电路深度多项式增长而以逆多项式速率收敛。

Conclusion: 本文为低深度随机量子电路输出统计近似服从Porter-Thomas分布这一被广泛使用的假设提供了首个严格的多项式深度收敛性证明，从而强化了随机电路采样与量子优势实验的理论基础。

Abstract: Porter-Thomas statistics are a characteristic feature of the output distribution of random quantum states and, more broadly, chaotic quantum many-body systems. Convergence to Porter-Thomas plays a central role in random circuit sampling and experimental demonstrations of quantum advantage, where the output statistics of low-depth random quantum circuits are expected to be approximately Porter-Thomas, despite the absence of a rigorous proof of convergence. We show that the output distribution of polynomial-depth brickwork random circuits converges inverse-polynomially in total variation distance to the Porter-Thomas distribution. Specifically, consider the output probability distribution over a fixed bitstring of a local random quantum circuit, constructed from nearest-neighbor Haar random gates. Then, for any $m \geq 0$, the distribution corresponding to circuits of depth $O(n^{2m+1}\log(n))$ is at most $O(1/n^m)$ far in total variation distance from the Porter-Thomas distribution. Our proof uses moment bounds from approximate designs, analytic estimates for characteristic functions, and a local anticoncentration property for inverse moments.

</details>


### [131] [Geometry of Knill-Laflamme Coefficients for Pauli Error Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01992)
*Baisong Sun,Ningping Cao,Yiu Tung Poon,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Knill-Laflamme系数向量的几何结构，将其刻画为联合高秩数值域，并给出表示重数充分性定理，揭示了系数范围凸性、连通性与签名谱区间的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Knill-Laflamme系数通过误差算子到码空间的标量压缩刻画精确量子纠错检测，但对固定码维数与给定Pauli观测下可达系数向量的几何结构（表示、码维数与检测约束如何影响该几何）缺乏系统理解。

Method: 将可达系数向量视为联合高秩数值域，其欧氏范数像为签名谱；通过算子表示与重数分析，证明重数充分性以将活跃期望数据提升为精确标量压缩，并结合交换Pauli族与子系统稳定子码框架进行分析。

Result: 主定理表明，当每个占据块的重数至少为码维数乘以对应活跃状态秩时，提升存在；此时系数范围为完整的活跃期望体，凸且连通，签名谱为闭区间。交换Pauli族中充分公共本征空间简并给出多胞形；子系统码中受保护逻辑子系统为非交换规范观测量提供重数空间。示例显示增大码维可使范围收缩、坍缩或变空，增大重数可将Bloch球变为实心球；三量子比特示例中额外非交换检测约束将四面体范围约化为重心与四顶点，产生不连通签名谱。

Conclusion: 该工作为Knill-Laflamme系数几何提供了结构性框架，统一了交换Pauli族与子系统稳定子码，并推动在结构化误差模型下寻找系数范围连通性与签名谱区间性质的更精细判据。

Abstract: Knill-Laflamme coefficients characterize exact quantum error detection through scalar compressions of error operators to the code space. For prescribed Pauli observables and a fixed code dimension, attainable coefficient vectors form a joint higher-rank numerical range, whose Euclidean norm image is the signature spectrum. We study how operator representation, code dimension, and detection constraints govern this geometry. Our main theorem identifies representation multiplicity as sufficient to lift active expectation data to exact scalar compressions: a lift exists whenever each occupied block has multiplicity at least the code dimension times the rank of the corresponding active state. When multiplicity suffices to realize all active states, the coefficient range is the full active expectation body and is therefore convex and connected, with a closed interval as its signature spectrum. This framework unifies commuting Pauli families, where sufficient common-eigenspace degeneracy yields polytopes, and subsystem stabilizer codes, where protected logical subsystems supply multiplicity spaces for noncommuting gauge observables. Explicit examples show how increasing the code dimension can make a range shrink, collapse, or become empty, while increasing multiplicity can turn a Bloch sphere into a filled ball. In the subsystem setting, parent-Hamiltonian constructions realize exact detecting codes as degenerate ground spaces, selecting distinguished vectors and continuous paths within these ranges. A final three-qubit example shows additional noncommuting detection constraints reduce a tetrahedral range to its barycenter and four vertices, producing a disconnected signature spectrum. The results provide a structural framework for Knill-Laflamme coefficient geometry and motivate sharper criteria for connected coefficient ranges and interval signature spectra under structured error models.

</details>


### [132] [Graph Neural Post-selection for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00504)
*Conor Carty,Tamas Noszko,Joschka Roffe,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用图神经网络（GNN）预测量子纠错解码失败，实现无需额外解码的后选择，在高码率qLDPC码上显著降低逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后选择可提升量子计算的逻辑可靠性，通过丢弃可能失败的采样（shots）来实现，但传统方法（如complementary gap）需要运行解码器，成本高。因此需要一种无需额外解码器执行即可预测解码失败的方法。

Method: 引入图神经网络（GNN），仅利用综合症状（syndromes）预测解码器失败；对于高码率量子低密度奇偶校验码（qLDPC），还利用已有的置信传播（belief-propagation）后验信息。在均匀去极化电路级噪声下，对旋转表面码和双变量自行车（BB）码存储进行评测。

Result: 保留90%采样时，[[72,12,6]]和[[144,12,12]] BB码在实际物理错误率下逻辑错误率分别约降低3700倍和740倍；对距离5表面码在物理噪声p=0.003下，神经后选择器在不运行解码器的情况下达到与领先complementary gap方法相当的逻辑错误抑制效果。

Conclusion: GNN后选择器无需额外解码即可有效预测解码失败，在高码率qLDPC码上实现显著逻辑错误率降低，并在表面码上与现有最优方法效果相当，展示了神经网络后选择在量子纠错中的潜力。

Abstract: Post-selection improves the logical reliability of quantum computation by rejecting shots which are likely to result in logical failure. We introduce graph neural networks that predict decoder failure without additional decoder executions, using only syndromes, and for high-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes, existing belief-propagation posteriors. We evaluate rotated surface and bivariate bicycle (BB) code memories under uniform depolarising circuit-level noise. Retaining 90% of shots gives approximately 3700x and 740x reductions in logical error rates for [[72,12,6]] and [[144,12,12]] BB codes at realistic physical error rates. For the distance 5 surface code at physical noise p=0.003, our neural post-selector achieves logical error suppression comparable to the leading complementary gap method without running the decoder.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Light Cones: State Preparation Complexity in Quantum Spin Glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02166)
*Omar Al-Ghattas,David Gamarnik,Bobak T Kiani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入基于泡利轮廓度量熵的有效轮廓复杂度，用于研究稠密量子 p-自旋哈密顿量的态制备复杂度，超越了单纯基于光锥的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电路光锥界无法处理浅层电路后接无限制 Clifford 电路等情形（后者可将局部可观测量扩散至全系统），因此需要新的复杂度度量来刻画稠密 p-自旋哈密顿量下的态制备复杂度。

Method: 以泡利轮廓的度量熵导出的有效轮廓复杂度为核心输入；证明结合了 Berta 等人的非对称量子 de Finetti 定理与高斯过程熵界；论证了需要 Ω(n^2/log n) 单/双量子比特门；获得了深度-宽度权衡、纠缠-深度与矩阵积态键维下界，以及 magic 层级各固定层级的障碍。

Result: 二次有效轮廓复杂度有界的类与基态能量保持至少 √n 的正间隔；亚二次复杂度下该类无法在主导阶上超越乘积态基准；达到近基态能量需 Ω(n^2/log n) 门；Clifford+T 电路在 o(n) 个 T 门时相对乘积稳定子态无主导阶能量优势。

Conclusion: 有效轮廓复杂度为稠密 p-自旋哈密顿量的态制备提供了超越光锥的新下界工具，统一导出电路宽度、深度、纠缠与 magic 层级等多类资源下界。

Abstract: We introduce a method for studying state preparation complexity in dense quantum $p$-spin Hamiltonians on $n$ qubits, going beyond bounds based only on circuit lightcones. The key input is the class's effective profile complexity, which is derived from the metric entropy of its Pauli profiles. These profiles record expectations of all Pauli operators supported on exactly $p$ qubits. Classes with uniformly bounded quadratic effective profile complexity remain separated from the ground-state energy by a positive multiple of $\sqrt n$ for sufficiently large fixed $p$. At subquadratic effective profile complexity, the class cannot outperform a suitable benchmark class at leading order, with product states providing a universal benchmark. The proof combines an adaptation of a nonsymmetric quantum de Finetti theorem of Berta et al. (arXiv:1810.12197) with Gaussian process entropy bounds.
  Applying this framework, we show that attaining near-ground-state energy requires $Ω(n^2/\log n)$ one- and two-qubit gates, even with arbitrary discardable ancillas. We also obtain depth-width tradeoffs, entanglement-depth and matrix product state bond-dimension lower bounds, and obstructions for both orientations at every fixed level of Parham's magic hierarchy (arXiv:2504.19966), with total circuit width $O(n)$. In first-level reverse magic, a shallow circuit is followed by an unrestricted Clifford circuit. The latter can spread local observables across the system, preventing a direct application of small-lightcone bounds. For this first-level class, our bounds also allow arbitrarily many clean ancillas at fixed shallow-circuit depth. A sharper benchmark shows that Clifford+$T$ circuits with $o(n)$ $T$-gates have no leading-order energy advantage over product stabilizer states, even with unrestricted Clifford operations and arbitrary discardable ancillas.

</details>


### [134] [Polynomial-time classical and quantum simulation of quantum impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02167)
*Jiaqing Jiang,Nathan Ju,Ojas Parekh,Chaithanya Rayudu,Andrew Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了量子杂质模型模拟的计算复杂性，证明其静态性质（基态能量、配分函数）可在经典计算机上多项式时间高效计算，而其动力学性质（如非平衡格林函数）即使有限温度下也能捕获量子计算的完整能力，从而界定了该问题的经典与量子可计算性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子杂质模型既是相互作用量子物质的范例模型，也是现代电子结构方法的关键计算原语，但其模拟的经典与量子可计算性边界此前尚不清晰，例如基态能量估计的最优经典复杂度仍为拟多项式时间，热平衡模拟缺乏严格的多项式时间保证。本文旨在系统地刻画这一复杂性边界。

Method: 采用计算复杂性理论分析手段，对耦合到非相互作用大浴的相互作用费米子子系统（杂质模型）进行静态与动力学性质的可计算性研究：为静态性质构造经典算法，并为动力学性质建立量子计算复杂性的等价性归约。

Result: 静态性质方面：(1) 以加法精度 δ 估计基态能量，时间 $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$；(2) 在逆温度 β 下以相对精度 δ 估计配分函数，时间 $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$，其中 n 为系统规模，将基态能量估计的最优已知复杂度从拟多项式改进为多项式，并首次给出热平衡模拟的多项式时间保证。动力学方面：计算非平衡格林函数即使在有限温度下也能捕获量子计算的完整能力，即经典困难而量子容易。

Conclusion: 研究结果排除了静态性质存在超多项式量子加速的可能，同时表明在模拟非平衡杂质物理方面存在实现量子优势的途径，从而清晰划定了量子杂质模型模拟中经典与量子可处理性的分界。

Abstract: Quantum impurity models are paradigmatic models of interacting quantum matter, as well as key computational primitives for modern electronic-structure methods. They describe a small subsystem of interacting fermions coupled to a large, noninteracting bath. We perform a comprehensive study of the computational complexity of simulating impurity models, delineating the boundary between classical and quantum tractability for this class of problems. Our main finding is that static properties of quantum impurity models can be calculated efficiently on a classical computer. Specifically, we give classical algorithms that (1) estimate the ground-state energy to additive precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,δ^{-1})$, and (2) estimate the partition function at inverse temperature $β$ to relative precision $δ$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,β,δ^{-1})$, where $n$ is the system size. These results improve the previous best-known complexity for ground-state energy estimation from quasipolynomial to polynomial time, while establishing for the first time rigorous polynomial-time guarantees for simulating impurity models in thermal equilibrium. On the other hand, we find that simulating dynamical properties of impurity models is hard for classical computers but easy on a quantum computer. As a canonical example, we show that computing their nonequilibrium Green's functions captures the full power of quantum computation, even at finite temperature. Taken together, our results rule out superpolynomial quantum speedups for computing static properties, but provide an avenue for quantum advantage in simulating impurity physics out of equilibrium.

</details>


### [135] [A computational phase diagram for the transverse field Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02079)
*Thuy-Duong Vuong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究横场Ising模型的配分函数与Gibbs态观测量的经典可计算性，用谱宽度与横场强度之比为阈值刻画可近似与NP难区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定横场Ising模型配分函数在何种参数区域可被高效经典近似、何种区域在复杂度上不可行，并扩展到Gibbs态Pauli串观测量的近似。

Method: 定义阈值量 Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η（Δ(J)为耦合矩阵J的谱宽）。在阈值≤1时构造随机化经典算法近似配分函数Z(β)；在阈值>1时给出NP困难性归约；阈值≤1时进一步给出近似Gibbs态Pauli串观测量的随机化经典算法。

Result: 阈值≤1时，存在多项式时间（n、β、模型参数、ε⁻¹）随机化经典算法将Z(β)近似到相对误差ε。阈值>1时，将Z(β)近似到exp(o(n))乘性因子是NP难的。阈值≤1时可高效近似Gibbs态Pauli串观测量到任意小加性误差；当观测量在X基下对角（{I,X}^⊗n）时可达任意小相对误差。

Conclusion: 阈值量Δ(J)·tanh(βη)/η精确区分了横场Ising模型配分函数高效经典可近似区域与不可行区域，并刻画了Gibbs态观测量的可近似性。

Abstract: We study the transverse field Ising model, defined by the Hamiltonian $H =\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i, j\in [n]} J_{ij} Z_i Z_j +\sum_{i=1}^n h_i^z Z_i + η\sum_{i} X_i$ where $J $ is the symmetric interaction matrix, and $η$ is the transverse field strength. Let $Δ(J)=λ_{\max}(J)-λ_{\min}(J)$ be the spectral width of $J.$ When the inverse temperature $β\geq0$ satisfies $Δ(J)\cdot\frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq1$, we give a randomized classical algorithm that approximates the partition function $Z(β)=\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})$ to a given relative error $ε\in(0,1)$ in time polynomial in $n$, $β$, the model parameters, and $ε^{-1}$. When $ Δ(J) \cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η > 1 ,$ we show that approximating $ Z(β)$ within an $\exp(o(n))$-multiplicative factor is $\textbf{NP}$-hard, and thus unlikely to admit an efficient classical or quantum algorithms under standard complexity theoretic assumptions. Furthermore, in the regime $Δ(J)\cdot \frac{\tanh(βη)}η\leq 1,$ we provide an efficient randomized classical algorithm that approximates Pauli string observables of the Gibbs state $ ρ_β= \frac{e^{-βH}}{\operatorname{Tr}(e^{-βH})}$ within an arbitrarily small additive error. In the special case when the observable is also diagonal in the $X$-basis, i.e. $P \in \{I, X\}^{\otimes n}$, the algorithm further achieves arbitrarily small relative error.

</details>


### [136] [Noisy Quantum Query Complexity via Fractional Block Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00506)
*Mehil Agarwal,Shravas Rao,Fang Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在不完美查询（oracle）模型下研究量子查询复杂度，提出基于分数块敏感度（fbs）的统一下界框架，覆盖疏忽型oracle、混合算法与IID退相位噪声，并进一步给出时变退相位模型的变分资源刻画，恢复已知界并得到新的查询复杂度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子查询复杂度的经典下界工具（如块敏感度、分数块敏感度）通常在理想oracle假设下成立。实际量子算法中oracle可能出错、退相位或混合经典查询，这些不完美访问如何影响量子加速，以及能否建立普适下界，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 以分数块敏感度fbs为共同框架推导下界：对以概率1-p返回正确查询的疏忽oracle，给出基于fbs(f,0^n)的下界，并构造保持量子查询复杂度的部分函数f'以说明疏忽不破坏指数加速；对Q次相干查询加C次经典查询的混合算法证明C+Q^2=Ω(fbs(f))；对每次查询以速率p独立退相位查询索引寄存器的IID退相位噪声证明Ω(p·fbs(f))；进一步引入时变退相位模型，构造总被fbs(f)下界的变分资源，将其计算归约为凸优化问题。

Result: 在疏忽oracle下得到基于fbs(f,0^n)的下界，并构造出在疏忽查询下仍具指数量子加速的部分函数，从而排除了该模型下基于fbs(f)的一般下界；对混合算法证明C+Q^2=Ω(fbs(f))；对IID退相位证明Ω(p·fbs(f))；时变退相位模型的变分资源可由凸优化求解，作为应用恢复了混合与IID退相位下界，并确定了退相位速率随时间增长时非结构化搜索的查询复杂度。

Conclusion: 论文建立了统一的不完美oracle量子查询复杂度下界框架，说明疏忽噪声不必然消除量子加速，而退相位类噪声则受fbs主导；时变退相位下的变分资源方法为新的噪声调度提供了通用且可计算的复杂度下界工具。

Abstract: We study quantum query complexity under several models of imperfect oracle access, and develop lower bounds through a common framework based on fractional block sensitivity (\(\fbs\)).
  For a negligent oracle that applies the correct query with probability \(1-p\), we prove a lower bound in terms of \(\fbs(f,0^n)\). We also show, perhaps surprisingly, that negligence need not destroy quantum speedups: any function \(f\) can be transformed into a partial function \(f'\) whose negligent query complexity essentially preserves the quantum query complexity of \(f\). This gives partial functions with exponential quantum speedups even under negligent queries, and in particular rules out a general lower bound in terms of \(\fbs(f)\) for partial functions in this model.
  For two other models, we obtain general lower bounds in terms of \(\fbs(f)\) for all Boolean functions. For hybrid algorithms using \(Q\) coherent and \(C\) classical queries, we prove the tradeoff \(C+Q^2=Ω(\fbs(f))\). For an IID dephasing noisy model where each query dephases the query-index register at rate \(p\) independently, we prove \(Ω\!\left(p\,\fbs(f)\right)\) queries are necessary.
  Finally, we introduce a broader family of time-varying dephasing models and identify a variational resource that is always lower bounded by \(\fbs(f)\). Computing this resource reduces to a convex optimization problem, providing a simple way to derive lower bounds for new noise schedules. As applications, we recover the hybrid and IID dephasing bounds and determine the query complexity of unstructured search when the dephasing rate grows over time.

</details>


### [137] [Blind Unforgeability implies Plus-One Unforgeability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00517)
*Mehil Agarwal,Calliope S. Reimann,Fang Song*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明量子盲不可伪造性蕴含量子加一不可伪造性，从而确立前者严格强于后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文献中关于量子盲不可伪造性与量子加一不可伪造性之间关系的公开问题。由于已知加一不可伪造性不蕴含盲不可伪造性，故需证明反向蕴含以确立二者的严格强弱关系。

Method: 提出一种新的"平滑"技术，以黑盒方式相干地重新缩放量子查询算法的查询历史，将任意成功的加一攻击者转化为盲不可伪造性攻击者，仅损失逆多项式因子。相较此前仅提取有用查询历史的思路，本文转向先相干地重塑查询历史之间的干涉方式。

Result: 证明了量子盲不可伪造性蕴含量子加一不可伪造性，结合已知的反向不蕴含，确立了盲不可伪造性在量子情形下严格强于加一不可伪造性；同时表明此前"仅提取查询历史"的方法在一般情况下必然失败。

Conclusion: 量子盲不可伪造性严格强于量子加一不可伪造性，且所提平滑技术为处理量子查询历史相干干涉提供了新范式。

Abstract: We prove that quantum blind-unforgeability implies quantum plus-one unforgeability, settling an open question in the literature. Since plus-one unforgeability is known not to imply blind-unforgeability, our result establishes that blind-unforgeability is \emph{strictly stronger} than plus-one unforgeability in the quantum setting.
  The implication is proven using a new \emph{smoothing} technique that coherently rescales the query histories of a quantum-query algorithm in a black-box manner. This enables us to transform any successful plus-one attacker into a blind-unforgeability attacker with only inverse-polynomial loss. Our proof represents a conceptual shift from merely extracting a useful query history as in earlier attempts, which we show inevitably fails in general, to first coherently reshaping how query histories interfere.

</details>


### [138] [Near-Optimal Ground-State Preparation without Controlled Hamiltonian Evolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00520)
*Dhrumil Patel,Steven T. Flammia,Raul Garcia-Patron*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究在仅允许查询非受控哈密顿量演化（$U_H$ 及其逆）的弱访问模型下，能否达到与使用受控哈密顿量演化或块编码算法相同的近最优复杂度，并给出了肯定回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态制备、能量估计和性质估计是量子模拟的核心任务。已有算法利用块编码或受控哈密顿量演化达到近最优复杂度，但一个自然的问题是：受控哈密顿量演化是否必要？能否在仅提供非受控哈密顿量演化访问的更弱模型中达到相同的复杂度标度？

Method: 作者提出两种仅通过 $U_H$ 及其逆进行查询的算法。第一种使用输入态的新鲜副本，查询复杂度为 $\widetilde O(\alpha_H/(\gamma\eta))$；第二种利用对输入态制备酉及其逆的相干访问，将复杂度改进为 $\widetilde O(\alpha_H/(\gamma\sqrt\eta))$。两者的核心机制是"谱下降原理"：从输入态 $|\psi\rangle$ 出发，沿能谱逐步下降，逐步靠近基态直到达到目标保真度。该方法还被扩展至基态性质估计和能量估计。

Result: 第二种算法的复杂度 $\widetilde O(\alpha_H/(\gamma\sqrt\eta))$ 在忽略对数因子的情况下与已知下界匹配，表明额外具有受控哈密顿量演化访问权限的算法无法将基态制备的查询复杂度改进超过对数因子。作者还给出了基态性质估计和能量估计的显式复杂度保证。

Conclusion: 受控哈密顿量演化对基态制备、性质估计和能量估计并非必要，仅在非受控演化访问下即可达到近最优复杂度；所提出的谱下降原理为这些任务提供了统一的算法框架。

Abstract: Ground-state preparation, energy estimation, and property estimation are fundamental tasks in quantum simulation. Existing ground-state preparation algorithms achieve near-optimal complexity using block encodings or controlled Hamiltonian evolution, raising a natural question: is controlled Hamiltonian evolution necessary, or can the same scaling be attained in the weaker access model that provides access to only uncontrolled Hamiltonian evolution? We resolve this question by giving two algorithms that access the input Hamiltonian $H$ only through queries to $U_H$ and its inverse $U_H^\dagger$, where $\|H\|\leα_H$ and $τ=Θ(1/α_H)$. Given an input state with ground-state weight at least $η$ and a spectral gap of $H$ of at least $γ$, our first algorithm uses fresh copies of the input state and requires $\widetilde O(α_H/(γη))$ queries to $U_H$ and $U_H^\dagger$, while our second algorithm uses coherent access to the input-state preparation unitary and its inverse to improve this complexity to $\widetilde O(α_H/(γ\sqrtη))$. The latter bound matches the known lower bound up to logarithmic factors, showing that algorithms with additional access to controlled Hamiltonian evolution cannot improve the query complexity for ground-state preparation beyond logarithmic factors. Both algorithms are based on a simple mechanism that we call the spectral descent principle, in which the algorithm starts from the input state $|ψ\rangle$ and progressively descends through the energy spectrum, moving closer to the ground state until the desired fidelity is reached. We also extend this framework to ground-state property and energy estimation and give explicit complexity guarantees for both tasks.

</details>


### [139] [One-Shot any Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02137)
*Andrew C. Yuan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 任何单次测量码都是自纠错的；任意[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码可被变换为[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码，具备单次量子纠错与O(log m)并行时间的局部解码器，阈值p_RG恒定，误差抑制为Ω(m^α)，并可增强输入码的误差抑制至Ω(m^α n^β)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何使量子LDPC码具备单次纠错能力，并在保持恒定阈值的同时提升误差抑制能力，以获得高效局部解码的纠错方案。

Method: 先证明任意单次测量码是自纠错的；再构造性变换：将任意[[n,k]] CSS QLDPC码转换为[[nm,k]] CSS QLDPC码，配备单次量子纠错和O(log m)并行时间的局部解码器，并分析联合局部随机物理与测量噪声下的性能。

Result: 在恒定阈值p_RG>0以下，T轮纠错后的逻辑失败概率为O(Tn)exp[-Ω(m^α)]（α>0常数），即阈值为p_RG、误差抑制为Ω(m^α)；若输入码带阈值p_c>0且误差抑制Ω(n^β)的单次解码器，则输出码将误差抑制增强至Ω(m^α n^β)，并保持解码器无关阈值p_RG，当p_c<p_RG时称为增强。

Conclusion: 该工作表明单次纠错码具有自纠错性，并提供将一般CSS QLDPC码升级为具备高效局部解码、恒定阈值与更强误差抑制的单次纠错码的通用方法。

Abstract: We first provide a simple proof showing that any single-shot code is self-correcting. We then show that any $[[n,k]]$ CSS quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) code can be transformed into an $[[nm,k]]$ CSS QLDPC code with single-shot (SS) quantum error correction and an efficient local decoder which runs in $O(\log m)$ parallel time. Below a constant threshold $p_{\rm RG}>0$ for joint local stochastic physical and measurement noise, the logical failure probability over $T$ correction rounds is bounded by \begin{equation*}
  O(T n)\exp[-Ω(m^α)] \end{equation*} for a constant $α>0$; we say that the \textit{threshold} is $p_{\rm RG}$ and \textit{error suppression} is $Ω(m^α)$. Moreover, if the input code is equipped with a single-shot decoder with threshold $p_c>0$ and error suppression $Ω(n^β)$, then the output code \textit{enhances} the error suppression to $Ω(m^α n^β)$, while maintaining the decoder-independent threshold $p_{\rm RG}$, and thus \textit{enhanced} if $p_c < p_{\rm RG}$.

</details>


### [140] [First-quantized quantum simulation with non-local potentials by matrix-product-state encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00521)
*Kazuki Tsuoka,Taichi Kosugi,Masari Watanabe,Hirofumi Nishi,Yu-ichiro Matsushita*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于矩阵乘积态(MPS)编码高效实现非局域赝势的方法，显著降低第一量子化量子模拟的电路深度，并在一维氢原子超短激光脉冲电离模型中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一量子化量子模拟相比第二量子化具有更低的空间与门复杂度；引入赝势可进一步减少模拟电子数，但赝势的非局域性质使其在量子电路中的实现颇具挑战。因此需要一种能高效实现非局域势的量子线路方案。

Method: 利用矩阵乘积态(MPS)编码实现非局域势，从而高效构造Trotter步算子，其电路深度为 O(deg(V_loc)·n^deg(V_loc) + 8L·n_atom·χ² + 2n²)，其中deg(V_loc)为多项式局域势的阶数，n_atom为原子数，L为所有原子中投影函数的最大数目，χ为MPS键维数，n为一维空间二进制编码波函数的量子比特数。

Result: 将该方法应用于一维氢原子在超短激光脉冲驱动下的电离模型。结果表明，与直接酉分解相比，MPS编码显著降低了所需电路深度。

Conclusion: MPS编码为非局域赝势的量子电路实现提供了高效途径，为实用的第一量子化量子模拟指明了可行方向。

Abstract: Quantum simulations based on first-quantized approaches can offer lower space and gate complexities than second-quantized approaches. The gate requirements for these simulations can be further reduced by employing pseudopotentials, a technique widely used in classical quantum chemistry calculations to decrease the number of simulated electrons. However, implementing pseudopotentials in quantum circuits remains challenging due to their non-local nature. In this study, we proposed a method to efficiently implement non-local potentials using matrix product state (MPS) encoding. MPS encoding allows us to efficiently implement a Trotter step operator with a circuit depth of $O(\mathrm{deg}(V_{\mathrm{loc}}) n^{\mathrm{deg}(V_{\mathrm{loc}})} + 8Lnn_{\mathrm{atom}}χ^2 + 2n^2)$, where $\mathrm{deg}(V_{\mathrm{loc}})$ is the degree of the polynomial local potential, $n_{\mathrm{atom}}$ is the number of atoms in the system, $L$ is the maximum number of projector functions across all atoms, and $n$ is the number of qubits used to represent the binary-encoded wave function in one-dimensional space. We demonstrated the effectiveness of this approach by applying it to a model of the ionization of a one-dimensional hydrogen atom driven by an ultrashort laser pulse.
