### [212] [Nonlocality without entanglement for multipartite quantum measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02032)
*Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明存在成对的两量子比特乘积测量，整体联合访问可完美区分，但在LOCC下不可完美区分，从而在测量层面实现了"无纠缠非局域性"的类比，并揭示其层次结构、方向性、多体扩展及光学实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典"无纠缠非局域性"（nonlocality without entanglement）多基于复杂纠缠态，本文希望在更简单的测量（product measurement）框架下建立对应概念，探索测量层面非局域性的最小实例与结构性质。

Method: 构造两量子比特乘积测量对；分析其在联合访问与分布式LOCC下可区分性差异；对比自适应与非自适应LOCC协议（六组分双体qutrit乘积测量）；研究LOCC可区分性的方向性；构造n量子比特乘积测量对，使其全局可完美区分但任意二分下即使允许任意联合量子操作也不可区分；设计基于量子过程非局域性的数据隐藏光学方案。

Result: 发现两量子比特乘积测量对在联合访问下可完美区分、LOCC下不可；自适应LOCC严格优于受限非自适应协议（六组分双体qutrit实例）；LOCC可区分性本质具有方向性（依赖哪一方发起）；构造n量子比特测量对全局可区分但任意二分下（含任意联合操作）不可区分；给出基于量子过程非局域性的数据隐藏的光学实现。

Conclusion: 测量层面的"无纠缠非局域性"可在极简设置中出现，并具有层次性、方向性与多体鲁棒性，可用于量子数据隐藏，为量子过程非局域性研究提供基本框架与可行实验路径。

Abstract: We show that a pair of two-qubit product measurements can be perfectly distinguished when accessed jointly, yet cannot be perfectly distinguished when their local components are distributed between distant parties restricted to local quantum operations and classical communication (LOCC). This establishes a measurement analogue of nonlocality without entanglement that, unlike its state-based counterpart, emerges in a remarkably simple setting. We further uncover a hierarchy of LOCC discrimination strategies: adaptive protocols, in which the probe used at one site may depend on classical information obtained from another, can strictly outperform restricted non-adaptive protocols, as demonstrated by a set of six bipartite qutrit product measurements. We also show that LOCC distinguishability of bipartite measurements can be intrinsically directional, depending on which party initiates the protocol. More generally, we construct pairs of $n$-qubit product measurements that are globally perfectly distinguishable but cannot be perfectly distinguished across any bipartition, even when all parties on either side of the bipartition are allowed arbitrary joint quantum operations. Finally, we show how quantum data hiding based on quantum-process nonlocality without entanglement can be realized using elementary optical components.

</details>


### [213] [Complexity and Applications of Nearest Stabilizer Product State Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02037)
*Daniel Grier,Hakop Pashayan,Luke Schaeffer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定子乘积态上的最近邻优化问题，通过改变单量子比特稳定子态集合S，给出其完整复杂度分类：在Clifford群对称性下共有9种不同的S，除最简单的两种外均为NP完全；同时该问题可用于经典模拟算法运行时间界、纠缠度量及低秩矩阵完成复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态之间的最近邻问题在量子信息与计算中有广泛意义，但针对稳定子乘积态这一受限集合的优化问题缺乏系统性复杂度刻画。作者希望统一理解该问题在不同集合S下的计算难度，并揭示其与多个领域的联系。

Method: 在n量子比特稳定子态|ψ⟩与单量子比特稳定子态集合S上，最大化|⟨ψ|φ_1,...,φ_n⟩|²。通过变化集合S，并利用Clifford群对称性对S进行分类，将可能的S归约为9种本质不同情形，再逐一分析其计算复杂度。

Result: 在Clifford群对称性下共有9种不同的可能集合S；除其中最简单的两种外，其余均为NP完全。该问题的解还可用于更紧的经典模拟算法运行时间界、纠缠度量以及低秩矩阵完成复杂度研究。

Conclusion: 最近稳定子乘积态问题具有完整的复杂度分类，并作为统一框架连接经典模拟、纠缠度量与低秩矩阵完成等多个问题；大多数自然选择的S导致NP完全性。

Abstract: Consider the following optimization problem over stabilizer product states: given an $n$-qubit stabilizer state $\left|ψ\right\rangle$ and a set of single-qubit stabilizer states $S$, maximize $\left|\langle ψ| φ_1, \ldots, φ_n \rangle\right|^2$ over single-qubit stabilizer states $\left|φ_i\right\rangle \in S$. By varying the set $S$, we show that solutions to this problem can be useful in a variety of settings: tighter runtime bounds for certain classical simulation algorithms; measures of entanglement; and the complexity of low-rank matrix completion. Moreover, we give a complete complexity classification of this nearest stabilizer product state problem. After accounting for the symmetries in the Clifford group, there are $9$ distinct possible sets $S$, and we show that all but the two simplest of these are $\NP$-complete.

</details>


### [214] [Fermionic Gaussianity can be tested with mode-independent sample-complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02050)
*Maxwell West,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的上界技术，用于界定"未知量子态属于或远离某族态"这一类判定问题的双拷贝测试接受概率，进而给出样本复杂度上界；作为应用，证明检测费米高斯性和Slater行列式态均可用最优的Θ(ε⁻²)拷贝数完成，并证明Bell采样对费米高斯性检验是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判定未知量子态是否属于、或远离给定态族是量子信息论的自然问题。通常存在自然的双拷贝测试，对属于目标族的态总是接受，但对远离族的态，其上界接受概率很难界定，从而导致判定问题的样本复杂度难以刻画。

Method: 作者发展了一种通用技术，用于对任意态族的双拷贝测试的接受概率给出上界，从而上界判定问题本身的样本复杂度。该框架被应用于著名的Bell采样程序以检验费米高斯性，并与允许任意集体测量和自适应测量的协议进行比较。

Result: 证明判定未知纯n模态是否为费米高斯态、或与所有高斯态的迹距离至少为ε，可用最优拷贝数Θ(ε⁻²)完成；并且证明Bell采样对检验费米高斯性是最优的，即使与任意集体和自适应测量协议相比也是如此。此外，Slater行列式态族的类似判定问题也能以与模数无关的样本复杂度求解。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用上界技术为量子态族判定问题提供了强有力的样本复杂度分析工具，并具体确立了Bell采样在费米高斯性检验中的最优性，同时推广至Slater行列式态族。

Abstract: Deciding whether an unknown quantum state either belongs to, or is far from, a given family of states is a natural question of quantum information theory. In many such cases, there is a natural 2-copy test which always accepts whenever the state indeed belongs to the target family; it is often much more difficult, however, to bound the probability of acceptance for states that are far from the family. Here we develop a simple new technique for upper bounding this probability for general families, and therefore upper bounding the sample-complexity of the decision problem itself. As a particularly striking example of this framework, we show that deciding whether an unknown pure $n$-mode state is fermionic Gaussian, or at least $\varepsilon$-far in trace distance from all Gaussian states, can be accomplished using the optimal number $Θ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ of copies. Remarkably, our analysis applies to the well-known Bell sampling procedure for testing fermionic Gaussianity, which we therefore show to be optimal, even compared to protocols making arbitrary collective and adaptive measurements. As a second example, we show that the analogous decision problem for the family of Slater determinant states can also be solved with mode-independent sample-complexity.

</details>


### [215] [Sequential Capacity of Quantum Processes with Finite Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02068)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"序贯响应容量"指标，量化固定内存量子设备在长运行下的响应复杂度，证明该容量随运行步数K以K log K增长，并给出噪声下对数增益的匹配上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定系统与内存尺寸下，量子设备运行更久时其响应能变得多复杂，缺乏定量刻画；同时需明确经典过程在相同测试下的容量对比，以及在噪声存在时量子对数增益的存续条件。

Method: 定义序贯响应容量：以多阶段自适应测试（每阶段一次新运行）在响应概率上以给定间隔区分不同过程的最大阶段数。对固定系统与内存尺寸，建立容量与运行长度及概率分辨率的紧致定律；构造基于单可见量子比特的时间依赖相位旋转方案；并针对存储经典标签选择的相位序列，分析已知独立Pauli噪声的影响。

Result: 固定分辨率下容量按K log K量级增长（K为每次运行的时间步数）；该构造仅用单可见量子比特的时间依赖相位旋转即可达到此增长，无需额外内部内存，且测试响应概率恰为0或1。相同测试下，每步固定基测量的经典随机过程在固定尺寸与分辨率下仅有线性容量。对存储经典标签的相位序列，理想控制且纠错后残余相位噪声较弱时，在固定小概率间隔下证明匹配的容量上下界；界表明逆残余相位翻转概率是限制额外对数增长的相干时间尺度。

Conclusion: 序贯响应容量刻画了固定内存量子设备长运行响应的复杂度上限，揭示了量子相对经典的对数级优势：该优势由残余相位翻转概率（相干时间尺度）限制，弱噪声下可严格保持。

Abstract: How complex can the responses of a quantum device become as it runs longer with a fixed internal memory? We quantify this complexity through sequential response capacity: how many adaptive testing stages, each using a fresh run, can continue to separate possible processes by a prescribed gap in response probabilities. For fixed system and memory sizes, we establish a tight law relating this capacity to run length and probability resolution. At fixed resolution, the capacity grows on the order of $K\log K$, where $K$ is the number of time steps in each run. Our construction attains this growth using time-dependent phase rotations on a single visible qubit with no additional internal memory; its tests give response probabilities exactly zero or one. Under the same tests, classical stochastic processes that measure in a fixed basis at every step have only linear capacity at fixed sizes and resolution. For phase sequences selected by a stored classical label, we then quantify how known independent Pauli noise changes this logarithmic enhancement. With ideal controls and weak residual phase noise after correction, we prove matching capacity bounds at a fixed small probability gap. These bounds identify the inverse residual phase-flip probability as the coherence timescale that limits the extra logarithmic growth.

</details>


### [216] [One-Way Quantum Symmetric Private Information Retrieval Protocol From A Single Database Server Using NISQ Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02093)
*Xiang Zou,H. F. Chau*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种在NISQ设备（无长期量子存储、到达即测量）下信息论安全的对称私有信息检索（SPIR）方案，利用单向BB84态、无歧义区分与联合随机置换分块实现用户与数据库隐私，并给出有限密钥分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单服务器SPIR对无限制量子能力的参与方不可能实现；已有bounded/noisy-quantum-storage模型依赖存储限制，本文希望在更严格的存储限制（仅NISQ设备、无长期量子存储、到达即测量，而窃听者全能力）下实现SPIR。

Method: 服务器单向发送BB84态；用户无歧义区分，获知随机比特子集；经联合随机置换分块后，用户仅公布其私下选定块的明确位置，服务器对所有块中这些位置哈希并取随机奇偶校验；置换使同一位置集匹配两个块的概率指数小。仅需在BB84设备上增加一个被动线性光学测量和一个辅助模式。用有限密钥量化认证任意无记忆用户与承诺置换所需的辅助传输，并将数据库隐私扩展至诱骗态弱相干脉冲。

Result: 对10^4比特数据库、数据库隐私10^-6，在可容忍噪声信道上约千量子比特的块即可满足使用规定测量的用户；证明对窃听者的保密性、通过公布集合的显式大小规则实现数据库隐私、以及对诚实准备并如实宣布的服务器的用户隐私；单光子情形精确成立，含小多光子项；仿真显示该界近乎紧致。

Conclusion: 在仅具NISQ设备、无长期量子存储且到达即测量的更严格存储限制下，SPIR可达到信息论安全；方案仅需BB84设备加一个被动线性光学测量与辅助模式，支持诱骗态弱相干脉冲，且界近乎紧致。

Abstract: Symmetric private information retrieval (SPIR) lets an inquirer obtain one specific bit only, without leaking any information about its index to the database owner. With a single server, this is impossible for parties with unrestricted quantum power. We show that it becomes possible, with information-theoretic security, when the server and the user hold only noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices without long-term quantum memory and measure each qubit on arrival, while the eavesdropper remains all-powerful; this is a stricter form of the memory restriction of the bounded- and noisy-quantum-storage models. The server sends BB84 states one way; the user discriminates them unambiguously and so knows a random subset of the bits. After a joint random permutation into blocks, the user announces the conclusive locations of one privately chosen block, and the server hashes and takes a random parity of these locations in every block; the permutation makes it exponentially unlikely that one set of locations fits two blocks. We prove secrecy against the eavesdropper, database privacy through an explicit size rule for the announced set, and user privacy against a server who prepares the prescribed BB84 states and announces truthfully, exactly for single photons and up to a small multi-photon term over a tolerated noise channel. The only addition to BB84 equipment is a passive linear-optical measurement with one ancillary mode. For a $10^{4}$-bit database and database privacy $10^{-6}$, blocks of about a thousand qubits suffice over a tolerated noise channel for a user who applies the prescribed measurement; certifying an arbitrary memoryless user and committing to the permutation need larger auxiliary transmissions, which we quantify with finite keys. Database privacy extends to decoy-state weak coherent pulses, and simulations show that the bound is nearly tight.

</details>


### [217] [Quantum state preparation for weighted d-DNNF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02094)
*Steef Hegeman,Joon Hyung Lee,Alfons Laarman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于由加权 d-DNNF（确定性、可分解的伪布尔电路）描述的量子态，可在仅计及复数运算的线性时间内构造出制备该量子态的量子电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态制备问题要求：给定量子态的描述，高效生成计算该量子态的量子电路。已有方法多针对特定表示形式，缺乏对加权 d-DNNF 这类通用结构化表示的高效构造结果。

Method: 利用加权 d-DNNF 的确定性与可分解性结构，设计算法在其上递归/自底向上地构造对应的量子电路，将时间复杂度归结为线性（不计复数算术开销）。

Result: 对任意由加权 d-DNNF 描述的量子态，可在关于描述规模的线性时间内（up to complex arithmetic）获得制备该态的量子电路。

Conclusion: 加权 d-DNNF 是一类支持量子态制备高效编译的表示，将态制备的电路构造复杂度降至线性，扩展了可高效制备的量子态类别。

Abstract: The quantum state preparation problem is to, given a description of a quantum state, efficiently generate a quantum circuit computing the state. We show that for quantum states described by weighted d-DNNF (deterministic, decomposable pseudo-Boolean circuits) a quantum circuit computing the state can be obtained in linear time up to complex arithmetic.

</details>


### [218] [An exponential separation between entanglement-assisted and unassisted one-way quantum communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02099)
*Ryan Anselm,Srijita Kundu,Olivier Lalonde,Ashwin Nayak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在单向通信模型中，存在全布尔函数的指数分离：带预共享纠缠时可用O(log n)比特经典单向通信计算，而无纠缠时需要Ω(n^{1/3})量子比特单向通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信复杂性的一个长期未解问题是：是否存在某类任务，在具有纠缠时只需很少通信量即可完成，但在无纠缠时却需要远多于前者的量子通信量。此类分离此前已知存在于关系型问题以及同时消息传递模型下的部分函数中，但对于全布尔函数是否也存在此类分离，一直悬而未决。

Method: 作者在单向设定下构造了一族全布尔函数 f_n: {0,1}^n × {0,1}^n → {0,1}，该函数是子群成员问题（subgroup membership problem）的特例，通信场景下最早由 Aaronson、Le Gall、Russell 和 Tani 研究。随后分别分析其在带预共享纠缠的经典单向通信和无纠缠的量子单向通信下的复杂度上界与下界。

Result: 构造的函数族在预先共享纠缠的情况下，可用 O(log n) 比特的经典单向通信计算；而在没有纠缠的情况下，则需要 Ω(n^{1/3}) 量子比特的单向量子通信。由此在全布尔函数上实现了通信复杂度的指数级分离。

Conclusion: 本文首次给出了全布尔函数在单向模型中纠缠带来的指数级通信优势，解决了量子通信复杂性中关于全布尔函数是否存在此类分离的长期开放问题。

Abstract: A longstanding question in quantum communication complexity is whether some task can be accomplished with a small amount of communication in the presence of entanglement, yet require much more quantum communication in the absence of entanglement. Separations of this nature were previously known for relational problems and, in the simultaneous message passing model, for partial functions. But it has remained unresolved whether any such separation exists for a total Boolean function. We resolve this question with an exponential separation in the one-way setting: we exhibit a family of total Boolean functions $f_n\colon \{0,1\}^n \times \{0,1\}^n \to \{0,1\}$ that can be computed with $O(\log n)$ bits of one-way classical communication given prior entanglement, but that require $Ω(n^{1/3})$ qubits of one-way quantum communication without entanglement. Our function is a special case of the subgroup membership problem, first studied in the communication setting by Aaronson, Le Gall, Russell, and Tani.

</details>


### [219] [On the pseudorandomness of simple quantum processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02100)
*Jesko Dujmovic,Jonas Haferkamp,Alexander Poremba*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Gowers关于简单局部随机可逆操作能否产生全局伪随机置换的猜想在量子酉情形下的类比，给出反例表明酉设计（统计矩匹配）一般不蕴含伪随机性，并提出简单量子过程如何产生伪随机性的新猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gowers（1996）猜想：反复组合局部随机可逆操作可得到与随机置换不可区分的全局置换。该猜想长期未解。作者希望借助量子酉类比来推进对这一经典问题的理解，并厘清“统计矩匹配（酉设计）”与“伪随机性”之间的关系。

Method: 构造高效可采样的分布族：1) 对每个固定t，给出定义在单量子比特和两量子比特门上的分布族，经T=O_t(n^2 log^2 n)步独立作用后，n量子比特系综为近似酉t-设计（误差exp(-Ω(log^2 n))），但高效量子算法仅用O_t(log^2 n)次查询即可将其与随机区分；2) 进一步给出多项式有界矩下更强的酉设计与伪随机酉的分离，但需高度结构化系综。基于此提出关于简单量子过程（如随机量子线路）中伪随机性如何产生的新猜想。

Result: 证明酉设计并不一般地导致伪随机性，反驳了Hoory–Magen–Myers–Rackoff猜想（ICALP '04）在置换上的酉类比；在多项式有界矩下得到更强的酉设计与伪随机酉的分离。结果提示用酉设计建模黑洞物理中的信息扰乱需谨慎，因为即使最大程度扰乱也可能存在高效实验可及的结构。

Conclusion: 统计矩匹配（酉设计）与伪随机性存在本质分离，简单局部量子过程的伪随机性不能仅由成为酉设计保证；作者提出关于简单量子过程（如随机量子线路）中伪随机性如何出现的新猜想，以指导后续研究。

Abstract: Can simple processes appear highly complex? Gowers (Comb. Prob. Comp. '96) conjectured that repeatedly composing local random reversible operations can yield global permutations that are indistinguishable from random. In this work, we study the unitary quantum analog of this question, in an attempt to make new progress on this longstanding conjecture.
  Our first result shows that statistical moment matching in the form of unitary designs does not generically lead to pseudorandomness---even for the simplest quantum processes: for every fixed $t$, we give an efficiently samplable family $\{ν_n\}_n$ of distributions on one- and two-qubit gates such that, after $T=O_t(n^2\log^2 n)$ independent steps, the resulting $n$-qubit ensemble is an approximate unitary $t$-design with negligible error $\exp(-Ω(\log^2 n))$, yet an efficient quantum algorithm distinguishes it from random using only $O_t(\log^2 n)$ queries. This refutes the unitary analog of the Hoory--Magen--Myers--Rackoff conjecture (ICALP '04) for permutations.
  Our second result is a stronger separation between unitary designs and pseudorandom unitaries at polynomially bounded moments; our counterexample, however, requires highly structured ensembles, in contrast with the simple local walks from before. This suggests caution when using unitary designs to model information scrambling in black-hole physics, as even maximally scrambled systems can exhibit structure which is accessible to efficient experiments. Motivated by these findings, we then propose new conjectures for how pseudorandomness can plausibly emerge within simple quantum processes, such as random quantum circuits.

</details>


### [220] [Time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02101)
*Fangqi Dong,Alex Lombardi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在随机预言机模型下证明了攻破量子密码学的近乎最优时间-空间下界，展示了量子密码学相对于经典密码学的新优势，并给出量子PRG、酉合成及随机二进制相位态伪随机性的多项改进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子密码学在预处理攻击（时间-空间权衡）下的安全性，探究量子密码学是否比经典密码学具有本质优势。已知后量子单向函数的最优界为O((T²+ST)/N)，且在S=N时存在平凡攻击，作者希望证明量子方案能在通信量仅n量子比特时，抵御空间高达N²的预处理攻击，从而展现量子密码学的新优势。

Method: 将最优预处理攻击表示为随机矩阵的算子范数，并利用迹矩方法（trace-moment method）在随机预言机上取期望进行界估计；这些迹矩借助压缩预言机（compressed oracles, Zhandry, Crypto 2019）获得自然解释并加以分析。该方法可视为对Liu（Eurocrypt 2023）用于证明后量子密码学时间-空间权衡方法的简化与推广。

Result: (1) 主结果：T次查询、S量子比特非均匀建议的对手从n量子比特二进制相位态|ψ_k⟩中恢复随机密钥k的概率至多为O((T²+√(ST))/N)，N=2^n；而数据表明S=N时仅需n量子比特通信即可抵御空间达N²的预处理攻击。(2) 收紧Liu对QROM中后量子PRG的分析，得到区分优势界O(T²/N+√(ST/N))。(3) 对酉合成，将Lombardi-Ma-Wright（STOC 2024）的单查询下界推广至可在函数查询前后做多项式多次（自适应）随机预言机查询的对手，并用压缩预言机解释原LMW24结果。(4) 证明随机二进制相位态对空间S区分者的伪随机性紧界为O(√S/N)。

Conclusion: 论文确立了量子密码学在随机预言机模型下抵御预处理攻击的时间-空间下界近乎最优，首次展示量子密码学相对经典密码学的通信效率优势，并对后量子PRG、酉合成下界及随机二进制相位态伪随机性给出改进或紧的结论，方法上统一并推广了压缩预言机与迹矩分析框架。

Abstract: We prove near-optimal time-space lower bounds for breaking quantum cryptography in the random oracle model. Specifically, we show that a $T$-query adversary with $S$ qubits of non-uniform advice can recover a random key $k$ from the $n$-qubit binary phase state $|ψ_k\rangle \propto \sum_{x} R(k,x) |x\rangle$ with probability at most $O(\frac{T^2 + \sqrt{ST}}{N})$ for $N=2^n$. In contrast, the best known bound for post-quantum one-way functions is $O(\frac{T^2 + ST}{N})$, with a trivial attack at $S = N$. This demonstrates a new advantage of quantum cryptography over classical cryptography: $n$ qubits of communication suffice for security against preprocessing attacks with space up to $N^2$ rather than $N$. Our methodology is simple: express the optimal preprocessing attack as the operator norm of a random matrix, and bound this value in expectation over the random oracle via the trace-moment method. These trace moments have a natural interpretation using compressed oracles [Zhandry, Crypto 2019], which we then analyze. This can be viewed as a simplification and generalization of the approach of Liu [Eurocrypt 2023] for proving time-space tradeoffs for breaking post-quantum cryptography. We also prove the following results: (1) We tighten Liu's analysis of post-quantum PRGs in QROM, achieving a distinguishing advantage bound of $O(\frac{T^2}N + \sqrt{\frac{ST}N})$. (2) For unitary synthesis, we extend the one-query lower bound of Lombardi-Ma-Wright [STOC 2024] to hold against adversaries that can make one arbitrary function query along with polynomially many (adaptive) queries to the random oracle, either before or after the function query. This also interprets the original LMW24 result in terms of compressed oracles. (3) Finally, we prove a tight $O(\frac{\sqrt{S}}N)$ bound for the pseudorandomness of random binary phase states against space $S$ distinguishers.

</details>


### [221] [Quantum Advantage for Two-Party Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02113)
*Daniel Alabi,Emil T. Khabiboulline*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出信息论安全的量子双方汉明距离协议（双方输出相同估计），在Klauck诚实、非抢占、消息保持模型下以O(n)通信实现O(1)期望误差，达到经典信息论不可达、仅在计算假设下可得的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息论隐私下汉明距离估计误差至少为Ω(√n)（纯DP）或Ω(√n/log n)（强近似DP），而O(1)误差仅能在计算安全性下实现。作者希望探究量子通信能否在信息论安全下突破该误差下界。

Method: 在Klauck模型中设计O(n)通信的量子协议，核心为"受守护的相干往返"与"等Gram刚性"原理，防止诚实方保留依赖输入的互补信息；对纯ε QDP给出误差上界(2/sinh ε)+γ，并通过hockey-stick散度精确计算近似(ε,δ) QDP下的误差。

Result: 纯QDP协议期望误差至多2/sinh ε+γ；近似QDP通过精确hockey-stick散度得到更小误差，且在δ=o(1/n)时保持O(1)与Ω(√n/log n)的分离，从而以量子通信实现O(1)信息论误差。

Conclusion: 该模型区别于较弱的指定信道隐私（已有精确经典实现）与完全抗保留安全（存在测量-中止攻击），表明保持非正交量子消息本身是一种可用的隐私资源。

Abstract: We introduce information-theoretically private quantum protocols for two-party Hamming distance when both parties must output the same estimate. Classically, for input length $n$, information-theoretic protocols require $Ω(\sqrt{n})$ error under pure differential privacy and $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ error under strong approximate differential privacy, whereas computational security permits $O(1)$ error. In Klauck's honest, nonpreemptive, message-preserving model, we give an $O(n)$-communication quantum protocol with pure $\varepsilon$ quantum differential privacy (QDP) and expected error at most $\frac{2}{\sinh \varepsilon}+γ$, for every $γ>0$. For approximate $(\varepsilon, δ)$ QDP, an exact hockey-stick divergence calculation yields strictly smaller error, while preserving the $O(1)$-versus-$Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ separation for $δ=o(1/n)$. Thus, quantum communication achieves $O(1)$ information-theoretic error, matching the accuracy available classically only under computational assumptions.
  The main construction uses a guarded coherent round trip and an equal-Gram rigidity principle that prevents an honest player from retaining input-dependent complementary information. We separate this model from weaker prescribed-channel privacy, which already admits an exact classical realization, and from fully retention-robust security, against which measurement-and-abort attacks remain possible. Therefore, we identify preservation of non-orthogonal quantum messages as a resource for privacy.

</details>


### [222] [Spiking neural networks for streaming qubit readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02129)
*Barry M. Dillon,Aqib Javed,Jim Harkin,Patryk Dabkowski,Benjamin Lienhard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用于超导量子比特读出的脉冲神经网络（SNN）判别器，以时间分块方式流式处理测量窗口，性能超越匹配滤波并接近全轨迹ANN精度，且经量化感知训练与hls4ml综合可在FPGA上实时完成每次推断更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器的反馈、校准和纠错需要快速准确的量子比特态判定。在超导平台中，频率复用的读出本质上是多变量问题：测量轨迹会编码串扰、量子比特态弛豫事件及其他瞬态非理想效应，而传统匹配滤波无法充分刻画这些特性，因此需要更强的判别方法。

Method: 采用脉冲神经网络（SNN）作为超导量子比特读出判别器，将测量窗口按连续时间块（time chunks）处理，利用时序结构并随数据到达不断更新分类得分，而非等到读出窗口结束。同时使用量化感知训练（quantisation-aware training）与hls4ml综合，将模型部署到FPGA上。

Result: 脉冲网络性能优于匹配滤波判别，精度接近全轨迹人工神经网络（ANN）。其关键优势在于能随读出信号采集过程提供流式的、时间分辨的量子比特态估计。借助量化感知训练与hls4ml综合，每次FPGA推断更新可在下一个读出时间块到达之前完成。

Conclusion: SNN为在FPGA硬件上实现低延迟、实时的量子比特读出提供了有前景的路径，并对时间关键的量子控制与科学推断应用具有更广泛的意义。

Abstract: Fast and accurate qubit-state assignment is essential for feedback, calibration, and error correction in quantum processors. In superconducting platforms, frequency-multiplexed readout makes this task intrinsically multivariate as measured traces can encode crosstalk, qubit-state relaxation events, and other transient nonidealities that are not fully captured by conventional matched filtering. Here, we introduce spiking neural network (SNN) discriminators for superconducting qubit readout. By processing the measurement window in successive time chunks, the networks exploit temporal structure and update classification scores as data arrive, rather than waiting until the end of the readout window. The spiking networks outperform matched-filter discrimination and approach the accuracy of a full-trace artificial neural network. Beyond reaching the performance of artificial neural networks, the key advantage of SNNs is that they provide a streaming, time-resolved estimate of the qubit state that evolves as the readout signal is acquired. Using quantisation-aware training and hls4ml synthesis, we further demonstrate that each FPGA inference update can be completed before the next readout chunk arrives. These results establish spiking neural networks as a promising route to low-latency, real-time qubit readout on FPGA hardware, with broader implications for time-critical quantum-control and scientific-inference applications.

</details>


### [223] [Quantum impurity models: easy at equilibrium, universal in motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02130)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sergey Bravyi,Anirban Chowdhury,Arkopal Dutt,Alexandru Gheorghiu,Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子杂质模型的平衡态性质可被经典算法高效逼近，而时间演化模拟却是BQP完全的，二者形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究嵌入自由费米子浴中的小规模相互作用杂质模型（量子杂质模型）的计算复杂度，厘清其基态能量、热平衡与动力学性质的计算难度差异。

Method: 利用按能量尺度与Krylov深度组织的基底下多粒子浴激发的指数抑制特性，设计经典算法计算基态能量与热平衡性质；并构造一个普适量子计算方案。

Result: (1) 基态能量可在poly(n,1/ε)时间内逼近至加性误差ε，优于此前最优算法的准多项式时间；(2) 逆温度β下，亥姆霍兹自由能与热场双态的经典描述可在poly(n,β,1/ε)时间内以精度ε计算；(3) 对时间无关且杂质尺寸固定的H，模拟e^{-iHt}是BQP完全的。

Conclusion: 平衡态性质经典可高效逼近，而时间演化却可实现普适量子计算；普适性构造实现了一个程序以自由传播费米子流形式到达的静态量子处理器。

Abstract: A quantum impurity model describes a small interacting subsystem embedded into a large bath of free fermions. Here we study the computational complexity of calculating the ground energy, thermal equilibrium, and dynamical properties of these models. Our work reveals a sharp contrast: equilibrium properties can be efficiently approximated by classical means, whereas time evolution can implement a universal quantum computation. More precisely, let $H$ be the Hamiltonian of an impurity model with $n$ fermionic modes and a constant-size impurity. We show that: (1) the ground energy of $H$ can be approximated to additive error $\varepsilon$ by a classical algorithm with runtime $\textsf{poly}(n,1/\varepsilon)$, improving on the quasi-polynomial runtime of the best previously known algorithm; (2) at inverse temperature $β$, the Helmholtz free energy and a classical description of the thermofield double state can be computed to precision $\varepsilon$ in time $\textsf{poly}(n,β,1/\varepsilon)$; (3) simulating the time evolution $e^{-iHt}$ is $\textsf{BQP}$-complete, for $H$ that is time-independent and has a fixed, constant impurity size. Our algorithms exploit exponential suppression of multi-particle bath excitations in a basis organized by energy scale and Krylov depth. Our universality construction realizes a stationary quantum processor whose program arrives in a stream of freely propagating fermions.

</details>


### [224] [Optimal transducers using symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02133)
*Benoît Dubus,Julien Ladeuze,Jérémie Roland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用对称群与表示论简化transducer的构造：证明最优catalyst可取协变形式、transducer可取等型分解下的块对角形式，从而系统导出无结构搜索、振幅放大与振幅估计的最优transducer及最优常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transducer是量子算法设计（尤其量子查询复杂度）中的有力框架：对偶adversary SDP的可行点可直接转化为transducer，且最优transduction复杂度等于adversary bound（即刻画有界误差量子查询复杂度的Las Vegas复杂度），并具有精确复合、开销小的优点。然而构造高效乃至最优的transducer仍很困难，因为需解adversary SDP并构造相应酉算子。本文旨在利用问题对称性降低这一难度。

Method: 针对状态转换问题，首先通过对称化论证（symmetrization）证明最优catalyst总可选取为在对称群某表示下协变（covariant）；其次证明transducer在群的两个不同表示之间交织（intertwine），因而可在Hilbert空间的等型分解（isotypic decomposition）中选为块对角形式。基于这两点将表示论方法系统化用于构造最优算法。

Result: 给出最优transducer及其最优常数的构造，覆盖多种常用量子算法原语，包括无结构搜索、振幅放大与振幅估计。该方法将此前用表示论计算adversary下界的工作（Høyer-Lee-Špalek 2007；Ambainis-Magnin-Roetteler-Roland 2011）扩展为对最优算法的系统性构造。

Conclusion: 对称群与表示论可同时简化adversary SDP求解与酉算子构造两步，使最优transducer的获得更加系统化，并推广至量子算法原语的显式最优算法设计。

Abstract: Transducers (Belovs, Jeffery and Yolcu, 2024) are a quantum computing framework describing a quantum algorithm as a unitary converting an input state into a target state using a catalyst, an auxiliary vector that is left unchanged. They are a powerful tool in quantum algorithm design, especially in the context of quantum query complexity: feasible points of the (dual) adversary semidefinite program directly translate into transducers and the optimal transduction complexity is equal to the adversary bound, i.e. the Las Vegas complexity, which is known to characterize bounded-error quantum query complexity. Moreover, contrary to bounded-error algorithms, transducers compose exactly, which limits overheads due to controlling errors in algorithms constructed by composition. Constructing efficient, let alone optimal, transducers in terms of quantum query complexity nevertheless remains a hard task since it still requires solving the adversary SDP and constructing the unitary to obtain an explicit algorithm. In this paper, we show how using the symmetry group of state-conversion problems simplifies both steps. First, using a symmetrization argument, we prove an optimal catalyst can always be chosen covariant under a representation of the symmetry group. Second, we prove that the transducer intertwines two different representations of the group and can thus be chosen block diagonal in the isotypic decomposition of the Hilbert space. Using those methods, we then derive optimal transducers, with optimal constants, for different widely used quantum algorithmic primitives, such as unstructured search, amplitude amplification and amplitude estimation. Our approach extends previous work on the use of representation theory to compute adversary lower bounds (Høyer, Lee, and {\v S}palek, 2007; Ambainis, Magnin, Roetteler and Roland, 2011) to the systematic construction of optimal algorithms.

</details>


### [225] [A Generalized quantum Stein lemma on von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02134)
*Li Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意von Neumann代数上，针对凸的、张量稳定的状态族与i.i.d.正态态之间的广义量子Stein引理：在存在与零假设相对熵有限的备择态条件下，任意I型错误容限ε∈(0,1)下最坏情况II型错误指数由正则化相对熵给出，并具有强逆性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子Stein引理刻画假设检验的最优错误指数，但既有结果多限于特定代数或状态族。本文旨在将量子Stein引理推广到任意von Neumann代数上的i.i.d.正态态对凸的、张量稳定族，以覆盖更一般的算子代数设定。

Method: 证明结合三项技术：相对熵的hockey-stick散度积分表示、模检验界（modular testing bound），以及凸极小极大（minimax）论证。

Result: 在备择态与零假设相对熵有限的假设下，对每个I型错误容限ε∈(0,1)，最坏情况II型错误指数由正则化相对熵达到，并满足强逆性质（strong converse）。

Conclusion: 该结果将量子Stein引理扩展至任意von Neumann代数上的i.i.d.正态态对凸张量稳定族，确立了正则化相对熵作为最优II型错误指数的刻画并带强逆。

Abstract: We prove a generalized quantum Stein lemma for i.i.d. normal states against convex, tensor-stable families on arbitrary von Neumann algebras. Assuming the existence of an alternative state with finite relative entropy from the null state, we show that, at every type-I error tolerance $\varepsilon\in (0,1)$, the worst case type-II error exponent is achieved with the regularized relative entropy with a strong converse. The proof combines the integral representation of relative entropy by hockey-stick divergences, a modular testing bound, and convex minimax argument.

</details>


### [226] [Random Quantum Circuits Beyond Moment Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02135)
*Shih-Han Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为强ε-近似酉k-设计所生成的单个输出概率分布相对于Porter-Thomas分布的逼近精度建立了定量保证，证明Kolmogorov距离上界为O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε)，该界在常数因子意义下最优；并证明局部不变性可带来对k的指数级改进，同时给出总变差距离保证，最后应用于输出分布Shannon熵的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路旨在高效复现理想随机量子演化的统计性质，近似酉设计通过矩匹配实现这一点，但此前缺乏关于这些设计能否准确复现各输出概率完整分布的定量刻画。作者希望明确矩匹配所能保证的分布精度，以及何种额外结构能显著提升该精度。

Method: 利用Kolmogorov距离与总变差距离作为度量，将任意强ε-近似酉k-设计的单个输出概率分布与有限维Porter-Thomas分布（参数为1和2^n-1的beta分布）进行比较；对具有作用于log k+O(1)量子比特的酉平移不变性的设计单独分析，推导更紧的误差界；并建立边际分布误差与输出分布Shannon熵期望偏差之间的联系。

Result: 对每个强ε-近似酉k-设计，单个输出概率分布与有限维Porter-Thomas分布的Kolmogorov距离不超过O(√(2^n/(k(k+2^n)))+ε)，且该上界在常数因子意义下最优。若设计额外具有作用在log k+O(1)量子比特上的酉平移不变性，则Kolmogorov距离误差降至2^{-Ω(k)}+O(ε)，总变差距离误差为2^{-Ω(k)}+O(εlog(2/ε))，即对k的依赖获得指数级改进。作为应用，若强近似设计的每个输出概率边际分布与Haar对应物的Kolmogorov距离为η，则输出分布期望Shannon熵与Haar值的偏差为O(√η)。

Conclusion: 矩匹配本身即可保证输出概率分布以最优速率逼近Porter-Thomas分布，而局部（平移）不变性可在此基础上带来指数级精度提升，并同时改善总变差距离。这些结果既刻画了矩匹配的分布精度极限，也指明了突破该极限所需的额外结构，并为输出分布熵的估计提供了工具。

Abstract: Random quantum circuits aim to efficiently reproduce the statistical properties of ideal random quantum evolution. One approach is to construct approximate unitary designs, which match the moments of Haar-random unitaries up to a prescribed order with controlled error. In this work, we establish quantitative guarantees for how accurately these designs reproduce the full distributions of individual output probabilities. We show that, for every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design on $n$ qubits, the distribution of each individual output probability is within $O\left(\sqrt{\frac{2^n}{k(k+2^n)}}+\varepsilon\right)$ in Kolmogorov distance of the finite-dimensional Porter-Thomas distribution, a beta distribution with parameters $1$ and $2^n-1$. This bound is optimal up to constant factors, implying that a substantially better uniform bound requires additional structure. We further show that local invariance yields an exponential improvement in the dependence on $k$, with guarantees also in the stronger metric of total variation: every strong $\varepsilon$-approximate unitary $k$-design invariant under unitary translations acting on $\log k+O(1)$ qubits achieves error $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon)$ in Kolmogorov distance and $2^{-Ω(k)}+O(\varepsilon\log(2/\varepsilon))$ in total variation distance. These results identify both the distributional accuracy guaranteed by moment matching and the additional structure that substantially improves it. As an application, we show that if the marginal distribution of each output probability of a strong approximate design is within Kolmogorov distance $η$ of its Haar counterpart, then the expected Shannon entropy of the output distribution differs from its Haar value by $O(\sqrtη)$.

</details>


### [227] [A provable quantum advantage for approximate optimization via decoded quantum interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02145)
*Maximilian J. Kramer,Elies Gil-Fuster,Benjamin D. M. Jones,Jens Eisert,Franz J. Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在oracle设定下证明DQI在折叠最优多项式交集（folded OPI）问题上相对所有多项式时间经典算法具有严格近似比优势，并通过改进算法进一步扩大该量子分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DQI是一种具有强性能保证、利用优化与编码理论对偶性的量子近似优化新范式，但其能否可证明地超越所有多项式时间经典算法这一核心问题尚待解决。

Method: 考虑接受集随机选取、经成员oracle访问的folded OPI优化任务；基于Jordan等人的DQI框架，将Yamakawa与Zhandry精确搜索oracle分离所依赖的经典下界方法推广到近似情形；并结合Sun与Wootters、Horinaga与Yamakawa、Jo等近期工作，构造改进版DQI算法。

Result: 建立了任意多项式时间经典算法可达近似比与DQI算法近似比之间的严格差距；改进版DQI在folded OPI上取得更大差距。以码率0.3为例，DQI与改进算法的期望得分约为0.85与0.95，而经典算法需超多项式多次成员查询才能以常数概率超过0.65的经典阈值一定量。

Conclusion: DQI在oracle模型下可证明地优于所有多项式时间经典算法，改进算法带来更强量子分离，为量子近似优化的优势提供了严格证据。

Abstract: Decoded quantum interferometry (DQI) is a novel paradigm for tackling approximate optimization problems on quantum computers. This framework comes with strong performance guarantees and exploits a well-established duality between optimization and coding theory. A central question, however, is whether DQI can actually provably outperform all polynomial-time classical algorithms. In this work, we establish such an advantage in an oracle setting: we consider an optimization task called folded optimal polynomial intersection (folded OPI), where the acceptance sets are chosen randomly and accessed through membership oracles. We establish a strict gap between the approximation ratio achievable by any polynomial-time classical algorithm and the approximation ratio achieved by the DQI algorithm. Our proof builds on Jordan et al.'s DQI framework for approximate optimization and extends the classical lower-bound method underlying Yamakawa and Zhandry's exact-search oracle separation to approximation. Building on recent developments by Sun and Wootters, Horinaga and Yamakawa, and Jo, we further show that a modified version of the DQI algorithm achieves a strictly larger gap on the folded OPI problem, yielding an even stronger quantum separation. As a concrete example, for code rate $0.3$, DQI and the modified algorithm achieve expected scores of approximately $0.85$ and $0.95$, respectively. In contrast, exceeding the classical threshold of $0.65$ by any fixed amount with constant probability on sampled instances requires super-polynomially many classical membership queries.

</details>


### [228] [Polynomial-time additive-error estimation of output probabilities for shallow quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02146)
*Matthew Coudron,Michael J. Gullans,Jon Nelson,Joel Rajakumar,Shi Jie Samuel Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性经典算法，可在 poly(n, 1/ε) 时间内估计常数深度量子电路 U 的输出概率 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2 至加性误差 ε，显著改进了此前最优算法的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计常数深度量子电路特定输出比特串的概率幅度是量子计算中的核心问题。此前最优经典算法需 n^{O(log n)} 时间，几何局域情形为 n^{O(log log n)}，二维几何局域电路为 n^{O(1)}。作者希望突破这一复杂度瓶颈。

Method: 给出一种确定性经典算法。所处理的电路 U 为由有界扇入门构成、具有任意连接性的常数深度量子电路；算法目标是在 poly(n, 1/ε) 时间内将 |⟨x|U|0^n⟩|^2 估计到加性误差 ε，x 为任意 n 比特输出串。

Result: 算法在 poly(n, 1/ε) 时间内完成估计，达到加性误差 ε。该复杂度优于此前 n^{O(log n)}（一般情形）、n^{O(log log n)}（几何局域）以及 n^{O(1)}（二维几何局域）的算法。

Conclusion: 通过确定性经典算法将常数深度量子电路输出概率估计的时间复杂度降至多项式级，改进了该问题此前已知的最优上界。

Abstract: We give a deterministic classical algorithm that estimates $|\langle x|U|0^n\rangle|^2$ to additive error $\varepsilon$ in $\mathrm{poly}(n, 1/\varepsilon)$ time, where $U$ is a constant-depth quantum circuit comprised of gates with bounded fan-in and arbitrary connectivity, and $x$ is an arbitrary $n$-bit output string. This improves over prior state-of-the-art algorithms that takes $n^{O(log(n))}$ time for the same task, $n^{O(log(log(n))}$ when $U$ is geometrically local, and $n^{O(1)}$ for 2D geometrically-local circuits.

</details>


### [229] [The Robustness of QAC0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02154)
*Daniel Grier,Jackson Morris,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 的两类鲁棒性：一是消除其计算布尔函数时固有的非零误差，二是限制单量子比特门集合后其计算能力是否保持。作者证明并行 $W$-检验的误差可通过精确振幅放大完全消除，从而精确 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 能以多项式多份输入副本模拟 $\mathsf{TC}^0$，且对每个素数 $p$，精确 $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$ 可计算 $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$ 之外的全布尔函数；同时证明任意 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路可被仅由广义 Toffoli、$S$ 和 Hadamard 门构成的电路近似实现，且该近似电路可由原电路的经典描述高效构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 通常允许非零误差，且允许在任意位置使用任意单量子比特门。作者希望检验这两项设定是否为计算能力所必需：即非零误差是否可去除，以及计算能力是否依赖于任意单量子比特门的可用性。

Method: 第一，针对 \cite{grier_morris_wu} 的并行 $W$-检验，在多副本情形下应用一种新颖的精确振幅放大技术，消除其固有误差。第二，构造仅使用广义 Toffoli、$S$、Hadamard 门的 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路，从原电路的经典描述出发高效近似实现任意 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路。

Result: 精确 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 可以借助多项式多份经典输入副本精确模拟 $\mathsf{TC}^0$；对每个固定素数 $p$，精确 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$（即 $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$）能计算 $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$ 之外的全布尔函数。此外，任意 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路都可由仅含广义 Toffoli、$S$ 与 Hadamard 门的 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 电路近似实现，且该电路可高效构造。

Conclusion: $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 对误差容限和门集合限制均具有鲁棒性：其非零误差并非必要，且其计算能力不依赖于任意单量子比特门的使用；受限门集合下的 $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ 仍具备原有近似计算能力并可高效编译。

Abstract: In this work we study the robustness of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ with respect to error tolerance and modifications to its gate-set. First, we investigate whether the non-zero error typically allowed for $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuits computing Boolean functions is truly necessary. We show that the error inherent in the parallel $W$-test of \cite{grier_morris_wu} can be eliminated entirely via a novel application of exact amplitude amplification in the many-copies context. Consequently, we find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ can \textit{exactly} simulate $\mathsf{TC}^0$ with polynomially many copies of the classical input and that for every fixed prime $p$ exact $\mathsf{QAC}^0$, $\mathsf{EQAC}^0$, can compute total Boolean functions outside of $\mathsf{AC}^0[p]$.
  Second, we ask to what extent the computational power of $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ follows from the fact that arbitrary single-qubit gates may be used at any point in the circuit. We find that $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ is in fact robust to restrictions on which single-qubit gates are permitted: every $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit can be approximately implemented by a $\mathsf{QAC}^0$ circuit consisting of just generalized Toffoli, $S$, and Hadamard gates. Moreover, this approximating circuit can be constructed efficiently from a classical description of the original circuit.

</details>


### [230] [Learning Many-Body Hamiltonians Using a Local Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02157)
*Suying Liu,Zitai Xu,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Xiaodi Wu,Yu-Xin Wang,Zhi-Yuan Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 仅用单个可测量量子比特，在连通参考骨干与快速单比特控制下，可在海森堡极限内学习N比特有界度二体哈密顿量的全部O(N)独立参数，链上总查询时间达Õ(N)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿量学习协议通常假设可对整个系统直接测量，这在实验上受限；本文旨在放宽该假设，探索仅通过局部测量接口学习多体哈密顿量大量参数的可能性。

Method: 利用快速单比特控制与连通的参考骨干，借助量子信号处理合成的鲁棒SWAP门，将远处哈密顿量参数下演化的态相干转移到可测量比特；该转移无需中间链路哈密顿量参数的先验校准。进一步设计并行学习架构，并推广到任意有界度相互作用图。

Result: 单个可测量比特即可学习N比特有界度二体哈密顿量全部O(N)个独立参数，达到海森堡极限精度；chain上总查询时间为Õ(N)且保持海森堡极限精度标度，与精度和信息传播下界在对数因子内匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了仅通过局部测量接口学习大量多体哈密顿量参数的可扩展路径，为哈密顿量学习提供了一种新的资源高效方案。

Abstract: Hamiltonian learning provides a systematic framework for reconstructing unknown quantum dynamics. However, existing protocols typically assume direct measurement access to the entire system. With fast single-qubit control and a connected reference backbone, we show that a single measurable qubit suffices to learn all $O(N)$ independent parameters of a bounded-degree two-body Hamiltonian on $N$ qubits at the Heisenberg limit. Crucially, our protocol uses robust SWAP gates synthesized by quantum signal processing, enabling coherent transfer of states evolving under distant Hamiltonian parameters to the measurable qubit. This transfer requires no prior calibration of the Hamiltonian parameters of the intermediate links. A parallel learning architecture achieves total query time $\widetilde{O}(N)$ for an $N$-qubit chain, while retaining Heisenberg-limited precision scaling. On the chain, these scalings match the fundamental precision and information-propagation lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. The framework further extends to arbitrary bounded-degree interaction graphs. Our results establish a scalable route to learning an extensive number of many-body Hamiltonian parameters through only a local measurement interface.

</details>


### [231] [Robust exponential lower bounds for fermionic and bosonic Gaussian ranks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02172)
*Fuchuan Wei,Kong-Wing Wu,Zhengwei Liu,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究玻色与费米系统中高斯秩问题，证明了任意纯非高斯态的张量幂的近似边界高斯秩随模数指数增长，得到稳健的指数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟的能力与局限是理解量子计算优势的核心。主流的模拟范式基于将量子态相干分解为经典可处理的自由态（此处为高斯态），分解秩决定模拟复杂度。然而证明高斯秩的强下界是极为困难且数学上丰富的问题，量子比特稳定子秩问题即为典型；高斯版本此前缺乏稳健的指数下界。

Method: 作者在玻色与费米系统中分别建立高斯秩的指数下界：证明对任意有限的纯非高斯态（费米情形要求确定宇称），其张量幂的近似边界高斯秩在任意固定小于一的范数误差下至少指数增长。费米情形的证明结合了到四模的归约与Majorana谱界；玻色情形则结合高斯后选择与基于熵的秩界。

Result: 得到了稳健的指数下界：对四模费米GHZ态与玻色单光子态导出显式的指数下界。玻色结果不依赖目标态能量的假设。

Conclusion: 结果表明非高斯性普遍意味着指数级的高斯分解复杂度，为基于分解的玻色与费米系统经典模拟设定了根本性限制。

Abstract: The power and limitations of classical simulation are central to understanding quantum computational advantages. A leading simulation paradigm is based on coherent decomposition into classically tractable free states where the decomposition rank determines the simulation complexity. Proving strong lower bounds on this number is a notoriously difficult and mathematically rich problem, as exemplified by the qubit stabilizer rank problem. Here, we study the Gaussian version of this problem in both bosonic and fermionic systems and establish robust exponential lower bounds on Gaussian rank. In particular, we prove that for every pure non-Gaussian state on finitely many modes, with definite parity in the fermionic case, the approximate border Gaussian rank of its tensor powers grows at least exponentially at any fixed norm error below one. Our proofs combine reduction to four modes with Majorana spectral bounds for fermions, and Gaussian postselection with an entropy-based rank bound for bosons. As concrete examples, we derive explicit exponential lower bounds for the four-mode fermionic GHZ state and the bosonic single-photon state. The bosonic results require no assumption on the energy of the target state. Our results show that non-Gaussianity universally entails exponential Gaussian decomposition complexity, setting fundamental limitations on decomposition-based classical simulation of bosonic and fermionic systems.

</details>


### [232] [From Permutation Symmetry to Communication Bounds and Additivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02176)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Debbie Leung,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明对任意有限维无记忆信道，全置换不变性限制了跨信道使用关联带来的优势：每使用的优化相干信息收敛于单次使用最大值，并在有限块长处达到上确界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨信道使用的关联可提升量子通信速率，但优化需在任意大块上进行，这是确定量子容量的核心困难；作者希望借助对称性（置换不变性）来刻画这种关联优势的极限。

Method: 利用全置换不变性与De Finetti归约、置换协方差，对有限维无记忆信道的优化相干信息进行渐近分析；并在统一的变分框架下研究完全有界范数与夹心Rényi信道量。

Result: 每使用的优化相干信息收敛到单次使用最大值，并在有限块长处取到上确界；有限块超可加性仍可能存在，而渐近优化约化为单次信道使用。对纯源纠缠生成给出强逆定理：速率高于单次相干信息最大值时保真度趋于零。同时为完全有界范数与夹心Rényi信道量的已知乘性与加性结果提供统一变分证明，并证明置换不变输入下输出熵每使用不能低于单次最小值（适用于Rényi阶大于1及冯诺依曼熵）。

Conclusion: 置换对称性将渐近量子通信优化约化为单次使用问题，并把有限块超可加性限制在有限块长；对称性从通信界推广到输出熵控制，并统一了若干范数与Rényi量的乘性/加性结果。

Abstract: Correlations across channel uses can improve quantum communication rates, making optimization over arbitrarily large blocks a central difficulty in determining quantum capacity. We show how full permutation invariance limits this advantage for every finite-dimensional memoryless channel. The optimized coherent information per use converges to the single-use maximum and consequently attains its supremum at a finite block length. Thus finite-block superadditivity remains possible, while the asymptotic optimization reduces to a single channel use. We also establish a strong converse for pure-source entanglement generation with permutation-invariant inputs: at any fixed rate above the single-use coherent-information maximum, the fidelity tends to zero. Beyond these communication bounds, we identify a common structure underlying completely bounded norms and the sandwiched Rényi channel quantities governing discrimination against a replacer and entanglement-assisted communication. De Finetti reduction and permutation covariance yield systematic alternative proofs of their known multiplicativity and additivity results within a shared variational framework. We further show that, for permutation-invariant inputs, correlations cannot sustain an asymptotic reduction in output entropy per use below the single-use minimum. This entropy limit holds for Rényi orders greater than one and for the von Neumann entropy, extending the role of symmetry from communication bounds to the control of output entropy.

</details>


### [233] [Learning SYK Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02178)
*Anurag Anshu,Srinivasan Arunachalam,Sitan Chen,Yeongwoo Hwang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究从Gibbs态副本中学习稠密SYK哈密顿量的问题，突破传统方法对几何局域性或有限相互作用度的依赖，利用模型的随机平均场结构，证明在恒定温度下可多项式样本学到逆多项式精度，并在足够小的逆温度下给出准多项式时间学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的哈密顿量学习算法通常依赖几何局域性或有限的相互作用度，而SYK模型中每个四次相互作用与Θ(n³)个其他项重叠，稠密耦合结构使这些方法失效，因此需要新的思路来克服这一障碍。

Method: 利用SYK模型的随机平均场结构，从Gibbs态副本中提取耦合信息。分析在恒定温度下，对SYK耦合的高概率情形，设计能够处理稠密相互作用的样本高效学习方案；并在逆温度为足够小常数时，构造准多项式时间的学习算法。

Result: 在任意恒定温度下，以高概率（对SYK耦合）可用多项式数量的样本将整个哈密顿量学到逆多项式精度；当逆温度限制为足够小的常数时，构造出准多项式时间学习算法，该算法在性质上不同于样本高效算法。

Conclusion: 稠密SYK哈密顿量虽不满足传统学习的局域性或低度假设，但通过 exploiting 随机平均场结构，仍可实现多项式样本的逆多项式精度学习；且在不同温度区间可得到在样本复杂度与计算复杂度上各有侧重、性质不同的学习算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning the dense Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian from copies of its Gibbs state. Existing algorithms for Hamiltonian learning typically rely on geometric locality or bounded interaction degree and therefore do not apply to SYK, where each quartic interaction overlaps with $Θ(n^3)$ others. We show that this obstruction can be overcome by exploiting the random mean-field structure of the model. At any constant temperature, we prove that with high probability over the SYK couplings, the entire Hamiltonian can be learned to inverse-polynomial accuracy using polynomially many samples. Furthermore, when the inverse temperature is restricted to be a sufficiently small constant, we construct a quasipolynomial-time learning algorithm which is qualitatively different from the sample-efficient algorithm.

</details>


### [234] [Single-Particle Spectral Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02183)
*Adrian Chapman,Charles Derby,Steven T. Flammia,Yeongwoo Hwang,Joel Klassen,Calum McCartney*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究被全局酉变换隐藏的非相互作用哈密顿量的学习问题，证明其DQC1困难，并提出SPICES算法高效恢复谱分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在材料建模中，平均场理论、格林函数和密度泛函理论等经典方法广泛依赖单粒子谱。然而，当哈密顿量的非相互作用结构被未知全局酉变换U隐藏时，如何在不重构U的前提下恢复单粒子能量成为关键问题。这为量子计算在材料模拟中的应用提供了新动机。

Method: 1) 问题设定：学习H=U†(∑_{i∈I} E_i n_i + H_O)U中非相互作用模式I的权重E_i，其中U未知、H_O为作用在不相交模式O上的旁观者项；2) 复杂性分析：证明该问题即使存在非相互作用结构的明显简化，仍为DQC1-hard；3) 提出SPICES算法：结合Hadamard测试与新颖的经典后处理，利用单粒子能量与复逆温度下配分函数零点对应关系的傅里叶频率，在自然假设下以Wasserstein距离高效恢复谱分布。

Result: 该学习问题被证明为DQC1-hard，表明即使有非相互作用结构的承诺，问题仍具有量子计算难度。SPICES算法在若干自然假设下，能够高效地恢复谱分布（以Wasserstein距离度量），且无需重构全局酉变换U。

Conclusion: 单粒子谱在下游材料计算中普遍出现，SPICES方法简单且易于实际执行，因此有望成为材料模拟中量子应用的有前景路径。

Abstract: We study Hamiltonian learning in a novel setting, where we are promised a physical structure that is obfuscated by a global unitary. In particular, we consider the task of learning the weights $E_i$ of a Hamiltonian $H=U^{\dagger}(\sum_{i \in I} E_i n_i + H_O)U$ comprised of a set of non-interacting modes $I$ and an arbitrary ``spectator'' $H_O$ acting on a disjoint set of modes $O$. The non-interacting structure is hidden by an unknown global unitary $U$, and our goal is to recover the $E_i$ without reconstructing $U$. Such Hamiltonians are well-motivated in the setting of materials modeling, where they find application for classical methods such as mean-field theory, Green's functions, and density functional theory. We show that this problem is DQC1-hard, despite the apparent simplification given by the non-interacting structure. We then introduce SPICES (single-particle inference via contour estimators), which combines a Hadamard test with a novel classical postprocessing procedure to efficiently recover the spectral distribution in Wasserstein distance under certain natural assumptions. The method exploits the correspondence between single-particle energies of the individual quasiparticles and the Fourier frequencies governing zeros of the partition function at complex inverse temperature. As quasiparticle spectra feature ubiquitously in downstream materials calculations, and with SPICES being simple and practical to execute, we expect that SPICES is a promising route to quantum applications in materials simulation.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [235] [Expanding quantum Proca field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00014)
*Bogdan Damski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究耦合外流的Proca理论，探讨电荷非守恒及纵场量子描述，分析电荷产生引起的因果演化，发现含移动前沿的膨胀Proca场非平衡量子态，并揭示点电荷产生中的激波结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Proca理论允许外流对应电荷不守恒，且能给出由电荷分布产生的纵向场量子描述；作者希望深入探究这些特性，特别是电荷产生过程中Proca场因果形成的量子动力学。

Method: 研究耦合外流的实质量矢量场Proca理论，进行量子化处理；通过分析电荷产生过程后Proca场因果形成的量子动力学，构造非平衡量子态，并与静态Proca场量子态比较；重点讨论瞬时开启外流的情形，并分析点电荷与扩展电荷产生问题。

Result: 所得非平衡量子态表现为膨胀的Proca场伴随移动前沿；与静态Proca场量子态存在本质差异；点电荷产生问题中出现激波结构；扩展电荷产生问题亦被讨论。

Conclusion: 详细讨论了外流存在下Proca理论的量子化及其一般量子动力学结果，强调瞬时开启外流问题，并揭示电荷产生过程中的激波结构。

Abstract: We consider the Proca theory of a real massive vector field coupled to an external current. This theory allows for non-conservation of the charge associated with the external current. It is so because the total conserved current is given by the sum of the external current and the internal current associated with the Proca field, neither of which is required to be conserved separately. Moreover, this theory admits the quantum description of a longitudinal field generated by a charge distribution. We explore both features by studying the quantum dynamics underlying the causal formation of the Proca field following the charge-creation process. The resulting non-equilibrium quantum state exhibits an expanding Proca field accompanied by a moving front. We compare this state with the one representing the corresponding static Proca field and identify fundamental differences between these states. The quantization of the Proca theory in the presence of an external current is discussed in detail. General results concerning the quantum dynamics of this theory are presented. Particular emphasis is placed on the problem of the instantaneous switching-on of an otherwise static external current. An intriguing shockwave structure is found in the point-charge creation problem, the extended-charge creation problem is also discussed.

</details>


### [236] [M1 Radiative Transitions in the $B_c$ System after the $B_c^\ast(1S)$ Observation: Radial-Node Cancellation and Quark-Line Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00138)
*Bing-Dong Wan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于相对论Salpeter波函数研究$B_c$介子的四个M1辐射跃迁，给出$B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)\gamma$宽度约$5.24\times10^{-2}$ keV，并揭示阻碍跃迁源于径向节点的严重相消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATLAS对$B_c^*(1S)$的观测确定了基态超精细劈裂，从而确定了$B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)\gamma$的相空间，为系统研究$B_c$介子M1辐射跃迁提供了实验锚点，同时$B_c^*(2S)$质量未知成为相关预言的主要不确定性来源。

Method: 采用标定到实测基态质量的相对论Salpeter波函数，计算四个1S与2S态的M1辐射跃迁；并对跃迁振幅做夸克线分辨分析，考察径向节点处的相消行为。

Result: 得到$Γ[B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)γ]=5.24\times10^{-2}$ keV；容许跃迁无径向相消，而阻碍的$2S\to1S$振幅仅为径向节点处约二十倍相消的残余；交叉阻碍道中$b$发射贡献存在$\mathcal O(10^2)$量级内部相消，$\bar c$贡献保持$\mathcal O(20)$量级，小$b$流源于增强的相消而非更小的非符号贡献。

Conclusion: $B_c$介子M1辐射跃迁可由相对论Salpeter方法自洽描述，阻碍跃迁的宽度由径向波函数节点的相消效应主导；$B_c^*(2S)$质量未测是目前限制$2S\to2S$辐射宽度预言精度的主要因素。

Abstract: The recent ATLAS observation of the $B_c^*(1S)$ meson fixes the ground-state hyperfine splitting and thereby determines the phase space for $B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)γ$. We study four $1S$ and $2S$ M1 radiative transitions using relativistic Salpeter wave functions calibrated to the measured ground-state masses, obtaining $Γ[B_c^*(1S)\to B_c(1S)γ]=5.24\times10^{-2}$~keV. The allowed transitions show no radial cancellation, whereas the hindered $2S\to1S$ amplitudes are residuals of an approximately twenty-fold cancellation across the radial node. A quark-line-resolved analysis shows a strong asymmetry in the crossed hindered channel: the $b$-emission contribution undergoes an $\mathcal O(10^2)$ internal cancellation while the $\bar c$ contribution remains at $\mathcal O(20)$. The corresponding unsigned line strengths show that the small $b$-current is generated by enhanced cancellation rather than by a smaller unsigned contribution. The unmeasured $B_c^*(2S)$ mass remains the main limitation on the $2S\to2S$ radiative width.

</details>


### [237] [Dark-State Interference in an Effective Confined Three-Level Color System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00146)
*S. D. Campos*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建QCD启发的有效模型，用类EIT的暗态干涉描述三态禁闭系统中色电探针吸收的相干抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子光学暗态干涉与禁闭色动力学的受控有效联系，解释介质不透明度的相干抑制机制。

Method: 三态系统（两个低能Q-Qbar态|1>、|2>与胶子激发杂化态|H>），静态色电场管背景进入未微扰哈密顿量，含时色电微扰以Λ型耦合，采用马尔可夫GKSL主方程，在双"光子"共振下分析稳态探测磁化率。

Result: 系统形成与|H>解耦的相干叠加态，在下态退相干消失时强烈抑制稳态探测磁化率Im χ_CE；杂化能的非阿贝尔唯象修正主要移动和重塑透明窗口，而非产生干涉本身。

Conclusion: 该模型并非介质不透明度的第一性原理QCD计算，而是暗态干涉与约化禁闭色动力学之间的受控有效联系。

Abstract: In this work, a QCD-inspired effective model describes dark-state interference in a confined three-state system with two low-energy $Q\overline{Q}$ states, $|1\rangle$ and $|2\rangle$, and a gluon-excited hybrid state, $|H\rangle$. A stationary chromoelectric flux-tube background enters the unperturbed Hamiltonian, while a time-dependent chromoelectric perturbation couples $|1\rangle$ and $|2\rangle$ to $|H\rangle$ in a $Λ$-type configuration. Under two-photon\footnote{We retain standard terms like ``two-photon'' from the EIT literature only by mathematical analogy, but they do not refer to electromagnetic photons but to the effective frequencies and energy mismatches of chromoelectric perturbations and gluonic excitations that connect confined states.} resonance and within a Markovian Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad master equation, the system forms a coherent superposition that decouples from $|H\rangle$, strongly suppressing the steady-state probe susceptibility $\operatorname{Im}χ_{\text{CE}}$ as lower-state decoherence vanishes. A phenomenological non-Abelian correction to the hybrid energy mainly shifts and reshapes the transparency window rather than creating the interference itself. The model is not a first-principles QCD calculation of medium opacity but a controlled effective link between dark-state interference in quantum optics and reduced confined color dynamics.

</details>


### [238] [Thermal Higgsino Dark Matter in GmSUGRA for the LZ Event and Solar-Neutrino Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00226)
*Imtiaz Khan,Waqas Ahmed,Tianjun Li,Shabbar Raza,Ali Muhammad*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在GmSUGRA边界条件下研究了MSSM热Higgsino暗物质，结合LUX-ZEPLIN高反冲事件与IceCube太阳中微子限制；LZ偏好能区被太阳俘获限制排除，但更大劈裂的高标度分支仍存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN高反冲事件暗示亚MeV级中性态劈裂与热Higgsino质量尺度，而IceCube太阳中微子限制对其形成约束，需检验该参数空间能否在GUT动机的超对称构造中动力学实现。

Method: 采用GmSUGRA非普适软项边界条件，Higgs扇区使用NUHM2型参数化（输入μ与m_A），进行数值扫描寻找Planck兼容的Higgsino分支。

Result: 扫描得到热质量附近的Planck兼容Higgsino分支，中性态劈裂从几百keV连续延伸至MeV量级；其中Δm≈0.3–0.4 MeV部分与LZ事件运动学区间重叠，但被当前太阳俘获再解释排除；Δm≈0.85、1.10、1.34 MeV的代表性谱超出太阳俘获限制而存活。

Conclusion: LZ高反冲解释所偏好的质量和亚MeV劈裂可在GUT动机的超对称框架中动力学实现，无需将中性态压缩作为弱标度输入；联合数据仅挑选共同GmSUGRA Higgsino分支的不同区段，而非排除热Higgsino构造本身。

Abstract: We study the thermal Higgsino dark matter in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model (MSSM) with Generalized Minimal Supergravity (GmSUGRA) boundary conditions after the high-recoil event reported by the LUX--ZEPLIN experiment and the subsequent solar-neutrino limits inferred from IceCube data. The Higgs sector is implemented in a NUHM2-type parametrization with $μ$ and $m_A$ as inputs, while the supersymmetry breaking soft terms obey the non-universal GmSUGRA relations. The numerical scan contains a Planck-compatible Higgsino branch near the thermal mass, with the neutral-state splitting extending continuously from the few-hundred-keV region to the MeV scale. The part of this branch with $\dmchi\simeq0.3$--$0.4$ MeV overlaps the kinematic interval relevant to the LZ event, but is disfavored by the current solar-capture reinterpretations for a full-density thermal Higgsino. The same high-scale branch persists at larger splittings, including representative spectra with $\dmchi\simeq0.85$, $1.10$, and $1.34$ MeV, beyond the quoted solar-capture limits. Our results demonstrate that the mass and sub-MeV splitting favored by the LZ high-recoil interpretation can be realized dynamically within a GUT-motivated supersymmetric construction, without imposing the neutralino compression as a weak-scale input. The combined data therefore select different parts of a common GmSUGRA Higgsino branch rather than excluding the thermal Higgsino construction itself.

</details>


### [239] [Fractional anomalous determinants and the chiral phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00472)
*Robert D. Pisarski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 高温瞬子气体下 \(U(1)_A\) 反常破缺由反常行列式的整数幂给出；作者提出手征相变以下拓扑荷分数化为整数乘以 \(1/N_c\)，从而在手征有效拉格朗日量中出现分数幂，推广了 Witten-Veneziano 项，并预测手征相变一般为二级，除一风味（可能 crossover）与三风味（可能弱一级）外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高温下瞬子形成稀薄气体，反常 \(U(1)_A\) 破缺由 \((\det\Phi)^Q\) 的整数幂描述且拓扑荷为整数；但在手征相变以下，'t Hooft 等人的图像可能要求拓扑荷分数化，以正确描述手征有效理论中的反常效应。作者旨在构造一个在高温与低温都自洽、且"beyond Landau"的模型，并推广 Witten-Veneziano 项至任意 \(N_f/N_c\)。

Method: 构造"syncretic"模型：在手征极限且 \(T>T_\chi\) 时仅出现反常行列式整数幂；在 \(T<T_\chi\) 时假设拓扑荷 \(Q\) 为整数乘以 \(1/N_c\)，据此在含 \(N_f\) 个简并风味的手征有效拉格朗日量中引入反常行列式的分数幂，从而将适用于小 \(N_f/N_c\) 的 Witten-Veneziano 项推广到任意 \(N_f/N_c\)。分析手征相变阶数，并给出晶格 QCD 检验方案，以及对称相中重子数算符的提议。

Result: 模型预测手征相变一般（generically）为二级相变；例外是单风味（可能为 crossover）和三风味（可能为弱一级相变）。可在格点 QCD 中用 \(2+1\) 风味，通过比较 \(T_\chi\) 到 \(\sim 2T_\chi\) 间 \(\pi^a\) 与 \(a_0^a\) 传播子之差的已知温度依赖与奇异夸克手征凝聚进行检验；对一至四简并风味在 \(T_\chi\) 附近也可做类似测量。最后提出了对称相中重子数算符。

Conclusion: 作者提出以分数拓扑荷、反常行列式分数幂为核心的手征有效理论，统一高温整数幂与低温分数幂描述，推广 Witten-Veneziano 关系并给出可检验的格点预测，暗示手征相变阶数依赖风味数（尤其三风味可能弱一级），并给出对称相重子数算符的构造。

Abstract: At high temperature instantons form a dilute gas, so in QCD-like theories the breaking of the anomalous $U(1)_A$ symmetry is given by integral powers of the anomalous determinant, $\sim (\det Φ)^{Q}$, where $Φ\sim \overline{q}_L q_R$ is bilinear in the quark fields, and with untwisted boundary conditions, the topological charge, $Q$, is an integer. A syncretic model is constructed, which is manifestly "beyond Landau". In the chiral limit, at temperatures above the chiral phase transition, $T > T_χ$, only integral powers of the anomalous determinant appear. Below $T_χ$, following 't Hooft et al. I assume that the topological charge $Q$ is fractional, as an integer times $1/N_c$, where $N_c$ is the number of colors. I suggest that consequently, fractional powers of the anomalous determinant appear in the chiral effective Lagrangian. For $N_f$ degenerate flavors, this generalizes the Witten-Veneziano term, valid for small $N_f/N_c$, to arbitrary $N_f/N_c$. In this model the chiral phase transition is generically of second order. The two exceptions are for one flavor, where it is probably crossover, and three flavors, where it could well be weakly first order. This can be tested in lattice QCD with $2+1$ flavors by comparing the (known) temperature dependence of the difference of the $π^a$ and $a_0^a$ propagators, to the chiral condensate of the strange quark, between $T_χ$ and $\sim 2 \, T_χ$. Analogous measurements are possible for one to four degenerate flavors about $T_χ$. Lastly, I propose an operator for baryon number in the symmetric phase.

</details>


### [240] [Three-Body Holonomy and $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00235)
*Tadashi Yoshikawa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"三体和乐"(three-body holonomy)机制，用以选择常规的 $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ 破缺方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大统一理论中，规范群破缺到标准模型群时，破缺方向（即真空取向）通常是人为设定的，缺乏动力学上的自然选择机制。作者希望构造一个"玩具机制"，说明三体系统的几何/和乐性质可以天然挑选出 $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ 这一特定方向。

Method: 利用三粒子少体系统：去掉质心坐标后，其相对空间是二维的，是能围绕"三重重合点"(triple-coincidence point)走出闭合回路的极小一维少体系统。一旦用匹配条件(matching conditions)编码两两接触，即可定义循环的三态质量算符：$M^{[k]}=M_0+2\Delta\cos((\theta_3+2\pi k)/3)$，$k=0,1,2$。通过分析该循环谱在 $\theta_3=0$ 处的分裂行为，并考察其在 trinification 型嵌入中左右扇区和乐的区别。

Result: 在 $\theta_3=0$ 时谱发生 $3\to2+1$ 分裂，诱导出的质量算符正比于 Cartan 生成元 $T_8$，因而其 commutant 为 $SU(2)\times U(1)$。在 trinification 型嵌入中，左右扇区不同的和乐留下的超荷组合，恰好复现了常规 trinification 物质分配下的标准模型超荷。

Conclusion: 该构造是一个用于选择破缺方向的玩具机制，能够自然给出 $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ 方向及正确的标准模型超荷，但秩的降低与真空的选择仍需引入额外的动力学。

Abstract: We show that a three-body holonomy can select the conventional $SU(3)\to SU(2)\times U(1)$ breaking direction. Three particles are special in the present construction: after removal of the center-of-mass coordinate they are the minimal one-dimensional few-body system with a two-dimensional relative space, allowing a closed cycle around the triple-coincidence point once pairwise contacts are encoded by matching conditions. A cyclic three-state mass operator gives \[ M^{[k]}=M_0+2Δ\cos\!\left(\frac{θ_3+2πk}{3}\right),\qquad k=0,1,2. \] At $θ_3=0$ the spectrum splits as $3\to2+1$, and the induced mass operator is proportional to the Cartan generator $T_8$. Its commutant is therefore $SU(2)\times U(1)$. In a trinification-like embedding, distinct left- and right-sector holonomies leave the hypercharge combination which reproduces the Standard-Model hypercharges for the usual trinification matter assignment. The construction is a toy mechanism for selecting the breaking direction; additional dynamics are required for rank reduction and vacuum selection.

</details>


### [241] [Full-event anomaly detection for new physics searches at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00308)
*Dimitrios Athanasakos,Sebastian Grieninger,Hongkai Liu,Tymothy Mangan,Felix Ringer,Robert Szafron*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在未来的电子-离子对撞机（EIC）上利用机器学习进行共振异常检测，使用完整事件粒子信息；结合弱监督学习与条件生成器构建背景参考，并探索无监督图自编码器，验证了全事件异常检测在EIC物理项目中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来EIC将为寻找超出标准模型的新物理提供新机遇，需要发展适用于其独特运动学特征的高灵敏度异常检测方法，以提升共振搜索的探测能力。

Method: 采用弱监督学习，基于完整事件的粒子内容（包含电子-喷注拓扑）进行共振异常检测；构建条件全事件生成器（改编自LHC喷注预训练模型）生成EIC背景事件作为学习到的背景参考；并构建全无监督图自编码器。

Result: 与高层观测量相比，电子运动学贡献了分离度的重要部分，而粒子间关联进一步提升灵敏度；对信号份额的扫描显示，使用学习到的背景参考可获得显著信号增强；无监督图自编码器在信号份额过小、弱监督失效时仍保持灵敏度，但增强幅度有限。

Conclusion: 结果支持将全事件异常检测纳入EIC物理项目，并为发展适配EIC独特运动学的方法指明了方向。

Abstract: The future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) will offer new opportunities to search for physics beyond the Standard Model. We study resonant anomaly detection using machine learning and the full particle content of an event. We consider a new neutral gauge boson and a heavy neutral lepton as illustrative signals with different electron-jet topologies. Weakly supervised learning uses this event information to enhance the sensitivity of an invariant-mass search without identifying individual signal events during training. Comparisons with high-level observables show that the electron kinematics account for an important part of the separation, while correlations among the particles provide further sensitivity. We also construct a conditional full-event generator by adapting a model pretrained on LHC jets to produce EIC background events. A scan over signal fractions demonstrates substantial signal enhancement using the learned background reference, providing a proof of concept for this approach at the EIC. Finally, we consider a fully unsupervised graph autoencoder that provides modest signal enhancement but retains sensitivity at signal fractions too small for effective weak supervision. Our results motivate full-event anomaly detection as part of the EIC physics program and identify directions for developing methods suited to its distinct kinematics.

</details>


### [242] [Primordial Black Hole and Gravitational Wave by Peaked Cosmic Perturbations from Axion Curvaton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00457)
*Keisuke Harigaya,Lian-Tao Wang,Jiangyi Yi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示轴子场在暴胀动力学中可产生峰值位于中等波数的扰动，通过curvaton机制计算其产生的引力波与PBHs谱：GW谱呈三角形，UV/IR斜率可作自由参数；PBH丰度计算中计入曲率-密度非线性关系与curvaton产生的原初非高斯性，PBHs可解释全部暗物质并伴随小尺度蓝倾等曲率扰动，GW谱IR斜率与暗物质等曲率扰动反相关，提供多信使检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轴子暴胀动力学能否产生峰值于中等波数的扰动，并系统刻画其引力波与原初黑洞的可观测信号，从而为暗物质起源及多信使检验提供新途径。

Method: 通过轴子场的暴胀动力学产生峰值扰动，并经curvaton机制计算引力波谱与原初黑洞丰度；在PBH计算中正确处理曲率扰动与密度扰动的非线性关系及curvaton产生的原初非高斯性。

Result: 引力波谱呈三角形，UV与IR斜率由轴子暴胀演化决定且可作自由参数；PBHs可占全部暗物质，并伴随小尺度蓝倾等曲率扰动；GW谱IR斜率与暗物质等曲率扰动幅度呈反相关。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了一种可由GW谱IR斜率与暗物质等曲率扰动联合检验的多信使方案，PBHs可解释全部暗物质。

Abstract: We demonstrate that an axion field can develop perturbations peaked at an intermediate wavenumber through its inflationary dynamics. We compute the resultant spectrum of gravitational waves (GWs) and primordial black holes (PBHs) generated through the curvaton mechanism. The GW spectrum exhibits a triangular shape, with UV and IR slopes determined by the axion's inflationary evolution and treatable as free parameters. In computing the PBH abundance, we properly account for the nonlinear relation between curvature and density perturbations and the primordial non-Gaussianity generated by the curvaton. The PBHs can account for all of dark matter, accompanied by blue-tilted isocurvature perturbations at small scales. The IR slope of the GW spectrum is anti-correlated with the magnitude of the dark matter isocurvature perturbations, offering a multi-messenger test of the model.

</details>


### [243] [QCD axion misalignment during reheating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00458)
*Disha Bandyopadhyay,Debasish Borah,Nayan Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在慢滚暴胀结束后延长的重加热阶段中，通过真空错位与动错位机制产生QCD轴子暗物质，针对三种微扰重加热情景进行分类分析，给出符合观测暗物质丰度及各类限制的参数空间，并探讨轴子探测与引力波实验的探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QCD轴子暗物质产生通常假设瞬时重加热，忽略了暴胀后延长的重加热阶段对轴子丰度的影响。本文旨在系统研究延长的重加热时期以及不同重加热渠道（玻色子、费米子、右手中微子）对真空错位与动错位两种机制下轴子遗迹丰度的影响，并寻找可被实验检验的参数空间。

Method: 假设暴胀前Peccei-Quinn对称性已破缺，采用重加热阶段的单项式暴胀子势，将研究划分为三种微扰重加热情景：(i) 暴胀子衰变为一对类标准模型玻色子；(ii) 衰变为一对类标准模型费米子；(iii) 仅衰变为一对重右手马约拉纳中微子，后者在经历一个中间时期主导宇宙能量密度后再衰变为标准模型末态。对每种情景分别计算真空错位与动错位机制下的QCD轴子遗迹密度，并扫描重加热参数、单项式势幂次与错位机制的组合。

Result: 针对重加热情景、单项式暴胀子势与错位机制的不同组合，确定了与观测暗物质遗迹密度一致且满足其他宇宙学与实验室限制的参数空间；同时评估了当前允许参数空间在轴子探测实验及引力波实验中的可探测性。

Conclusion: 延长的重加热阶段及不同重加热渠道会显著改变QCD轴子暗物质的产生，真空错位与动错位机制在不同情景下给出互补的参数空间，其中部分区域可被轴子探测与引力波实验检验，为未来实验探索提供了目标。

Abstract: We study the production of QCD axion dark matter (DM) via vacuum and kinetic misalignment mechanisms during an extended reheating period after the end of slow-roll inflation. With pre-inflationary Peccei-Quinn breaking and considering a monomial inflaton potential during reheating, we classify our study into three different perturbative reheating scenarios in which the inflaton decays into (i) a pair of Standard Model (SM)-like bosons, (ii) a pair of SM-like fermions, or (iii) exclusively into a pair of heavy right-handed neutrinos, which eventually decays into the SM final states after dominating the energy density of the Universe for an intermediate epoch. For each of these reheating scenarios, we study the details of QCD axion relic separately via vacuum and kinetic misalignment mechanisms. For different combinations of reheating, monomial inflaton potential and misalignment mechanisms, we identify the parameter space consistent with the observed DM relic density while satisfying other cosmological and laboratory constraints. We also check the scope of probing the currently allowed parameter space at axion-detection and gravitational wave (GW) experiments.

</details>


### [244] [ALP-Mediated Dark Matter at Low Reheating Temperature and the Belle II Excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00460)
*Joel Jones-Pérez,Luca Merlo,Javier Silva-Malpartida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 重新评估Belle II的B⁺→K⁺νν̄超出，发现标准宇宙学下仅存狭窄可行窗口，而低温柔重加热（熵注入稀释遗迹丰度）可显著改善模型与该超出的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle II报告B⁺→K⁺νν̄存在2.7σ超出，此前有研究将其解释为由类轴子粒子中介的热暗物质所致。作者希望用更完整的数据与搜索来检验该解释是否成立。

Method: 使用同一数据集的新质量微分测量，并补充此前分析遗漏的两个可见衰变搜索，重新推导对类轴子粒子参数空间的限制；进而考虑暗物质在低温柔重加热时期冻结退出、伴随熵注入稀释遗迹丰度的宇宙学情形。

Result: 新限制使标准宇宙学历史下的预言与该超出仅勉强兼容，可行参数空间被压缩为狭窄窗口；在低温柔重加热情形下，由于需要更小的耦合以匹配观测到的暗物质密度，模型与Belle II超出的兼容性显著提升。

Conclusion: 标准宇宙学解释该Belle II超出的空间十分有限，而引入低温柔重加热与熵注入的暗物质冻结机制可重新打开可行的参数空间，缓解模型与实验数据的张力。

Abstract: Belle II has reported a $2.7σ$ excess in $B^+\to K^+ν\barν$ over its Standard Model prediction, recently interpreted as arising from an axion-like particle mediating a thermal dark matter candidate. We reassess this interpretation using a new mass-differential measurement of the same data set and adding two visible-decay searches that earlier analyses omitted. The resulting bounds leave the predictions from a standard cosmological history only marginally compatible with the excess, viable in a narrow window of parameter space. Thus, we consider a situation where dark matter freezes out during a low-temperature reheating era, featuring entropy injection diluting the relic abundance. This requires smaller couplings compared to those in standard cosmology in order to match the observed dark matter density, opening a region of parameter space where the compatibility of the model with the Belle II excess is significantly improved.

</details>


### [245] [The Great Wall of the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00461)
*Yang Bai,Yiming Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究标准模型规范群整体结构 $[SU(3)_c\times SU(2)_L\times U(1)_Y]/\Gamma$ 中 $\Gamma\subseteq\mathbb{Z}_6$ 的未定性，提出热圈紧致化产生的 $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$ 离散零形式对称性自发破缺会形成宇宙畴壁，并计算其温度依赖的张力、宽度及网络演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型规范群的整体形式（即 $\Gamma\subseteq\mathbb{Z}_6$ 的选取）至今未被确定，而这一整体结构可通过宇宙学观测量进行探测；因此需要寻找能区分不同 $\Gamma$ 选择的可行宇宙学信号。

Method: 对于 $\Gamma=\mathbb{Z}_p$，SM 具有残余全局电一形式对称性 $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$；在热圈上紧致化后得到普通离散零形式对称性，其自发破缺可产生宇宙畴壁。作者计算了电弱能标以下 $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$ 畴壁的温度依赖张力与宽度（除 $\Gamma=\mathbb{Z}_6$ 外均存在），并发展了一个唯象的、含温度依赖表面张力、惯性与等离子体拖曳的热速度依赖单尺度模型来追踪其网络演化。

Result: 畴壁厚度在再电离时期附近于基准参数下可达公里尺度；对于足够早的形成，基准估计在 BBN 起始附近的壁能量分数约为 $10^{-14}$ 量级，且在更晚时期进一步被压低。

Conclusion: 若这些宏观热界面（"标准模型长城"）被产生，尽管能量分数极小，它们仍可能探测 SM 规范群的整体结构，从而为针对其宇宙学特征的专门搜寻提供动机。不确定性主要来自表面自由能与物理壁能的关联，以及晚期等离子体演化与结构形成。

Abstract: The global form of the Standard Model (SM) gauge group, $[SU(3)_c\times SU(2)_L\times U(1)_Y]/Γ$, with $Γ\subseteq\mathbb{Z}_6$, remains undetermined. For $Γ=\mathbb{Z}_p$, the SM possesses a residual global electric one-form symmetry $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$. Compactification on the thermal circle yields an ordinary discrete zero-form symmetry whose spontaneous breaking can produce cosmological domain walls. We calculate the temperature-dependent tension and width of the $\mathbb{Z}_{6/p}$ domain walls below the electroweak scale, which exist for every quotient except $Γ=\mathbb{Z}_6$, finding that their thickness can reach the kilometer scale around reionization in our benchmarks. These macroscopic thermal interfaces, if produced, would constitute the "Great Walls" of the Standard Model. We develop a phenomenological thermal velocity-dependent one-scale model to follow their network evolution, incorporating temperature-dependent surface tension, inertia, and plasma drag. For sufficiently early formation, our benchmarks give an estimated wall energy fraction of order $10^{-14}$ near the onset of Big Bang nucleosynthesis, with further suppression at later times. Uncertainties arise from relating surface free energy to physical wall energy and from late-time plasma evolution and structure formation. Despite their small energy fraction, these walls could probe the global structure of the SM gauge group, motivating dedicated searches for their cosmological signatures.

</details>


### [246] [Quantum Information in High-Energy Physics: a top subject](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00467)
*Yoav Afik,Juan Ramón Muñoz de Nova*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文综述量子信息技术在高能物理中的应用，以顶夸克为相对论量子比特范例，研究顶夸克对产生中的纠缠，并介绍量子层析实验方案及ATLAS与CMS的纠缠观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子信息领域的技市如何应用于高能物理，并以顶夸克这一相对论量子比特的典型体系为切入点，研究量子力学中最本质的特征——纠缠。

Method: 以顶夸克-反顶夸克对产生为研究对象，分析其在高能对撞机（如LHC）中的纠缠现象；提出针对顶夸克对的量子层析协议，并推动实验观测。

Result: 所提出的量子层析方案最终促成ATLAS与CMS合作组的纠缠观测，这是迄今能量最高的纠缠观测。相关概念与技术已催生出高能物理中量子信息这一广阔而丰富的研究领域。

Conclusion: 量子信息方法可有效应用于高能物理，顶夸克体系是关键范例；该研究方向已发展为一个成熟且活跃的新兴领域。

Abstract: As a work selected for the Frontiers of Science Award, we present in the Proceedings of the ICBS an overview of how the techniques from the field of Quantum Information (QI) can be implemented in High-Energy Physics (HEP), including also some novel insights. We focus on the paradigmatic case of the top quark, a drosophila of a relativistic qubit. We study the presence of entanglement, perhaps the most genuine feature of Quantum Mechanics, in top-antitop quark production in high-energy colliders such as the LHC. We also analyze our experimental proposal of a quantum tomography protocol for the top-antitop quark pair, eventually leading to the entanglement observations by the ATLAS and CMS collaborations, which constituted the highest-energy observations of entanglement ever. We discuss how the concepts and techniques developed in our work have effectively spawned an already vast and rich field of QI in HEP.

</details>


### [247] [Glueballs and fractional anomalous determinants at nonzero $θ$, and the decays of the X(2370)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00484)
*Francesco Giacosa,Shahriyar Jafarzade,Győző Kovács,Péter Kovács,Robert D. Pisarski,Fabian Rennecke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用分数反常行列式构建SU(3)规范理论中痕量与轴矢反常模型，通过标量与赝标量胶球耦合描述θ角依赖，并解释X(2370)衰变通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立能统一描述非零θ角下SU(3)规范理论痕量与轴矢反常的模型，并解释格点数据及BESIII观测到的胶球候选态衰变，提供可检验的理论预言。

Method: 采用分数反常行列式（fractional anomalous determinants），通过标量与赝标量胶球耦合构造有效模型，处理θ依赖与拓扑荷密度，并应用于纯规范理论与QCD中的胶球衰变。

Result: 在纯规范理论中复现了真空能量的θ依赖及标量胶球质量的格点结果，估计了θ=π时一级相变的拓扑荷密度跳变；在QCD中，该模型描述了BESIII测量的0^{-+}胶球候选态X(2370)的全部四个三赝标量衰变通道。

Conclusion: 测量ηηη与ηηη'衰变率可为该模型提供严格的检验。

Abstract: Using fractional anomalous determinants, we construct a model of the trace and axial anomalies of a $SU(3)$ gauge theory at nonzero $θ$ angle through the couplings to scalar and pseudoscalar glueballs. In the pure gauge theory, we reproduce the $θ$ dependence of the vacuum energy and the scalar-glueball mass from the lattice, and estimate the jump in the topological charge density for the first-order transition at $θ= π$. In QCD, the coupling of a pseudoscalar glueball to fractional anomalous determinants describes all of the four three-pseudoscalar decay channels of the $0^{-+}$ glueball candidate $X(2370)$ measured by the BESIII collaboration. Measuring the decay rates for $ηηη$ and $ηηη'$ can provide a stringent test of the model.

</details>


### [248] [A massive right-handed sterile neutrino in three-body ${\bar B}\to D^*\ell^-{\bar N}_R$ decays - a Monte Carlo simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00488)
*Bhubanjyoti Bhattacharya,Thomas E. Browder,Lucien M. Cremaldi,Horacio Crotte Ledesma,Nilakshi Das,Alakabha Datta,Tejhas Kapoor,Kumar Pandey,Alexei Sibidanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-\bar{N}_R$ 微分衰变分布，开发基于 EvtGen 的蒙特卡洛事件发生器，验证其正确性并讨论在 Belle II 上搜寻右手中微子的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重右手中微子（RHN）是超出标准模型物理的重要候选，但其在 $B$ 介子半轻子衰变中的实验搜寻需要可靠的模拟工具，目前缺乏相应的蒙特卡洛事件发生器支持。

Method: 采用新开发的、基于 EvtGen 框架、可模拟超出标准模型过程的蒙特卡洛事件发生器，对 $\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-\bar{N}_R$（$\ell=e,\mu$）的微分衰变分布进行模拟；并将 MC 结果与解析结果在 $q^2$、缺失质量等运动学变量以及 $A_{FB}$、$S_3$、$S_5$ 等角分布观测量上进行比较验证，同时讨论各观测量对 RHN 存在的敏感性和 Belle II 实验分析的可行性。

Result: MC 结果与解析结果在 $q^2$、缺失质量及 $A_{FB}$、$S_3$、$S_5$ 等角分布特征上一致，验证了该模型的实现；该发生器可支持新的 RHN 实验搜寻，并揭示了不同观测量对 RHN 的敏感性。

Conclusion: 所开发的蒙特卡洛事件发生器可为 RHN 的实验搜寻提供支持，并为在 Belle II 上开展相关实验分析奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate the differential decay distributions of $\bar{B}\to D^*\ell^-{\bar N}_R $, where $N_R$ is a heavy right-handed neutrino (RHN) and $\ell = e, μ$. We employ a newly developed Monte Carlo event generator based on the EvtGen framework that can simulate beyond-the-Standard-Model processes. We validate the implementation of the model by comparing the Monte-Carlo results with analytical results for several kinematic variables such as $q^2$ and missing mass, as well as signatures in the angular distributions such as $A_{FB}$, $S_{3}$, $S_{5}$, etc. This MC Generator will enable new experimental searches for RHNs. We discuss the sensitivity of various observables to the presence of an RHN and the feasibility of an experimental analysis at Belle II.

</details>


### [249] [Unified description of longitudinal and transverse parton dynamics in cold nuclear matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00514)
*Weiyao Ke*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文用SCET_G统一描述了冷核物质中的纵向动量演化与横动量展宽，将介质诱导能损与横动量尺度耦合，并给出SIDIS、Drell-Yan及EIC相关预言，指出单点能量关联函数中能损贡献被压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷核物质中纵向动量演化（能损）与横动量展宽通常被分开处理，缺乏统一描述；且介质诱导能损与可观测分辨的横向尺度之间的耦合（LPM干涉）需要更系统的理论框架，并需为EIC相关观测提供预言。

Method: 基于含Glauber胶子相互作用的软共线有效理论（SCET_G），结合依赖碰撞参数的能损的重整化群演化与碰撞核的BFKL型快度演化，并加入运动学约束与跑动耦合改进。

Result: 用同一组冷核物质参数可解释半单包容DIS与Drell-Yan中的主要核修正模式，并给出EIC上TMD强子多重数的预言；在DIS单点能量关联函数中，能量加权压低末态能损主导贡献，EIC运动学下剩余动力学修正较小，核修正主要由初态核部分子分布主导。

Conclusion: SCET_G框架实现了对冷核物质中纵向能损与横向展宽的统一描述，并成功刻画多种核修正现象；对单点能量关联函数而言，核修正主要来自初态核部分子分布而非末态能损动力学。

Abstract: We present a unified description of longitudinal momentum evolution and transverse-momentum broadening in cold nuclear matter using soft collinear effective theory with Glauber gluon interactions (SCET$_{\rm G}$). In a finite nucleus, Landau--Pomeranchuk--Migdal interference couples medium-induced energy loss to the transverse scale resolved by the observable. Our framework combines RG evolution of impact-parameter-dependent energy loss with BFKL-type rapidity evolution of the collision kernel, improved by kinematical constraints and running coupling. With a common set of cold-nuclear-matter parameters, it accounts for the main nuclear modification patterns in semi-inclusive DIS and Drell--Yan production and provides predictions for TMD hadron multiplicities at the EIC. We also discuss the one-point energy correlator in DIS, whose energy weighting suppresses the leading final-state energy-loss contribution. In the EIC kinematics studied, the residual dynamical corrections are small, leaving the nuclear modification dominated by the initial-state nuclear parton distributions.
