</details>


### [250] [Charged scalar portal dark matter with $U(1)_Y$ non-restoration and gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00522)
*Debasish Borah,Dhruv Ringe,Indrajit Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过引入带电单态标量Portal，可复活被直接探测实验排除的标量单态暗物质"沙漠"区域，同时引发早期宇宙超荷对称性非恢复及强一阶相变，可与轻子生成和引力波探测关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小标量单态暗物质经Higgs portal湮灭，受直接探测实验严格限制，仅在Higgs共振附近和TeV附近两个狭窄区间可行，中间存在大片"沙漠"参数空间被排除。作者希望引入新机制复活该沙漠区域，并探索其宇宙学效应。

Method: 在最小标量单态暗物质模型基础上，额外引入一个带电单态标量作为Portal。计算该扩展下暗物质湮灭与一圈级直接探测速率，并分析高温下带电标量非零真空期望值驱动的U(1)_Y超荷对称性非恢复，以及电荷破缺真空通过强一阶相变恢复为Higgs真空的过程。

Result: 带电标量Portal使原本被排除的沙漠区域重新获得可行的暗物质参数空间，同时一圈级直接探测速率仍处于当前及未来实验探测范围内。部分参数空间可实现强一阶相变，为轻子生成提供可能，且与暗物质遗迹丰度、直接探测、对称性非恢复等约束相符的大片参数空间可被LISA、BBO、DECIGO等未来引力波探测器探测。

Conclusion: 带电单态标量扩展是复活标量单态暗物质沙漠区域的可行方案，兼具对称性非恢复、强一阶相变、轻子生成和引力波信号等多重可检验预言，具有丰富的现象学前景。

Abstract: We explore the possibility of reviving scalar singlet dark matter (DM) with a minimal extension by a charged singlet scalar which also leads to hypercharge non-restoration in the early Universe. The minimal scalar singlet DM annihilating via the Higgs portal is tightly constrained by direct-detection experiments, leaving a desert between two narrow regions of the DM mass near the Higgs resonance and around a TeV. The addition of a charged scalar portal revives this desert region while keeping the one-loop direct-detection rate within reach of current and future experiments. A non-zero vacuum expectation value of the charged scalar at high temperature can also drive $U(1)_Y$ hypercharge symmetry non-restoration in the early Universe. In a subset of the parameter space, the charge-breaking minimum gets restored to the standard Higgs minimum via a strong first-order phase transition, making it a viable scenario for the generation of baryon asymmetry of the Universe via leptogenesis. A large part of the parameter space consistent with DM relic, direct-detection, symmetry non-restoration and other experimental bounds can be probed at upcoming GW detectors such as LISA, BBO and DECIGO.

</details>


### [251] [Inelastic Dark Matter from a Higher-Dimensional Operator: The LUX-ZEPLIN High-Recoil Event and Thermal Relic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00645)
*Debajyoti Choudhury,Jaydeb Das,Divya Sachdeva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出用一个含单维度-6算符的费米暗物质扩展模型，通过微小软对称破缺项实现非弹性（吸热）散射，无需精细调节即可同时解释LUX-ZEPLIN约248 keV核反冲事件与观测到的遗迹丰度，暗物质质量约在(0.2,1) TeV范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LUX-ZEPLIN(LZ)数据出现一个约248 keV的核反冲事件，暗示暗物质可能倾向于在大反冲能量处散射，作者受此激发探索相应的理论解释。

Method: 构建一个简单扩展模型：费米暗物质场通过单个维度-6算符与标准模型相互作用，并引入微小软对称破缺项以实现吸热跃迁，同时自然压低弹性散射。

Result: 在无需任何精细调节的情况下，暗物质质量处于约(0.2,1) TeV区间时，可同时解释LZ数据与观测到的遗迹密度。

Conclusion: 非弹性散射的图像为LZ的大反冲事件提供了合理且经济的解释，该单算符费米暗物质扩展模型在自然参数下具有可行性。

Abstract: The recent LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) data shows a nuclear-recoil event around $248\,\mathrm{keV}$, motivating scenarios in which dark matter (DM) preferentially scatters at large recoil energies. We investigate an inelastic scattering interpretation in terms of a simple extension with a fermionic DM field interacting with the Standard Model through a single dimension-6 operator. A tiny soft symmetry breaking term facilitates an endothermic transition while naturally suppressing elastic scatterings. Without any fine tuning, a simultaneous explanation of the LZ data and the observed relic density can be effected for a DM mass in the $\sim (0.2,1)$ TeV range.

</details>


### [252] [Neutrino trident process at a muon collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00723)
*Reinaldo Francener,Victor P. Goncalves*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在TeV级μ子对撞机中，利用μ子衰变产生的准直高能中微子束，10 kg基准中微子探测器每年可获得O(10³)例中微子三叉过程(event)，从而高精度检验标准模型并搜寻新物理；以L_μ−L_τ模型中的Z′玻色子为例，可显著扩展其当前被排除的参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeV级μ子对撞机直线段中的μ子衰变可产生高强度、高准直的高能中微子束，近期研究表明其可用于中微子物理的高精度测量。本文旨在探索利用该束流发现稀有中微子三叉(neutrino trident)过程的潜力，并评估其对标准模型检验与新物理搜寻，特别是对轻规范玻色子的探测能力。

Method: 中微子三叉过程特征在于中微子携带的弱玻色子与靶核库仑场中的光子发生融合。作者计算了μ子对撞机中微子束在该过程上的产率，针对末态不同味道的带电轻子对进行配置，采用10 kg基准中微子探测器估算年事例数；并以L_μ−L_τ模型预言的新规范玻色子Z′为例，推导其对Z′参数的预期灵敏度。

Result: 对于末态不同味道带电轻子对的各种配置，10 kg基准中微子探测器每年可获得O(10³)量级的中微子三叉事例率。基于Z′的灵敏度分析表明，μ子对撞机处的中微子探测器将显著扩展当前Z′被排除的参数空间。

Conclusion: μ子对撞机配合远前方中微子探测器可提供高产率的中微子三叉过程，能够实现标准模型的高精度检验并具备搜寻新物理的潜力；对L_μ−L_τ模型中Z′的预期灵敏度优于现有实验约束，显示该方案在轻规范玻色子探测上的重要前景。

Abstract: A TeV-scale muon collider enables the production of a highly collimated high-energy neutrino flux by muon decay in the straight sections of the collider ring. Recent studies have demonstrated that these neutrinos can be used for high-precision measurements in neutrino physics with a dedicated far-forward neutrino detector. In this study, we investigate the potentiality of the muon collider to discover the rare neutrino trident process, which is characterized by the fusion between a weak boson from the neutrino and a photon from the Coulomb field of the nucleus target. Our results indicate large neutrino trident rates per year [$\mathcal{O}(10^3)$] in a baseline 10 kg neutrino detector for a configuration of different flavour pairs of charged leptons in the final state, which will allow high-precision tests of the Standard Model and searches for New Physics. As a first example, we investigate the impact of the a new gauge boson $Z'$, as predicted by the $L_μ- L_τ$ model, and derive the projected sensitivity to this new gauge boson. Our findings show that a neutrino detector at a muon collider will significantly extend the current excluded regions of the parameter space.

</details>


### [253] [Systematic Bayesian investigation of the (2+1)-flavor QCD phase transition in a holographic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01104)
*Liqiang Zhu,Xun Chen,Kai Zhou,Hanzhong Zhang,Mei Huang,Enke Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究构建了一个贝叶斯全息QCD模型，将EMD框架与零化学势下的格点QCD数据（熵、声速平方、重子数磁化率）及误差估计相结合，利用贝叶斯推断校准参数，预测QCD相图中临界终点（CEP）的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极端条件下强相互作用物质相变的理论研究中，确定QCD相图临界终点（CEP）的位置是关键问题。传统全息模型参数标定不够精确，缺乏对格点QCD误差信息的系统利用，因此需要一种能够结合格点数据及其误差、实现精确参数校准的新框架。

Method: 构建贝叶斯全息QCD模型：将Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton（EMD）框架与零化学势下的格点QCD数据（熵、声速平方、重子数磁化率）结合，系统纳入LQCD结果的误差估计；采用贝叶斯推断框架对模型参数进行精确校准，进而研究零及有限化学势下(2+1)味QCD的热力学性质，并预测CEP位置。

Result: 在最大后验（MAP）估计下，CEP位于(T, μ)_MAP = (0.119, 0.615) GeV。68%置信水平的预测区间为(T, μ)_68%CL = (0.1176–0.1204, 0.59–0.63) GeV；95%置信水平为(T, μ)_95%CL = (0.1172–0.1206, 0.58–0.63) GeV。与其他理论模型预测的对比验证了方法的稳健性和预测能力。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了全息建模的新型分析框架，为极端条件下强相互作用物质相变提供了有价值的理论见解，并验证了贝叶斯全息QCD方法在预测CEP位置上的稳健性与预测能力。

Abstract: In this study, we construct a bayesian holographic QCD model by integrating the Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton (EMD) framework with lattice quantum chromodynamics (QCD) data at zero chemical potential, specifically entropy, the square of the speed of sound, and baryon number susceptibilities, while systematically incorporating error estimates from the lattice QCD (LQCD) results. Leveraging a bayesian inference framework, we first achieve a precise calibration of the model parameters, then perform a comprehensive investigation into the thermodynamic properties of \((2+1)\)-flavor QCD at both zero and finite chemical potentials, and finally provide a prediction for the location of the critical end point (CEP) in the QCD phase diagram. Our results indicate that, under the maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation, the CEP is located at $(T, μ)_{\mathrm{MAP}} = (0.119, 0.615)\mathrm{GeV}$. Furthermore, we provide the predicted regions for the CEP at $68\%$ and $95\%$ confidence levels (CL), yielding $(T, μ)_{68\% \mathrm{CL}} = (0.1176\text{--}0.1204,0.59\text{--}0.63)\mathrm{GeV}$ and $(T, μ)_{95\% \mathrm{CL}} = (0.1172\text{--}0.1206,0.58\text{--}0.63)\mathrm{GeV}$, respectively. A thorough comparison with predictions from other theoretical models validates the robustness and predictive power of our approach. This work not only establishes a novel analytical framework for holographic modeling but also provides valuable theoretical insights into the phase transitions of strongly interacting matter under extreme conditions.

</details>


### [254] [Generalized fluctuation-dissipation theorem and Einstein relation in rotating equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01797)
*Shuo Fang,Shi Pu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过精确的相空间KMS关系，推导出旋转热平衡下矢量场的广义涨落耗散定理，揭示了旋转诱导的张量贡献、新型爱因斯坦关系及其对重夸克偶素轨道极化的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转量子物质在多个物理领域受到关注，但缺乏描述旋转热平衡下矢量场涨落与耗散之间关系的微观普适约束，需要建立模型无关的理论框架。

Method: 从精确的相空间Kubo-Martin-Schwinger（KMS）关系出发，推导旋转热平衡中矢量场的广义涨落耗散定理（FDT）。在局域输运极限下，导出含旋转的广义爱因斯坦关系，并在热涡旋二阶展开下分析对称动量扩散与耗散拖曳的联系。同时考察谱函数零模与平衡细节平衡关系。

Result: 所得FDT包含超出标量热因子的旋转诱导张量贡献；广义爱因斯坦关系中对称动量扩散依赖超出耗散拖曳所编码的低频谱信息；发现联系谱函数零模与非旋转拖曳系数的新爱因斯坦型关系，该零模构成重夸克偶素轨道极化的新机制；修正的细节平衡关系其一阶涡旋修正由跃迁极化支配，且不显式依赖浴模型。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了研究旋转量子物质的微观平衡约束，为多个领域中旋转量子物质的研究提供了普适的理论工具。

Abstract: We derive a generalized fluctuation-dissipation theorem (FDT) for vector fields in rotating thermal equilibrium from an exact phase-space Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) relation. The resulting FDT contains rotation-induced tensorial contributions beyond a scalar thermal factor. In the local transport limit, we obtain a model-independent generalized Einstein relation with rotation in which, through second order in thermal vorticity, symmetric momentum diffusion depends on low-frequency spectral information beyond that encoded in the rotation-dependent dissipative drag. Remarkably, we find a new Einstein-type relation linking zero modes in the spectral function to the nonrotating drag coefficient. These zero modes underlie a new mechanism for the orbital polarization of heavy quarkonium. The same FDT constraint yields a modified detailed-balance relation whose first-order vortical correction is governed by transition polarization, without explicit dependence on the bath model. Our findings establish microscopic equilibrium constraints for studying rotating quantum matter in various fields.

</details>


### [255] [Analyticity Bootstrap of Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01154)
*Xiang Li,Dao-Ming Mu,Yan-Qing Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出解析性自举方法确定费曼积分约化：利用泰勒展开区域的有限性约束约化系数，结合少量IBP采样或渐近展开确定全部参数，系统发展单变量与多变量情形，并揭示微分方程与解析性约束的双向关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费曼积分的约化系数通常是运动学变量的有理函数，传统上完全依赖积分分部（IBP）方法求解，计算成本高。作者观察到维度正规化的费曼积分在任意运动学点存在泰勒展开区域（解析区域），该区域的有限性会对约化系数施加强约束，因此希望利用这一解析性质来低成本甚至无需IBP地获得完整约化。

Method: 核心是解析性自举：写出与奇点结构相符的最一般拟设（ansatz），再用少量IBP采样或渐近展开固定剩余参数，从而得到完整约化。方法在单变量和多变量情形下被系统发展，并给出多个显式例子。同时分析微分方程（DE）与解析性约束的相互关系。

Result: 证明了主积分的微分方程决定了所有解析性约束；反之这些约束也有助于构造微分方程。特别地，辅助质量流方法中的微分方程可以显著降低计算成本地构造，甚至可以完全不用IBP而完全确定。

Conclusion: 解析性自举提供了一种确定费曼积分约化的新途径，将泰勒区域有限性转化为对约化系数的有效约束，减少对IBP的依赖，并建立了微分方程与解析性约束之间的等价/互促联系，在辅助质量流方法中有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We propose an analyticity bootstrap method to determine the reduction of Feynman integrals (FIs). The key observation is that, at any kinematic point, a dimensionally regularized FI possesses a Taylor expansion region (analytic region), and the finiteness of the Taylor region strongly constrains the integral reduction coefficients, which are rational functions of the kinematic variables. By writing the most general ansatz compatible with the singularity structure and then fixing the remaining parameters with only a few integration-by-parts (IBP) samples or asymptotic expansions, one can obtain the full reduction. We develop the method systematically in both single-variable and multi-variable cases, with many explicit examples. Interestingly, the differential equations (DEs) of the master integrals determine all these analyticity constraints and, conversely, the constraints facilitate the construction of the DEs. In particular, we show that the DEs in the auxiliary mass flow method can be constructed at significantly reduced cost and can even be fully determined without using IBP.

</details>


### [256] [Parton-shower and fixed-order QCD effects in Higgs boson production in weak-boson fusion and its decays to bottom quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01347)
*Arnd Behring*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究了高阶QCD修正对弱玻色子融合产生希格斯玻色子并衰变为底夸克对的影响，发现固定阶计算在真实跃迁相空间切割下修正很大，而采用部分子簇射匹配描述可改善微扰收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱玻色子融合产生希格斯并衰变到底夸克对是重要的测量道，其高阶QCD修正与相空间切割及衰变子过程的辐射修正存在复杂关联，需要厘清固定阶计算的可靠性与收敛性。

Method: 采用固定阶高阶QCD修正计算，考虑现实的fiducial切割；并进一步将希格斯衰变子过程与部分子簇射进行匹配处理，比较两种描述下的修正大小与微扰收敛性。

Result: 在固定阶计算中观察到较大修正，来源于所选取的fiducial切割与衰变子过程辐射修正之间的相互影响；切换到部分子簇射匹配描述后，在最低阶即能捕捉到相当一部分修正，并改善了计算的微扰收敛性。

Conclusion: 部分子簇射匹配的衰变描述能更有效地包含重要修正并提升微扰稳定性，对弱玻色子融合希格斯产生及底夸克对衰变的精确计算具有意义。

Abstract: We study the impact of higher-order QCD corrections on Higgs production via weak-boson fusion with subsequent decays of the Higgs boson to bottom-quark pairs. We consider realistic fiducial cuts and observe large corrections in a fixed-order calculation. They arise from the interplay of the particular fiducial cuts considered and the radiative corrections to the Higgs decay subprocess. Switching to a parton-shower-matched description of the decay captures a significant portion of the corrections already at the lowest order and improves the perturbative convergence of the calculation.

</details>


### [257] [Gravitational D-form factors of the nucleon and pion with mixing between two-quark and four-quark states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01396)
*Mamiya Kawaguchi,Yao Ma,Yong-Liang Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在包含两夸克与四夸克态及核子场的扩展线性σ模型(eLSM)中研究核子与π介子的引力D形状因子，发现介子混合压低核子D形状因子并与格点QCD一致，且该效应与手征不变质量效应难以区分；π介子D形状因子可作为区分二者、深入理解核子质量分解与介子夸克组成的补充探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核子D形状因子携带核子质量分解的关键信息，而格点QCD结果与手征有效模型之间存在差异。既有研究在宇称双重态模型(PDM)中发现核子质量的手征不变成分会压低D形状因子，但其与介子混合等机制的区分性尚不明确，因此需要寻找能够区分不同物理机制的观测量。

Method: 采用包含两夸克态、四夸克态以及核子场的扩展线性σ模型(eLSM)，在标量介子主导图像下计算核子与π介子的引力D形状因子；考察两夸克与四夸克态之间的介子混合效应，并与宇称双重态模型(PDM)的结果进行对比；进一步在手征极限下分析π介子D形状因子对介子混合与手征不变质量的依赖。

Result: 介子混合压低核子D形状因子，使eLSM结果与格点QCD一致；该压低压低效果与PDM中手征不变质量引起的压低在数量上相似，说明仅由核子D形状因子难以区分这两种机制。π介子D形状因子在前向极限下于eLSM与PDM中均固定为-1，但介子混合会改变其对动量转移的依赖，而手征不变质量不直接影响π介子D形状因子。

Conclusion: 核子D形状因子单独无法区分介子混合与手征不变质量的贡献；结合核子与π介子D形状因子的联合分析，可为核子质量分解及介子夸克组成提供新的判别依据。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational $D$-form factors of the nucleon and pion within an extended linear sigma model (eLSM) that incorporates both two-quark and four-quark states, as well as nucleon fields. Within the scalar-meson-dominance picture, we find that meson mixing between two-quark and four-quark states suppresses the nucleon $D$-form factor and leads to agreement with the lattice-QCD results. Interestingly, a quantitatively similar suppression was previously found in the parity doublet model (PDM) as a consequence of a nonzero chiral invariant component of the nucleon mass. This suggests that the effects of meson mixing and the chiral invariant mass are difficult to distinguish from the nucleon $D$-form factor alone. We therefore examine the pion $D$-form factor in the chiral limit. While its value in the forward limit remains fixed at $-1$ in both the eLSM and the PDM, meson mixing modifies its momentum-transfer dependence. In contrast, the chiral invariant mass does not directly affect the pion $D$-form factor. These results suggest that a combined analysis of the nucleon and pion $D$-form factors can provide further insight into the mass decomposition of the nucleon and the quark composition of mesons.

</details>


### [258] [Probing an up-phobic Higgs at colliders: the role of $b$-associated production in the democratic 3HDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01412)
*Dipankar Das,Teesha Khandelwal,Ipsita Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在常规重希格斯产生机制被压低的up-phobic极限下，b-associated产生成为主导机制；民主三希格斯二重态模型（3HDM）为这一极限提供了可行的BSM框架，现有b-associated搜索给出最敏感约束，未来对撞机搜索可进一步探测其参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规重希格斯产生机制在某些理论上有良好动机的希格斯部门中可能被压低，此时需要识别替代的主导产生道，并寻找能够实现该极限的具体BSM模型，以指导LHC及未来对撞机的实验探测。

Method: 提出并证明存在理论上有充分动机的希格斯部门，使常规重希格斯产生机制被抑制，从而由b-associated产生道主导；以民主三希格斯二重态模型（3HDM）作为可实现该极限的BSM框架，分析其up-phobic极限下的现象学，并利用现有b-associated搜索数据进行约束，同时给出未来对撞机搜索的投影。

Result: 在民主3HDM的up-phobic极限下，现有的b-associated搜索是LHC对该情景最敏感的探针，并由此导出了对模型参数空间的相应约束；未来对撞机搜索的投影表明其有潜力探测该模型up-phobic参数空间的更多区域。

Conclusion: 民主3HDM是实现常规重希格斯产生被压低、b-associated产生主导这一极限的可行BSM框架；b-associated搜索是当前最敏感的LHC探针并已给出参数空间约束，未来对撞机可进一步拓展对该模型up-phobic参数空间的探测范围。

Abstract: We show that there exists a theoretically well-motivated Higgs sector in which the conventional heavy-Higgs production mechanisms are suppressed. In this limit, the $b$-associated production channel assumes the role of the primary production mechanism. We demonstrate that the democratic three Higgs-doublet model (3HDM) provides a viable beyond the SM (BSM) framework in which this limit can be realized. We further show that existing $b$-associated searches provide the most sensitive LHC probe of this 3HDM scenario in the up-phobic limit and derive the corresponding constraints on its parameter space. We also present projections for future collider searches, demonstrating their potential to probe further regions of the model's up-phobic parameter space.

</details>


### [259] [Mechanisms of $D \bar{D}$ pair production in $e^{+} e^{-} \to e^{+} e^{-} D \bar{D}$ and $AA \to AA D \bar{D}$ reactions and production of exotic charmonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01420)
*Antoni Szczurek,Izabela Babiarz,Piotr Lebiedowicz,Wolfgang Schäfer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 $e^+e^-$ 碰撞中 $D\bar D$ 对产生机制，论证 Belle/BaBar 观测到的 3.8 GeV 宽鼓包源于连续谱而非 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振，并给出 $\chi_{c2}(3930)\to D\bar D$ 分支比及 Belle II、LHC 超周边碰撞的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle 与 BaBar 在 $M_{D^0\bar D^0}=3.8$ GeV 处观测到宽鼓包，其起源（连续谱 vs. $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振）以及 $\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 的性质和衰变分支比尚不明确，需要理论机制分析加以判别。

Method: 考虑 $D^0\bar D^0$ 连续谱机制中的 $t/u$ 道 $D^*$ 矢量介子交换，耦合常数由 $D^*\to D\gamma$ 衰变导出；$D^+D^-$ 道主要考虑 $D^\pm$ 交换。同时研究 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$、$\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 的共振产生，并将模型结果与 $\Gamma(\chi_{c2}(2P)\to\gamma\gamma)=0.544$ keV（Buchmüller-Tye 势）及 BaBar 数据对比。

Result: $D^0\bar D^0$ 道的连续谱贡献相对较大，$D^+D^-$ 道显著较小，表明 3.8 GeV 宽鼓包为连续谱起源。$\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 是首个激发 $P$ 波粲偶素 $\chi_{c2}(2P)$ 的良好候选，拟合得到 $B(\chi_{c2}(3930)\to D\bar D)=0.58\pm0.13$。给出了 Belle II 运动学下多个变量的微分分布结果。

Conclusion: 3.8 GeV 宽鼓包应解释为连续谱效应而非 $\chi_{c0}(3860)$ 共振；$\chi_{c2}(3930)$ 可识别为 $\chi_{c2}(2P)$，其 $D\bar D$ 分支比已确定。文末还简述了 LHC 上低不变质量（$M_{D\bar D}<4.3$ GeV）超周边重离子反应 $PbPb\to PbPb D\bar D$ 的预测。

Abstract: We discuss mechanisms of $D \bar{D}$ pair production in $e^+ e^-$ collisions. We consider the $D^0 {\bar D}^0$-continuum mechanism with the $t/u$-channel $D^*$ vector-meson exchanges, where the $D^* D γ$ coupling constants are derived from the $D^* \to D γ$ decays. For the $D^+ D^-$ channel, we consider mainly the $D^{\pm}$ exchanges. We find a relatively large continuum contribution for the $D^0 {\bar D}^0$ channel and a significantly smaller one for $D^+ D^-$, concluding that the broad bump at $M_{D^0 {\bar D}^0} = 3.8$ GeV observed by the Belle and BaBar Collaborations has a continuum origin rather than corresponding to the broad $χ_{c0}(3860)$ resonance. We also investigate the resonant production of $D \bar{D}$ pairs, including $χ_{c0}(3860)$ and $χ_{c2}(3930)$. The $χ_{c2}(3930)$ meson is a good candidate for the first excited $P$-wave charmonium $χ_{c2}(2P)$ state. This resonance can decay into both $D \bar{D}$ channels; however, its branching fractions are not well known at present. Comparing our model result with $Γ(χ_{c2}(2P) \to γγ) = 0.544$ keV (corresponding to the Buchmüller-Tye potential) to the BaBar data, we find $B(χ_{c2}(3930) \to D \bar{D}) = 0.58 \pm 0.13$. We present results for differential distributions in several variables for Belle II kinematics. Finally, we shortly mention predictions for the ultraperipheral heavy-ion reaction $Pb Pb \to Pb Pb D \bar{D}$ for low invariant mass ($M_{D \bar{D}} < 4.3$ GeV) at the LHC.

</details>


### [260] [Radiative corrections to the subprocess of annihilation of quark-antiquark pair of prompt photon production in proton-proton collisions at NICA energies $\sqrt{\mathbf{s}}$= 10 GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01552)
*Mohsun R. Alizada,Azar I. Ahmadov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文对NICA能区质子-质子碰撞中即时光子产生的夸克-反夸克湮灭过程开展了量子色动力学与量子电动力学辐射修正的理论与数值研究，解析计算与PYTHIA模拟结果在中心运动学区域高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即时光子产生是探测质子内部结构与部分子动力学的关键过程，而低碰撞能量与小横动量区域的辐射修正不可忽略；NICA能区(√s = 10 GeV)缺乏系统的辐射修正定量评估，亟需明确QCD与QED修正对截面及双自旋不对称度的影响。

Method: 针对q q̄ → gγ的湮灭过程，系统研究三类辐射修正过程：1. q q̄ → q q̄γ、2. q q̄ → ggγ、3. q q̄ → gγγ。利用FeynCalc进行解析计算，获得微分截面与双自旋不对称度A_LL，并在PYTHIA 8.316中对结果进行模拟验证，同时考察质子极化的影响。

Result: 辐射修正在低√s和小光子横动量p_T处显著。积分截面满足σ(ggγ) > σ(q q̄γ) > σ(gγγ)；而微分截面关系相反，为dσ(q q̄γ)/dp_T² > dσ(gγγ)/dp_T² > dσ(ggγ)/dp_T²。质子极化对三个过程影响近似相同，且上述关系依然成立。FeynCalc的严格领头阶解析计算与PYTHIA 8.316模拟在中心运动学区域吻合极好。

Conclusion: 该工作定量刻画了NICA能区即时光子产生中QCD/QED辐射修正的贡献，揭示了不同末态过程的截面层级关系及其在极化条件下的稳定性，验证了解析方法与蒙特卡洛模拟的一致性，为低能区即时光子极化测量提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, a comprehensive theoretical and numerical study of the quantum-chromodynamic and quantum-electrodynamic radiative corrections: 1. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ$, 2. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ$, and 3. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ$ to the annihilation of a quark-antiquark pair $q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγ$ of prompt photon production in proton-proton collisions at NICA energies $\sqrt{s}$ = 10 GeV was performed. The differential cross-sections and double spin asymmetries $A_{LL}$ of the radiative corrections were investigated analytically using the FeynCalc and modulated in PYTHIA 8.316. Radiative corrections are significant at low collision energies of protons $\sqrt{s}$ and small transverse momentum of photons $p_{T}$. It was shown that radiative corrections to the annihilation of a quark-antiquark pair process satisfy the following relations:
  $$ 2. σ_{\sqrt{s}}(q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ) > 1. σ_{\sqrt{s}}(q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ) > 3. σ_{\sqrt{s}}(q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ) $$ $$ 1. \frac{dσ(q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ)}{dp_{T}^{2}} >
  3.
  \frac{dσ(q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ)}{dp_{T}^{2}} >
  2. \frac{dσ(q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ)}{dp_{T}^{2}} $$
  The polarization of colliding protons has almost the same effect on processes 1. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow q\bar{q}γ$, 2. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow ggγ$, and 3. $q\bar{q} \rightarrow gγγ$ and in this case above indicated relation is also satisfied.
  A direct comparison between strict leading order analytical calculations in FeynCalc and PYTHIA 8.316 modulations revealed excellent agreement in the central kinematic regions.

</details>


### [261] [From the LZ Event to Grand Unification: Heavy Higgsinos and Proton Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01613)
*Shihwen Hor,Natsumi Nagata,Tsutomu T. Yanagida*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将LZ事件暗示的重希格斯诺暗物质（质量~10^5 GeV）置于最小超对称SU(5)框架下，证明考虑GUT标度阈值修正后规范耦合统一与质子衰变约束可以兼容，并预言p→K⁺ν̄寿命落在下一代实验探测范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ事件重新激发了对非弹性希格斯诺暗物质的兴趣。弱标度希格斯诺虽能简单解释该事件，但与IceCube基于太阳中暗物质俘获与湮灭给出的限制相冲突。规避该矛盾需要更重的希格斯诺（约10^5 GeV量级，中性态质量劈裂约400 keV），这又指向约10^7 GeV的gaugino质量。如此重的超对称谱通常被认为不利于超对称大统一理论，因为会恶化规范耦合统一、降低统一标度，并可能导致过快的质子衰变。本文即重新审视这一传统预期。

Method: 在最小超对称SU(5)模型中重新分析规范耦合统一条件，重点纳入GUT标度的阈值修正。考察伴随希格斯自耦合较小以及gluino显著重于wino的参数区域，分析SU(5)规范玻色子质量、色三重希格斯质量以及由此决定的维度六与维度五质子衰变寿命。

Result: 当伴随希格斯自耦合较小时，统一条件允许SU(5)规范玻色子保持足够重，从而压低维度六质子衰变。同时色三重希格斯质量可维持在传统GUT标度附近，M_H_C~10^16 GeV，尤其在gluino显著重于wino时。在该区域，维度五衰变模式p→K⁺ν̄的寿命可落在下一代质子衰变实验（Hyper-Kamiokande、JUNO、DUNE）的探测范围内。

Conclusion: 重希格斯诺暗物质所需的超重超对称谱并不必然与最小超对称SU(5)大统一相矛盾；考虑阈值修正后，统一条件、质子衰变约束与暗物质解释可以共存，并给出可被下一代实验检验的p→K⁺ν̄预言。文中还讨论了能够产生所需超对称质量谱的超对称破缺情景。

Abstract: The recently reported LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) event has renewed interest in inelastic Higgsino dark matter. While a weak-scale Higgsino provides a simple interpretation of the event, such a scenario is in tension with the IceCube bound from dark-matter capture and annihilation in the Sun. This tension can be avoided for a much heavier Higgsino, with a mass of order $10^5$ GeV and a neutral-state mass splitting of $\simeq 400$ keV, which in turn points to gaugino masses around $10^7$ GeV. Such a heavy supersymmetric spectrum is often thought to be unfavorable for supersymmetric grand unified theories (GUTs), since it tends to worsen gauge coupling unification and lower the unification scale, potentially leading to excessively rapid proton decay. In this work, we revisit this expectation in minimal supersymmetric SU(5), taking into account the GUT-scale threshold corrections. We show that the resulting unification conditions allow the SU(5) gauge bosons to remain sufficiently heavy when the adjoint-Higgs self-coupling is small, thereby suppressing dimension-six proton decay. At the same time, the color-triplet Higgs mass can remain near the conventional GUT scale, $M_{H_C}\sim 10^{16}$ GeV, particularly when the gluino is significantly heavier than the wino. Remarkably, in this region the dimension-five decay mode $p\to K^+\barν$ can have a lifetime within the reach of next-generation proton-decay searches, including Hyper-Kamiokande, JUNO, and DUNE. We also discuss supersymmetry-breaking scenarios that can give rise to the required supersymmetric mass spectrum.

</details>


### [262] [Overlapping Subcritical Bubbles: Free Energy and Lifetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01629)
*Guangshang Chen,Yang Xiao,Jin Min Yang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 亚临界气泡的空间重叠会降低自由能代价并延长其寿命，从而产生集体效应，应在亚临界气泡群体的动力学描述中加以考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱一阶相变期间可形成大量亚临界气泡，但通常被当作孤立涨落处理，忽略了相邻气泡之间的空间重叠是否会影响其演化这一问题。

Method: 结合复合高斯剖面的解析自由能计算与重叠构型的Langevin模拟。

Result: 重叠会降低自由能代价，并总体延长亚临界气泡的寿命；增强的持续存在性可能反过来影响其丰度。

Conclusion: 气泡重叠可产生集体效应，应被纳入亚临界气泡群体的动力学描述中。

Abstract: Subcritical bubbles can form an appreciable population during weak first-order phase transitions, but are usually treated as isolated fluctuations. This raises the question of whether spatial overlap between neighboring subcritical bubbles can modify their evolution. We address this question by combining analytic free-energy calculations for composite Gaussian profiles with Langevin simulations of overlapping configurations. We find that the overlap lowers the free-energy cost and generally increases the lifetime of subcritical bubbles, with the enhanced persistence potentially feeding back on their abundance. Such overlap can therefore generate collective effects and should be incorporated into kinetic descriptions of subcritical-bubble populations.

</details>


### [263] [Spin relaxation of heavy quarks from momentum diffusion in the quark-gluon plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01791)
*Ankit Kumar,Sourav Dey,Vinod Chandra,Amaresh Jaiswal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立随机自旋-动量耦合框架，由Thomas进动将重夸克动量扩散与自旋弛豫联系起来，导出耦合Fokker-Planck方程并给出τ_s与κ的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克自旋动力学可作为探测夸克-胶子等离子体中重夸克与介质相互作用的潜在探针，需要建立自旋弛豫与动量输运系数之间的理论联系。

Method: 从Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi方程与重夸克动量的Langevin方程出发，构建随机框架：引起动量扩散的随机动量kick同时通过Thomas进动导致自旋转动。推导耦合自旋-动量Fokker-Planck方程；在重夸克极限下（动量比自旋更快趋于平衡）积分掉动量自由度，得到自旋取向的旋转Fokker-Planck方程，从而将τ_s与κ直接关联。

Result: 非相对论极限下得到 τ_s=2τ_p(m_Q/T)^2（τ_p为动量弛豫时间）。结合格点QCD、强耦合估计、T-matrix计算和重味唯象学给出的κ值，得到粲夸克与底夸克的Thomas进动对自旋弛豫时间的贡献。相对论运动学修正仅在重离子碰撞相关温区带来有限改变，不确定度主要由κ主导。

Conclusion: 该结果给出重夸克自旋弛豫中的Thomas进动贡献，并明确与色磁场直接耦合构成额外的自旋弛豫通道。

Abstract: Heavy-quark spin dynamics in the quark-gluon plasma provides a potential probe of the interaction between heavy quarks and the medium. We develop a stochastic framework in which the random momentum kicks responsible for heavy-quark diffusion also induce spin rotation through Thomas precession. Starting from the Thomas-Bargmann-Michel-Telegdi equation and a Langevin equation for heavy-quark momentum, we derive a novel coupled spin-momentum Fokker-Planck equation. In the heavy-quark limit, where momentum equilibrates faster than spin, the momentum degrees of freedom can be integrated out, yielding a rotational Fokker-Planck equation for the spin orientation. This establishes a direct relation between the Thomas precession contribution to the spin-relaxation time $τ_{s}$ and the heavy-quark momentum-diffusion coefficient $κ$. In the non-relativistic limit, we find $τ_s=2τ_p(m_Q/T)^2$, with $τ_p$ being the momentum relaxation time. Using recent determinations of $κ$ from lattice QCD, strong-coupling estimates, $T$-matrix calculations, and heavy-flavor phenomenology, we obtain the corresponding Thomas precession contribution to spin relaxation times for charm and bottom quarks. Relativistic kinematics modify the result by only a modest amount over the temperature range relevant for heavy-ion collisions, while the uncertainty is dominated by that of $κ$. We emphasize that the result represents the Thomas precession contribution to heavy-quark spin relaxation; direct coupling to the chromomagnetic field provides an additional channel.

</details>


### [264] [Probing the neutrino mass through semileptonic decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01808)
*Claire Chevallier*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过分析半轻子衰变中与前后不对称性相关的观测量，可检验有质量中微子的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种可区分无质量中微子与有质量中微子的实验判据。该文指出半轻子衰变的细致分析能提供这样的检验手段。

Method: 选取与前后不对称性相关的关键观测量：对无质量中微子其值严格为零；若中性轻子较重且通过左手算符与标准模型场相互作用则非零。对右手相互作用，即使中微子无质量该量也显著非零。以赝标量介子衰变为另一赝标量介子为例进行显式验证。

Result: 该关键观测量在无质量情形下为零，而在中微子有质量（左手算符）或存在右手相互作用时变为非零，从而可作为区分性信号。

Conclusion: 半轻子衰变中前后不对称性相关观测量是检验有质量中微子及左右手相互作用结构的有效探针。

Abstract: We argue that a detailed analysis of semileptonic decays can test the possibility of a massive neutrino. The key observable, related to the forward-backward asymmetry, is exactly zero for a massless neutrino but becomes non-zero if the neutral lepton is heavy and interacts with Standard Model fields via left-handed operators. For right-handed interactions, this quantity differs significantly from zero even for a massless right-handed neutrino. We demonstrate this explicitly using the example of a pseudoscalar meson decaying into another pseudoscalar meson.

</details>


### [265] [Near Threshold Kaluza-Klein Graviton Decays and the Dark Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01825)
*Kevin Langhoff*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 近阈值的KK引力子intra-tower衰变是纯d波，速率Γ∝p^5，而非暗维度文献中假设的s波Γ∝p；平坦暗维度中KK引力子不能作为暗物质，并由此给出再加热温度上限及对额外维度半径的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗维度文献通常假设KK引力子的intra-tower衰变为s波（Γ∝p）以论证其作为暗物质的可行性；本文旨在检验该近阈值衰变行为的正确性及其对KK引力子暗物质场景的影响。

Method: 分析Kaluza-Klein引力子近阈值intra-tower衰变的波函数或角动量性质，推导衰变速率随衰变产物动量p的标度关系，并结合冻结产生（freeze-in）丰度、MeV伽马射线观测、大爆炸核合成与宇宙微波背景对再加热温度的限制进行分析。

Result: 近阈值intra-tower衰变为纯d波，速率Γ∝p^5而非Γ∝p；在平坦暗维度中KK引力子被排除作为暗物质候选。即使假设其他暗物质来源，KK引力子的不可约冻结产生丰度会产生MeV伽马射线，从而给出再加热温度的上限。结合大爆炸核合成和宇宙微波背景给出的再加热温度下界，要求额外维度半径比距离猜想所倾向于的数值小近三个数量级，R∼Λ^{-1/4}。

Conclusion: KK引力子近阈值衰变的d波特性排除了其在平坦暗维度中作为暗物质的可能，并对再加热温度和额外维度半径施加了严格约束，与距离猜想的朴素预期存在显著张力。

Abstract: Near threshold, intra-tower decays of Kaluza-Klein (KK) gravitons are pure $d$-wave, with rate $Γ\propto p^5$ in the decay products momentum $p$, rather than the $s$-wave $Γ\propto p$ assumed in the dark dimension literature. In a flat dark dimension this excludes KK gravitons as a dark matter candidate. Even if some other source of dark matter is assumed, the irreducible freeze-in abundance of KK gravitons produce MeV gamma rays which allows one to set upper limits on the reheating temperature; additionally using the lower bound on the reheating temperature from big-bang nucleosynthesis and the cosmic microwave background requires the radius of the extra dimension to be almost three orders of magnitude smaller than the distance conjecture would naively prefer, $R\sim Λ^{-1/4}$.

</details>


### [266] [An optimal strategy for experimental searches in high-mass dilepton production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01965)
*J. Alcaraz Maestre,O. M. Carretero*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于NLO精度重加权的方法，用于研究LHC高不变质量双轻子产生中的新物理效应，仅需标准模型pp→V(*)→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻样本即可适用于多种接触相互作用与s道中性共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高不变质量双轻子产生中寻找新物理时，传统方法需要针对每种新物理假设（不同接触相互作用结构、耦合，或不同共振质量、耦合、宽度）重新生成大量模拟样本，计算成本高昂。因此需要一种通用且高效的方法，能够复用同一初始样本覆盖广泛的新物理情景。

Method: 采用重加权（reweighting）程序，其精度达到量子色动力学次领头阶（NLO）。唯一计算代价较高的输入是一个标准模型pp→V(*)→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻样本（V=Z、γ*），该样本需以相同精度生成，并在高不变质量相空间具有足够的统计覆盖。基于该样本，通过重加权可任意改变接触相互作用的底层结构与耦合，或调整s道中性共振的质量、耦合与宽度。

Result: 该方法适用于任意q q̄ ℓ⁺ℓ⁻接触相互作用情景，无论假定的底层结构和耦合如何；同时适用于寻找s道产生的中等至大宽度新中性共振，且无需重新生成样本即可修改质量、耦合或宽度参数。

Conclusion: 所提NLO重加权方法为LHC高不变质量双轻子新物理研究提供了一种通用、高效且精确的工具，显著降低了探索广泛新物理参数空间的模拟成本。

Abstract: We present an optimal method for studying new physics effects in high-mass dilepton production at the LHC. This method relies on a reweighting procedure that is precise to the next-to-leading order in quantum chromodynamics. The only computationally expensive input required is a standard model $\mathrm{pp\rightarrow V^{(*)}\rightarrow\ell^+\ell^-}$ sample, with $\mathrm{V=Z,γ^*}$, generated at the same level of precision and with sufficient statistical coverage of the phase space at high invariant masses. The proposed method is suitable for any $\mathrm{q\overline{q}\ell^+\ell^-}$ contact-interaction scenario, regardless of the assumed underlying structure and couplings. It is also ideal for searching for medium-to-large-width new neutral resonances produced in the s-channel, as changes to the input values for mass, coupling, or width can be easily implemented using the same initial generated samples.

</details>


### [267] [Confronting $K^-d$ dynamics with kaonic atom measurements within a novel unitary framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01977)
*Albert Feijoo,Àngels Ramos,Juan Torres-Rincon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作基于手征K̄N相互作用模型（OR与BCN），在Faddeev方程的固定中心近似下构造K⁻d三体振幅，并引入相干多次散射过程以满足幺正性约束，扩展至耦合道与同位旋破缺效应，进而用SIDDHARTA-2的kaonic氢/氘测量对BCN模型进行bootstrap分析，实现参数重调并揭示其对K̄N同位旋敏感观测量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能K⁻d相互作用的理论描述常缺乏幺正性约束，且K̄N相互作用的同位旋矢量分量（isovector component）约束较差。新的kaonic原子实验（SIDDHARTA-2对kaonic氢和kaonic氘的测量）提供了额外信息，需要评估其与现有K̄N模型（如BCN）的兼容性并据此改进模型。

Method: 采用手征K̄N相互作用模型（OR与BCN）作为输入；在Faddeev方程的固定中心近似（Fixed-Center Approximation）下构建三体K⁻d振幅，通过纳入相干多次散射过程形式化实现常规方法中缺失的幺正约束；将幺正流程扩展至耦合道，包含电荷交换过程，并使用物理粒子质量引入同位旋破缺效应；据此确定散射长度与有效程；最后通过bootstrap分析评估BCN模型与SIDDHARTA-2 kaonic氢、氘测量数据的兼容性。

Result: 该幺正流程给出了低能K⁻d振幅的自洽描述，并得到散射长度与有效程；bootstrap分析导致BCN参数化的重新调整，该调整在若干对同位旋敏感的K̄N散射观测量中产生可见效应。

Conclusion: 新kaonic原子测量数据为更好约束K̄N相互作用中约束较差的同位旋矢量分量提供了额外信息，凸显了kaonic原子实验对改进K̄N相互作用模型的价值。

Abstract: We study the low-energy interaction of negative kaons with deuterons using chiral $\bar{K}N$ interaction models (OR and BCN) as input. The three-body $K^-d$ amplitude is constructed within the Fixed-Center Approximation to the Faddeev equations, where the unitary constraints missing in the conventional approach are formally implemented with the incorporation of coherent multiple-scattering processes. The unitary procedure is extended to coupled channels including charge-exchange processes, as well as isospin-breaking effects through the use of physical particle masses. This provides a consistent description of the low-energy $K^-d$ amplitude and allows for the determination of the scattering length and the effective range. The resulting framework is then used to assess the compatibility of the BCN $\bar{K}N$ interaction model with the recent SIDDHARTA-2 measurements of kaonic hydrogen and kaonic deuterium through a bootstrap analysis. The procedure leads to a readjustment of the BCN parameterization which produces visible effects in several isospin-sensitive $\bar{K}N$ scattering observables, hence highlighting the additional information provided by the new kaonic-atom measurements to better constrain the poorly known isovector component of the ${\bar K}N$ interaction.

</details>


### [268] [Effective field theory of scalar glueballs: Form factors and interaction radii](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01988)
*Adamu Issifu,Orlando Oliveira,Tobias Frederico*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于Yang-Mills胶子凝聚，构建标量胶球相互作用的规范不变有效场论，导出胶球形状因子并预测相互作用半径，与格点结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量胶球（如f_0(1710)）的动力学需要在有效场论与第一性原理格点计算之间建立系统联系，尤其其相互作用半径与形状因子缺乏参数化理论描述。

Method: 从Yang-Mills真空的相干态表述出发，推导标量胶球及其与胶子相互作用的有效拉氏量；将交叉对称的四胶子截断格林函数投影到色单态J^{PC}=0^{++}通道，得到归一的标量胶球形状因子，其分解为普适运动学结构与内禀胶球函数；并纳入第一激发0^{++}态修正。

Result: 预测基态标量胶球（共振f_0(1710)）的无参数有效相互作用半径 r_int=sqrt(6)/m_φ=0.28 fm，与格点Yang-Mills质量-半径测定值0.263(31) fm高度吻合；形状因子动量依赖与格点引力形状因子数据一致，f_0(1710)给出最佳整体拟合；纳入第一激发态后得到r_int^*=0.240 fm的适度修正及稳定的形状因子变化。

Conclusion: 该框架为标量胶球动力学的有效场论与第一性原理格点Yang-Mills计算之间提供了系统性桥梁。

Abstract: We develop a gauge-invariant effective field theory for scalar glueball interactions based on the Yang-Mills gluon condensate. Starting from a coherent-state formulation of the Yang-Mills vacuum, we derive an effective Lagrangian for the scalar glueball and its interactions with gluons. The resulting crossing-symmetric amputated four-gluon Green's function is projected onto the color-singlet $J^{PC}=0^{++}$ channel, yielding a normalized scalar glueball form factor that factorizes into universal kinematic structures and an intrinsic glueball function. The formalism predicts a parameter-free effective interaction radius for the ground-state scalar glueball with resonance $f_0(1710)$, $r_{\rm int}=\sqrt{6}/m_φ=0.28$ fm, which is in excellent agreement with recent lattice Yang-Mills determinations of the mass-radius ($r=0.263(31)$ fm). The predicted momentum dependence of the normalized form factor is consistent with lattice Yang-Mills gravitational form factor data, with the $f_0(1710)$ candidate providing the best overall fit and $r_{\rm int}$. Inclusion of the first excited $0^{++}$ glueball produces modest corrections to the interaction radius ($r_{\rm int}^*=0.240$ fm) while yielding stable and kinematically robust modifications of the form factor. This framework provides a systematic bridge between effective field theory and first-principles lattice Yang-Mills calculations of scalar glueball dynamics.

</details>


### [269] [Electron and monopole properties in a dark matter model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02083)
*Jacob García-Moreno,Vicente Vento*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将引入暗单极子的QED模型推广至电弱理论，通过暗扇区（暗光子与暗单极子）与可见扇区的混合，使电子等粒子转化为双荷子，并分析两个模型中对电子的实验可观测性，指出暗光子质量是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单极子自被提出用以对称化麦克斯韦方程以来，一直是理论与实验研究的热点，但迄今尚无存在性证据。近期提出的含暗无质量光子与暗单极子的QED推广模型，通过两扇区间的混合相互作用将轻子与单极子转化为双荷子，为研究单极子物理提供了新途径。作者希望进一步将这一思路推广到电弱理论。

Method: 在电弱理论中引入含大质量光子与单极子的暗扇区，采用与先前QED模型相同的混合机制，使电子等粒子转变为双荷子；随后分别在QED推广模型与电弱推广模型框架下，推导并分析电子相关的实验后果，考察暗光子质量对可观测性的影响。

Result: 将原有QED模型成功推广至电弱理论，构建了含大质量暗光子与暗单极子的暗扇区扩展；分析表明，在两个模型中电子均因混合效应而获得双荷子性质，且暗光子的质量对实验可观测性起决定性作用。

Conclusion: 通过将单极子纳入电弱理论的暗扇区扩展，为单极子物理提供了新的实验探测路径；暗光子质量是决定该类效应能否被观测的关键参数。

Abstract: Monopoles have been a subject of much theoretical and experimental research since they were proposed to symmetrize Maxwell's equations. No experimental signature of their existence has been detected. A model generalizing QED with dark massless photons and dark monopoles was proposed not long ago. In this model, by introducing a mixing interaction between the two sectors, leptons and monopoles are transformed into into dyons. This particular behavior of the electrons determines a new path to study the physics of monopoles. Here we generalize the Electroweak theory in the same way by incorporating a dark sector with massive photons and monopoles, and analyze experimental consequences for electrons in both models. The mass of the dark photon plays an important role regarding observability.

</details>


### [270] [A consistent $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD description of decays and high-$P_T$ prompt production of charmonia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02096)
*Valerio Bertone,Jean-Philippe Lansberg,Kate Lynch,Tanjona R. Rabemananjara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于高$P_T$ LHC数据，在NLO+NLL领头幂碎裂框架下全局确定NRQCD中6个长程矩阵元(LDMEs)，能同时很好地描述$J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$和$\chi_c$的强子产生及极化数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验$\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD能否统一描述多种粲偶素（$J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$、$\chi_c$）在大$P_T$下的强子产生，需通过全局拟合长程矩阵元来解决此前LDMEs确定不唯一、难以同时符合截面与极化数据的问题。

Method: 采用NLO+NLL领头幂碎裂框架，利用$\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV下ATLAS与CMS的$\psi(2S)/J/\psi$产额比及CMS的$\psi(2S)$极化数据，拟合6个$\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ LDMEs，每个粲偶素注入一个衰变宽度，并计入$\chi_c$与$\psi(2S)$的直接及feed-down贡献。

Result: 拟合得到的LDMEs可对CMS与ATLAS的$J/\psi$截面数据（$P_T$高达360 GeV）以及CMS的$J/\psi$极化数据给出极好的预言，与产生和衰变数据均高度吻合。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD足以描述大$P_T$下$J/\psi$、$\psi(2S)$和$\chi_c$的强子产生。

Abstract: We present a global determination of the six long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs) within non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD) appearing in the production of prompt $J/ψ$ up to $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ using high-$P_T$ LHC data for direct and feed-down contributions from $χ_c$ and $ψ(2S)$. Using a leading-power fragmentation framework at NLO+NLL, we fit $ψ(2S)/J/ψ$ yield ratios from ATLAS and CMS and $ψ(2S)$ CMS polarization data at $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV, injecting one decay width per charmonium. The fit LDMEs provide excellent predictions of the CMS and ATLAS cross-section data up to $P_T=360$ GeV for the $J/ψ$, as well as the $J/ψ$ polarization data from CMS. The obtained excellent agreement with production and decay data demonstrates that $\mathcal{O}(v^2)$ NRQCD is sufficient to describe $J/ψ$, $ψ(2S)$, and $χ_c$ hadroproduction at large $P_T$.

</details>


### [271] [The Scalar MSW Effect: Compact analytical formulas for neutrino oscillations with large matter effects, including $μ-μ$ and $τ-τ$ dominant matter potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02107)
*Sandhya Choubey,Andreas Lund*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Jacobi对角化方法通过特定的Euler旋转参数化PMNS混合矩阵，推广到奇异物质势，导出了标量非标准相互作用（SNSI）的有效混合参数映射，并评估了其精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解未来中微子实验现象学需要简单且精确的三味中微子在物质中的振荡概率公式，而已有方法难以处理奇异物质势。

Method: 扩展Jacobi对角化方法，通过特定排列的Euler旋转对PMNS混合矩阵U_PMNS进行参数化；先复验标准物质效应的情形，再将其应用于标量非标准相互作用（SNSI），推导三种单对角耦合情形下的有效参数映射，并加入一阶微扰修正。

Result: 所有对角SNSI耦合均可产生标量MSW（SMSW）效应，即类似MSW效应的能量无关共振增强；对δm_ee精度极佳；对δm_μμ和δm_ττ精度较差（因须忽略A_CC），需一阶微扰修正改善。

Conclusion: 该扩展的Jacobi对角化方法能有效导出奇异物质势下的精确有效混合参数映射，揭示了SNSI的SMSW效应，其公式对δm_ee在各类实验中精度优秀，对δm_μμ和δm_ττ经微扰修正后精度可改善。

Abstract: Deriving simple and accurate three-flavor neutrino oscillation probability formulas in matter is critical for understanding the phenomenology of upcoming experiments. In this article, we extend the Jacobi diagonalization method for deriving effective parameter mappings for neutrino oscillations in matter. The key insight behind the extension is to purposefully parameterize the mixing matrix, $U_{\text{PMNS}}$, using particular arrangements of Euler rotations. This novel extension allows for deriving simple and accurate effective mixing parameter mappings for exotic types of matter potentials. First, we revisit the Jacobi diagonalization method for neutrino oscillations with standard matter effects and use this to demonstrate our generalization. Subsequently, we apply the Jacobi diagonalization method to scalar non-standard interactions (SNSI) and derive effective parameter mappings for all three single diagonal coupling cases. The resulting formulas reveal that all diagonal SNSI couplings could give rise to a scalar MSW (SMSW) effect, that is an energy independent resonant enhancement analogous to the MSW effect. Finally, we study the accuracy of the derived SNSI formulas for various contemporary and upcoming experiments. For $δm_{ee}$, the accuracy is excellent for all considered experiments. For $δm_{μμ}$ and $δm_{ττ}$ the accuracy is worse since $A_{CC}$ must be neglected, and first order perturbative corrections are applied to amend this.

</details>


### [272] [Drell-Yan lepton pair production from low to high transverse momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02169)
*Benoît Assi,Enrico Bothmann,John Campbell,Christian Gütschow,Stefan Höche,Wan-Li Ju,Max Knobbe,Marek Schönherr,Jesse Thaler,Michael L. Wagman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个粒子级Drell-Yan轻子对产生计算方法，在小到中等横动量区达到N³LO和近似N⁴LL′精度，在高横动量区包含Z+4/5/6喷注的树级精度与近似NLO电弱精度，并采用连续极限格点QCD给出的Collins-Soper核，最后通过基于最大熵原理的信息论方法将高精度理论结果转为严格正的事件权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drell-Yan过程是LHC精密测量的关键基准过程，需要在全横动量范围内同时具备高微扰精度与非微扰输入，并需与粒子级事件生成及蒙特卡洛事件权重相兼容。

Method: 在粒子级实现Drell-Yan轻子对产生：小到中等横动量采用N³LO加近似N⁴LL′重求和；高横动量采用Z+4、5、6喷注的树级精度并含近似NLO电弱修正；非微扰Collins-Soper核取自连续极限格点QCD结果；利用基于最大熵原理的信息论方法将理论结果重铸为严格正的事件权重。

Result: 实现了跨横动量范围的高精度统一描述，并得到严格正的事件权重，可直接用于蒙特卡洛事件生成与实验分析。

Conclusion: 该技术为Drell-Yan过程提供了高精度、全区间且与事件生成兼容的理论预言，结合格点QCD非微扰输入与最大熵加权方案，适用于LHC精密测量。

Abstract: We present a technique for computing Drell-Yan lepton pair production at particle-level that achieves N$^3$LO and approximate N$^4$LL$'$ accuracy at small to moderate transverse momentum, tree-level precision for $Z+4$, $5$, and $6$ jets, and approximate NLO EW precision at high transverse momentum. A crucial non-perturbative input, the Collins-Soper kernel, is obtained from continuum-limit lattice QCD results. High-precision theory results are recast into strictly positive event weights using an information-theoretic approach based on the maximum entropy principle.

</details>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [Direct Group Laws on Classical Genus-One Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00029)
*Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文用"剩余相交"统一构造多类椭圆曲线模型上的群律，并以"亏格1光滑射影曲线无单极点非平凡有理函数"这一基本事实证明结合律；给出特征2、3下的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对各椭圆曲线模型（Weierstrass、Edwards、Hessian等）的群律多分别处理，缺乏统一、初等的构造与结合律证明框架，尤其对低特征（2、3）的显式公式讨论不足。作者希望用统一的几何方法（直线/平面与曲线的剩余相交）一并处理多种模型。

Method: 方法为"剩余相交"（residual intersection）：在平面三次曲线上，用直线给出群运算；在PP^3中两张二次曲面光滑交线上，用平面给出运算——即取过两点的直线/平面，其与曲线的第三个/剩余交点定义和。结合律通过比较三个直线或平面方程乘积：先消去公共零点，剩余的零点等价性由"亏格1光滑射影曲线不存在仅有一个单极点的非常值有理函数"这一初等事实推出。对曲线(u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d的公式在任意特征下成立，并显式写出特征2与3的情形；其他模型在特征≠2,3下处理。

Result: 对短Weierstrass三次曲线、(u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d、Edwards曲线、Hessian曲线、Jacobi四次曲线、Jacobi交线曲线、Huff曲线及对称QRT双二次曲线，统一构造了群律并证明了结合律；给出了(u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d在任意特征（含2、3）下的显式公式，其余模型给出特征≠2,3下的公式。

Conclusion: 以剩余相交为核心，可统一、初等地导出多种椭圆曲线模型的群律与结合律证明；结合律本质上归结为亏格1曲线除子理论的简单事实。低特征（2、3）的处理被专门展开，为不同曲线模型的密码学与算术应用提供了统一公式基础。

Abstract: This exposition constructs group laws by residual intersection on short Weierstrass cubics, the curves $(u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d$, Edwards curves, Hessian curves, Jacobi quartics curves, Jacobi intersections curves, Huff curves, and a symmetric QRT biquadratic. Lines give the operation on plane cubics, while planes give the operation on smooth intersections of two quadrics in $\PP^3$. Associativity is proved by comparing two products of three line or plane equations. After the common zeros are cancelled, equality of the remaining zeros follows from the elementary fact that a smooth projective curve of genus one admits no nonconstant rational function with one simple pole. The formulas for $(u^2+u)(v^2+v)=d$ are valid in every characteristic and are written explicitly in characteristics $2$ and $3$; the other models are treated in characteristic different from $2$ and $3$.

</details>


### [274] [Categorical Torelli theorem for (weighted) hypersurfaces via singularity category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00130)
*Xun Lin,Jieheng Zeng,Shizhuo Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文利用完备仿射锥的奇点范畴证明普通及加权超曲面的范畴Torelli定理：Fano情形用Kuznetsov分量，一般型情形用分次矩阵分解范畴；在度与权重和互素时，内部平移可用Serre函子与上同调平移表出，从而dg轨道内蕴，取perfect hull后与完备锥的dg奇点范畴一致，再由范畴Mather–Yau定理恢复形式奇点。一般齐次方程情形给出射影线性等价，加权情形由对偶环面作用确定Tjurina代数的分次进而确定加权齐次方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Torelli定理关注形式/解析不变量能否由几何范畴恢复；本文动机在于建立超曲面（普通与加权）的范畴Torelli定理，并统一处理Fano与一般型两类超曲面，同时给出一般型超曲面的新结果。

Method: 以完备仿射锥的奇点范畴为核心：Fano超曲面使用Kuznetsov分量，一般型超曲面使用全部分次矩阵分解范畴。当次数与权重和互素时，将内部分次平移用Serre函子与上同调平移表示，证明其dg轨道内蕴，并在取perfect hull后与完备锥的dg奇点范畴一致；再通过范畴Mather–Yau定理恢复形式奇点。对普通齐次方程得到射影线性等价，对加权情形利用对偶环面作用确定Tjurina代数的分次，从而恢复加权齐次方程。

Result: 在所述数值假设下，对Fano超曲面给出统一证明；并证明了一般型超曲面的新的范畴Torelli定理。普通齐次方程情形导出射影线性等价，加权情形由对偶环面作用确定Tjurina代数的分次并恢复加权齐次方程。

Conclusion: 该文通过奇点范畴与矩阵分解范畴，建立了普通与加权超曲面的范畴Torelli定理；在次数与权重和互素的条件下，内部平移的dg轨道内蕴且与完备锥的dg奇点范畴一致，可恢复形式奇点及相应齐次方程，并给出一般型超曲面的新结果。

Abstract: We prove categorical Torelli theorems for ordinary and weighted hypersurfaces using the singularity category of the completed affine cone. For Fano hypersurfaces we use the Kuznetsov component, whereas for hypersurfaces of general type we use the full category of graded matrix factorizations. When the degree is coprime to the sum of the weights, the internal grading shift can be expressed in terms of the Serre functor and the cohomological shift. Its dg orbit is therefore intrinsic and, after taking the perfect hull, agrees with the dg singularity category of the completed cone. The formal singularity is then recovered by the categorical Mather--Yau theorem. For ordinary homogeneous equations, the resulting formal equivalence induces a projective linear equivalence; in the weighted setting the dual torus action determines the grading of the Tjurina algebra and hence the weighted homogeneous equation. The argument gives a uniform proof for Fano hypersurfaces under the stated numerical assumptions and proves new categorical Torelli theorems for hypersurfaces of general type.

</details>


### [275] [Completed cycle transfer and the conifold gap for (P^2, E)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00179)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明连通椭圆完备圈迹的正则性定理，并据此建立射影平面在光滑三次边界与顶级Hodge类插入下的极大接触相对Gromov-Witten理论的Nekrasov-Shatashvili conifold gap。通过higher genus local/relative correspondence与椭圆迹修正、中心差分恒等式，证明非真空连通贡献可被conifold坐标与耦合平方整除，从而将普通局部gap经三角归纳转移到相对理论，并给出有限部分恒等式、正则Taylor系数有限公式及全纯歧义重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conifold boundary conditions constrain higher genus topological string free energies, but the cancellation of subleading poles is not implied by their leading singular behavior, so the previously known leading behavior is insufficient to control the full polar structure. The paper aims to prove the needed regularity and thereby transfer the known ordinary local projective plane gap to the relative theory.

Method: The authors prove a regularity theorem for connected elliptic completed cycle traces, then apply the higher genus local/relative correspondence to express the ordinary potential via the relative potential and differentiated lower genus contributions. They identify the conifold polarized correction with an elliptic trace, use a central difference identity to resum each non-vacuum partition weight into a content product times a regular exponential, and perform triangular induction.

Result: For arbitrary regular parts of the input potentials and any nome with a simple zero, the normalized non-vacuum connected contribution is divisible by the conifold coordinate and by the square of the coupling; hence the entire polar and finite parts are determined by the vacuum constants. The known ordinary local projective plane gap is transferred to the relative theory. The paper also obtains finite part identities, finite formulas for regular Taylor coefficients, and reconstruction of the holomorphic ambiguities.

Conclusion: The Nekrasov-Shatashvili conifold gap is established for the maximal contact relative Gromov-Witten theory of the projective plane with a smooth cubic boundary and a top Hodge class insertion. The argument is formal in the coupling and yields holomorphic remainders at every fixed genus.

Abstract: Conifold boundary conditions constrain higher genus topological string free energies, but the cancellation of subleading poles is not implied by their leading singular behavior. We prove a regularity theorem for connected elliptic completed cycle traces and use it to establish the Nekrasov-Shatashvili conifold gap for the maximal contact relative Gromov-Witten theory of the projective plane with a smooth cubic boundary and a top Hodge class insertion. The higher genus local/relative correspondence expresses the ordinary potential in terms of the relative potential and differentiated lower genus contributions. We identify its conifold polarized correction with an elliptic trace. A central difference identity resums each non-vacuum partition weight into a content product multiplied by a regular exponential. For arbitrary regular parts of the input potentials and any nome with a simple zero, the normalized non-vacuum connected contribution is divisible by the conifold coordinate and by the square of the coupling. The entire polar part and finite part are consequently determined by the vacuum constants. A triangular induction transfers the known ordinary local projective plane gap to the relative theory. We also obtain finite part identities, finite formulas for regular Taylor coefficients, and reconstruction of the holomorphic ambiguities. The argument is formal in the coupling and gives holomorphic remainders at every fixed genus.

</details>


### [276] [A characterization of the Ree curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00189)
*Saeed Tafazolian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在 q=3q₀²（q₀=3ˢ, s≥3）情形下，若光滑射影几何不可约曲线在 F_q 上有 q³+1 个有理点且亏格为 (3/2)q₀(q−1)(q+q₀+1)，则该曲线 F_q-同构于 Ree 曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ree 曲线是一类重要的极大曲线，其有理点数与亏格由上述公式给出。本文的动机是给出 Ree 曲线的一个刻画性结果，即在特定参数与有理点、亏格条件下唯一确定 Ree 曲线。

Method: 设定 q=3q₀²，其中 q₀=3ˢ 且 s≥3。对满足 F_q 上 q³+1 个有理点、亏格为 (3/2)q₀(q−1)(q+q₀+1) 的光滑射影几何不可约曲线进行刻画，证明其必 F_q-同构于 Ree 曲线。

Result: 光滑射影几何不可约曲线若具有 q³+1 个 F_q-有理点且亏格为 (3/2)q₀(q−1)(q+q₀+1)，则它 F_q-同构于 Ree 曲线。

Conclusion: 该结果为 Ree 曲线提供了一个基于有理点数目与亏格的唯一性刻画，在 q=3q₀²（q₀=3ˢ, s≥3）条件下成立。

Abstract: Let $q=3q_0^2$, where $q_0=3^s$ and $s\ge3$. We prove that a smooth, projective, geometrically irreducible curve over $\F_q$ with $q^3+1$ rational points and genus \[
  \frac32q_0(q-1)(q+q_0+1) \] is $\F_q$-isomorphic to the Ree curve.

</details>


### [277] [All Genus Gopakumar-Vafa Reconstruction from Central Difference Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00217)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入一个中心差分算子，将曲线计数生成函数中的"多重覆盖（曲线次数）"与"拓扑弦耦合（BPS 亏格）"两类信息以可逆方式分离，得到 Chebyshev-Fejér 幽灵变换及其算子值 Möbius 逆，并刻画其整性像与局部曲线几何实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲线计数生成函数中混有两类信息：多重覆盖混合曲线次数，拓扑弦耦合混合 BPS 亏格。已有工作（如 genus zero 的经典特化重构）只处理单亏格情形，缺乏一个能同时分离二者、且对所有亏格统一的机制。作者希望找到一个单一算子，使这种分离精确且可逆，并把 genus zero 重构理解为全亏格层级的第一分次片。

Method: 令 y=(2 sin(λ/2))^2，将 Bryan-Pandharipande 覆盖多项式与相应 Fejér 因子结合，构造归一化差分 forcing 的 Chebyshev-Fejér 幽灵变换；给出其算子值 Möbius 逆。分析该变换的 y-adic 滤过，证明其恰为 BPS 亏格滤过。进一步刻画变换的整性像，给出 Witt-Dwork 型 Frobenius 同余与 Fejér 可除性条件的两阶段判据。最后在局部曲线上给出几何实现。

Result: 1) forcing 模 y^{G+1} 等价于亏格不超过 G 的全部 BPS 不变量，故经典特化的 genus zero 重构是全亏格层级的第一 associated graded 片。2) 整 BPS forcing 等价于两阶段条件：幽灵坐标的 Witt-Dwork 型 Frobenius 同余 + 中心差分算子施加的额外 Fejér 可除性条件；在素数幂次数下得到包含完整 Fejér 因子的强化 Dwork 同余。3) 局部曲线情形：超刚性椭圆曲线给出 forcing 的精确 Dedekind η 表达式；平衡高阶局部曲线理论中，上次数二 BPS 不变量实现为对称积上平方根自然 stack 的 top Chern 数。

Conclusion: 中心差分方程与 BPS 重构、算术幽灵结构及局部曲线几何之间存在深刻联系：一个中心差分算子即可精确可逆地分离多重覆盖与亏格效应，其 y-adic 滤过即 BPS 亏格滤过，整性由 Frobenius 同余加 Fejér 可除性刻画，并在局部曲线上获得具体几何实现。

Abstract: Generating functions in curve counting theory mix two different kinds of information: multiple covers mix curve degrees, while the topological string coupling mixes BPS genera. We show that a single central difference operator separates these two effects in a precise and invertible way for the all genus Gopakumar-Vafa expansion along an effective ray of a Calabi-Yau threefold.
  Writing \[ y=\left(2\sin\fracλ{2}\right)^2, \] we combine the Bryan-Pandharipande cover polynomials with the corresponding Fejér factors and obtain a Chebyshev-Fejér ghost transform for the normalized difference forcing. The transform admits an explicit operator-valued Möbius inverse. Its $y$-adic filtration is exactly the BPS genus filtration: the forcing modulo $y^{G+1}$ is equivalent to the complete collection of BPS invariants of genera at most $G$. Thus the genus zero reconstruction from the classical specialization is the first associated graded piece of an all genus hierarchy.
  We also characterize the integral image of the transform. Integral BPS forcing is equivalent to a two stage condition: Witt-Dwork type Frobenius congruences for the underlying ghost coordinates, together with an additional Fejér divisibility condition imposed by the central difference operator. In prime power degree this gives a strengthened Dwork congruence containing the full Fejér factor.
  For local curves, the formalism has concrete geometric realizations. A super-rigid elliptic curve gives an exact Dedekind $η$ expression for the forcing. In the balanced higher genus local curve theory, the upper degree two BPS invariants are realized as top Chern numbers on natural stacks of square roots over symmetric products. These results connect central difference equations with BPS reconstruction, arithmetic ghost structures, and the geometry of local curves.

</details>


### [278] [Cohomology of the fine compactified universal Jacobians $\overline{J}_2$ and $\overline{J}_{2,1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01829)
*Marco Fava*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 计算了亏格2、0个与1个标记点的稳定曲线模空间上精细紧化万有雅可比簇的有理上同调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细紧化万有雅可比簇是代数曲线模空间研究中的重要对象，其上同调结构可揭示模空间的几何与拓扑性质。已有工作主要集中于一般亏格的理论框架，而低亏格（尤其亏格2）的具体计算结果尚不明确，需要给出显式刻画。

Method: 针对亏格2、标记点数n=0和n=1的情形，构造精细紧化万有雅可比簇，并利用其代数几何结构（如与稳定曲线模空间的纤维化关系）计算有理上同调群。

Result: 得到了亏格2且n=0、1时精细紧化万有雅可比簇的有理上同调群的具体描述。

Conclusion: 在亏格2低标记点情形下，精细紧化万有雅可比簇的有理上同调可被显式计算，为理解其几何结构及相关模空间的上同调提供了具体数据。

Abstract: We compute the rational cohomology of the fine compactified universal Jacobians over the moduli spaces of stable curves of genus $2$ with $0$ and $1$ marked points.

</details>


### [279] [Pentagram relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00225)
*Nikita Markarian*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究五点有理曲线模空间上迭代Kummer扩张的上同调，用二维收缩构造比较五个遗忘投影给出的Betti表示，由此得到Hodge缺陷所对应的五角星关系，并证明其与Drinfeld associator的五边形方程等价，进一步推出shuffle与正则化double shuffle关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续先前关于迭代Kummer扩张上同调的工作，旨在通过模空间上的几何构造，理解Drinfeld associator所满足的代数关系及其Hodge实现来源。

Method: 在五点有理曲线模空间上引入二维收缩构造，比较五个遗忘投影诱导的Betti表示，提取Hodge缺陷，得到系数的二次关系（五角星关系），并在group-likeness、归一化和反转条件下讨论其与五边形方程的等价性，结合反转与纯幂系数消失导出shuffle及正则化double shuffle，并用卷积特化恢复Hodge实现。

Result: 得到了Drinfeld associator系数间的五角星二次关系，证明其在group-likeness、归一化与反转下等价于五边形方程；在反转及纯幂系数消失条件下，该关系蕴含shuffle与正则化double shuffle关系，其Hodge实现可由卷积特化恢复。

Conclusion: 模空间上的二维收缩构造为Drinfeld associator的代数关系提供了统一的几何与Hodge理论解释，将五边形方程、shuffle与正则化double shuffle纳入同一框架。

Abstract: We study the cohomology of iterated Kummer extensions on the moduli space of five-pointed rational curves, continuing [arXiv:2609.19341, arXiv:2609.21356]. A two-dimensional shrinking construction compares the Betti presentations obtained from its five forgetful projections. The corresponding Hodge defects give pentagram relations, quadratic relations between coefficients of the Drinfeld associator, which are equivalent to the pentagon equation under group-likeness, normalization, and reversal. Under reversal and vanishing of pure-power coefficients, the relations imply shuffle and regularized double shuffle, whose Hodge realization is recovered by a convolution specialization.

</details>


### [280] [GW/PT-correspondence for CY 4-folds-I: Canonical Orientations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02061)
*Ivan Karpov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该文建立非紧复解析Calabi-Yau 4-流形模栈上定向的凝聚拓扑理论，推广Joyce-Upmeier与Bojko的工作，得到Pandharipande-Thomas对模空间的标准族定向，并为4维GW/PT对应证明提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使Calabi-Yau 4-流形上的Gromov-Witten/PT对应（Pardon纲领在4维的实现）得以证明，需要为相应模空间（如PT对模空间）建立一致的、典范的定向理论。已有Joyce-Upmeier、Bojko等工作，但需在非紧复解析CY 4-流形模栈层面系统化与推广。

Method: 采用代数拓扑方法研究表示KU-理论与KO-理论的映射空间，并利用Lie群E_8以及HSpin(16)的若干性质，来构建模栈上定向的凝聚拓扑理论。

Result: 建立了非紧复解析CY 4-流形模栈上定向的凝聚拓扑理论，特别地获得了所有Pandharipande-Thomas对模空间的典范族定向。

Conclusion: 该工作为与Felix Thimm合作证明CY 4-流形的GW/PT对应提供了定向理论基础，服务于在4维实现Pardon纲领。

Abstract: We build a cohesive topological theory of orientations on moduli stacks for non-compact complex-analytic Calabi-Yau $4$-folds by extending and generalising the work of Joyce and Upmeier, and the work of Bojko. As a result, we get, in particular, canonical family orientations for all moduli spaces of Pandharipande-Thomas pairs. Our proofs use the algebraic topology of mapping spaces representing $KU$- and $KO$- theory, as well as some properties of the Lie groups $E_8$ and, surprisingly, $\operatorname{HSpin}(16)$.
  This is part of a joint project with Felix Thimm dedicated to the proof of the $GW/PT$-correspondence for Calabi-Yau $4$-folds by implementing Pardon's programme in dimension $4$.

</details>


### [281] [Derivative and multiplicity loci of elementary symmetric polynomials in positive characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00259)
*Ying Xie*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文完全确定了正特征代数闭域上初等对称多项式所有导数轨迹与高阶重数轨迹的维数，给出维数公式，分类达到较大维数的分支并证明其泛光滑性，确定局部环与泛重数Q，识别平移稳定子概形并导出Frobenius标准形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正特征下初等对称多项式的奇异轨迹结构，厘清导数零点轨迹与高重数轨迹的维数问题，并解决Orzel猜想中关于阶二情形的维数问题（当d为p的幂时需修正）。

Method: 设n元d次初等对称多项式，令Q = p^{v_p(n-d+1)}。计算阶q导数公共零点轨迹与重数至少s的零点轨迹的维数；分类达到较大维数的不可约分支并证明泛光滑；沿小维数情形的坐标分支计算局部环与泛重数；识别完整平移稳定子概形并推导Frobenius标准形。

Result: 阶q导数公共零点轨迹维数为 d - q - 1 + 1_{Q>d-q}；重数至少s的零点轨迹维数为 d - s + 1_{Q>d}。阶二情形解决Orzel猜想的维数问题（d为p的幂时需修正）。较大维数分支泛光滑；小维数坐标分支泛重数恰为Q；得到平移稳定子概形与Frobenius标准形。

Conclusion: 结果区分了维数跳跃与无穷小加厚，并统一描述了它们对特征的依赖关系。

Abstract: We determine the dimensions of all derivative loci and all higher multiplicity loci of an elementary symmetric polynomial over an algebraically closed field of positive characteristic. For the degree-d polynomial in n variables, set Q = p^{v_p(n-d+1)}. The common zero locus of its order-q derivatives has dimension d - q - 1 + 1_{Q>d-q}, whereas the locus of zeros of multiplicity at least s has dimension d - s + 1_{Q>d}. The order-two case settles the dimension question in a conjecture of Orzel, with a necessary correction when d is a power of p. We classify all components attaining the larger dimension and prove that they are generically smooth. Along the coordinate components in the smaller-dimension cases, we determine the local rings and obtain generic multiplicity exactly Q. Finally, we identify the full translation stabilizer scheme and derive a Frobenius normal form. These results distinguish dimension jumps from infinitesimal thickening and give a uniform description of their dependence on the characteristic.

</details>


### [282] [Contraction Algebras, Cyclotomic Forms, and Reflection Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00263)
*Xiaobin Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文为有限分块数据构造正定分圆型与Householder反射表示，证明其算术刚性定理，并应用于三折flop的收缩代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限分块多重性数据所诱导的反射群何时有限，并将其与半单代数同构型、三折flop收缩代数维数联系起来，寻找有限性判据。

Method: 构造分圆型B_n与Householder反射表示，利用带标号Fourier谱和Möbius反演重构多重性序列；借助Toda宽度公式联系收缩代数；给出BPS/Riemann–Hilbert实现。

Result: N≥2时反射群有限当且仅当保持满秩格，等价于supp(n)={1,N}且n_1=N^2 n_N，此时B_n=(I+J)/2、群为W(A_N)≅S_{N+1}；半单代数情形等价于S≅k^{N^2 m}×M_N(k)^m。N=2非有限情形为D_∞的稠密嵌入。对三折flop收缩代数，长度≥3的不可约flop均无限；长度2时Γ_f有限当且仅当dim A_con=2 dim A_con^ab。

Conclusion: 分圆型反射群的有限性完全由分块支撑与归一化条件刻画，并与半单代数同构型和flop收缩代数的维数关系一致，提供统一算术刚性框架。

Abstract: We associate to finite block size data \[ \bm n=(n_d)_{d\ge1},\qquad n_1>0, \] a positive definite cyclotomic form and a Householder reflection representation. Writing \[ N=\operatorname{lcm}\{d:n_d\ne0\},\qquad \wt=\sum_d d^2n_d, \] the form on the cyclic group $C_N$ is \[ B_{\bm n}(g,h)=\frac1{\wt}\sum_{(g^{-1}h)^d=1}d^2n_d. \] Its labelled Fourier spectrum reconstructs the complete multiplicity sequence by Möbius inversion. If the $n_d$ are the Wedderburn multiplicities of a split semisimple algebra $S=\prod_d M_d(k)^{n_d}$, the divisibility widths are dimensions of characteristic sums of Wedderburn blocks, and the form together with $\dim_kS$ recovers the isomorphism type of $S$.
  The main theorem is an arithmetic rigidity result. For $N\ge2$, the reflection group is finite if and only if it preserves a full rank lattice, equivalently \[ \supp(\bm n)=\{1,N\},\qquad n_1=N^2n_N. \] In this case $B_{\bm n}=\frac12(I+J)$ and the group is $W(A_N)\cong S_{N+1}$. In semisimple algebra terms, finiteness is equivalent to \[ S\cong k^{N^2m}\times M_N(k)^m \] for some $m\ge1$. For $N=2$, every nonfinite case is a dense copy of $D_\infty$ in the corresponding orthogonal group.
  For contraction algebras of threefold flops, Toda's width formulas identify the total scale and primitive Fourier mode with $\dim A_{\con}$ and $\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}$. This yields infinitude for all irreducible flops of length at least three and, in length two, \[ Γ_f\text{ finite}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad \dim A_{\con}=2\dim A_{\con}^{\ab}. \] The final part gives a complementary BPS/Riemann--Hilbert realization of the same reflection matrices.

</details>


### [283] [Motivic Vanishing Cycles at Infinity and Toric Landau-Ginzburg Mirror Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00289)
*Haoxu Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文比较了无穷远处的motivic nearby cycles与有理紧化上的类，证明$1/f$定义的open-pair class与Raibaut紧化无关的motivic nearby cycle at infinity一致，并由此得到非退化Laurent多项式的toric证明、Newton公式、Fano-Landau-Ginzburg镜像恒等式及单值性比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: motivic nearby cycles at infinity此前由Raibaut定义为紧化无关的对象，但缺乏与直接在有理紧化上计算的类之间的明确比较，也缺少toric情形下的直接证明与Hodge/Newton层面的精细化应用。

Method: 设$f$为特征零域上光滑簇的正则函数，$X$为光滑紧化，假设含边界与$\operatorname{div}_X(f)$支集的约化除子在极集附近为简单正规交叉；通过横截零-极交叉点爆破下的局部消去，比较$1/f$定义的open-pair class与Raibaut的motivic nearby cycle at infinity；并结合等变Hodge实现、加权Ehrhart理论、极限权滤过与极限-irregular比较。

Result: 证明open-pair class与Raibaut的motivic nearby cycle at infinity一致；对无穷远非退化Laurent多项式给出Raibaut essential-face公式的toric直接证明；Newton公式保持上同调次数与极限权滤过，并确定irregular Hodge数；权重遗忘特化计算同径向支撑的toric stacky fan的零势stringy不变量，得到对应Fano-Landau-Ginzburg镜像恒等式的motivic-Hodge推导；在满维弱Fano情形，精细公式将单值性特征值与Jordan块大小识别为惯性年龄与反规范Lefschetz串。

Conclusion: 该比较建立了motivic nearby cycles at infinity与紧化上直接计算之间的联系，并为essential-face公式、Newton公式、irregular Hodge数、镜像恒等式及单值性提供统一框架，在$\mathbb C$上给出非规范比较。

Abstract: We compare motivic nearby cycles at infinity with classes computed directly on rational compactifications. Let $f$ be a regular function on a smooth variety over a field of characteristic zero, and let $X$ be a smooth proper compactification. Suppose that a reduced divisor containing the boundary and the support of $\operatorname{div}_X(f)$ has simple normal crossings near the polar locus. We prove that the open-pair class defined by $1/f$ agrees with Raibaut's compactification-independent motivic nearby cycle at infinity. The key step is a local cancellation under blow-ups of transverse zero-pole intersections.
  For Laurent polynomials nondegenerate at infinity, this comparison yields a direct toric proof of Raibaut's essential-face formula. Equivariant Hodge realization and weighted Ehrhart theory give Newton formulas retaining cohomological degree and the limit-weight filtration, and determine irregular Hodge numbers through the limiting-irregular comparison. The weight-forgetting specialization computes the zero-potential stringy invariant of toric stacky fans with the same radial support, yielding a motivic-Hodge derivation of the corresponding Fano-Landau-Ginzburg mirror identity. In the full-dimensional weak Fano case, the refined formulas identify monodromy eigenvalues and Jordan-block sizes with inertia ages and anticanonical Lefschetz strings, giving a noncanonical comparison over $\mathbb C$.

</details>


### [284] [Transcending Algebraic Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00335)
*Carlos Simpson*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述性阐述，旨在梳理通向Jacob Tsimerman在算术与代数几何中发展o-极小可定义性理论这一突破性工作的相关思想与背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍Tsimerman将o-极小性引入算术与代数几何这一开创性成果之前的思想脉络，帮助读者理解该理论的来源与发展线索。

Method: 采用思想史式的阐述（exposition）方式，梳理并串联若干关键概念与前期工作，为理解o-极小可定义性理论提供背景铺垫。

Result: 概述了o-极小可定义性理论在算术与代数几何中发展的思想基础，属于背景性、导引性成果，而非新的定理证明。

Conclusion: 该文定位为导论性综述，强调Tsimerman工作的突破意义，并为后续深入阅读提供思想框架。

Abstract: We give an exposition of some of the ideas leading up to Jacob Tsimerman's groundbreaking work developing the theory of o-minimal definability in arithmetic and algebraic geometry.

</details>


### [285] [A Pluripotential-Theoretic Approach to Finiteness of Polarized Calabi--Yau Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00361)
*Truong Dinh Dat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用多重位势论（global m-Hessian容量）研究极化 Calabi–Yau 流形有限性问题的框架，将 m-Hessian 容量作为连接复势论与整体几何有界性的定量中介。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极化 Calabi–Yau 流形的有限性（微分同胚型与拓扑型数量有限）本身涉及分析（度量紧性、直径与曲率控制）与代数（Hilbert 多项式、Hilbert 概型、光滑族有界性）两方面。作者希望不把这些蕴含关系视为自动成立，而建立一个可检验的、结构化的程序，明确其中需要进一步发展的关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建一条统一估计链：m-Hessian 容量 → 体积–容量不等式 → 容量–周长估计 → Sobolev 界 → 相应 Ricci-flat Kähler 度量的非塌缩；在适当正则性假设下导出统一的直径与曲率控制，从而得到度量紧性。代数部分则通过有界次数的统一射影嵌入，借助 Macaulay–Gotzmann 理论得到有限多个可能的 Hilbert 多项式及有限个 Hilbert 概型，再用 Ehresmann 纤维化定理将光滑代数族的有界性转化为微分同胚型与拓扑型的有限性。

Result: 给出了一个连接复 Hessian 势论与几何、代数有界性的结构化纲领。指出容量–周长估计以及从前述度量估计导出统一代数嵌入数据这两步，是本质上仍需进一步发展的关键环节。

Conclusion: 文章并不假定度量紧性必然蕴含代数嵌入有界性等蕴含关系成立，而是将其组织为一个显式的、分层的证明程序，从而分离并突出 Calabi–Yau 有限性问题中主要的分析瓶颈与代数瓶颈。

Abstract: We propose a pluripotential-theoretic framework for studying finiteness questions for polarized Calabi--Yau manifolds. The central idea is to use global \(m\)-Hessian capacities as a quantitative intermediate between complex potential theory and global geometric boundedness. We formulate a chain of uniform estimates connecting \(m\)-Hessian capacity, volume--capacity inequalities, capacity--perimeter estimates, Sobolev bounds, and non-collapsing of the associated Ricci-flat Kähler metrics. Under suitable regularity assumptions, these estimates lead to uniform diameter and curvature control and hence to metric compactness. We then describe the algebraic part of the argument. Uniform projective embeddings with bounded degree lead, through the Macaulay--Gotzmann theory, to only finitely many possible Hilbert polynomials and hence to a finite collection of Hilbert schemes. Ehresmann's fibration theorem then converts boundedness of the smooth algebraic families into finiteness of diffeomorphism and topological types. The resulting framework isolates the main analytic and algebraic bottlenecks in a potential-theoretic approach to Calabi--Yau finiteness. In particular, the capacity--perimeter estimate and the derivation of uniform algebraic embedding data from the preceding metric estimates constitute the essential steps requiring further development. Thus the paper provides a structured program linking complex Hessian potential theory with geometric and algebraic boundedness, rather than assuming that these implications are automatic.

</details>


### [286] [Stratification of Joint Moduli Spaces of Higgs Bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00378)
*Shiyu Cao*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究Teichmüller空间上零次稳定SL_r(C)-Higgs丛的带标记解析模空间：全行列式为一稳定子定义的轨道型轨迹连通分支构成复Whitney分层，投影为全纯淹没，各层带相对全纯辛形式并给出垂直全纯Poisson括号，其辛叶为纤维内轨道型层；Hitchin-Kobayashi对应在每层为双全纯且识别超Kähler结构，调和代表与超Kähler张量沿层光滑变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画Teichmüller空间上Higgs模空间的解析几何结构，特别是由稳定子（轨道型）决定的奇异分层，并把辛/Poisson与超Kähler结构以及Hitchin-Kobayashi对应提升到分层层面，以统一理解纤维与总空间上几何结构的相容性与光滑依赖。

Method: 考察零次polystable SL_r(C)-Higgs丛带标记解析模空间；以全行列式为一稳定子定义轨道型轨迹，取连通分支；证明其形成复Whitney分层；分析到Teichmüller空间投影在各层上的全纯淹没性质；构造各层相对全纯辛形式；由这些形式定义约化结构层上的垂直全纯Poisson括号；利用Hitchin-Kobayashi对应在层上与纤维超Kähler结构比对；研究调和代表与垂直超Kähler张量沿层的依赖性。

Result: 轨道型轨迹连通分支构成复Whitney分层；投影在各层为全纯淹没；各层具典范相对全纯辛形式；这些形式确定约化结构层上的垂直全纯Poisson括号，其辛叶正是纤维内轨道型连通层；在每条纤维上，Hitchin-Kobayashi对应在每个层为双全纯并识别其超Kähler结构；调和代表与垂直超Kähler张量沿总空间的层光滑变化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Teichmüller空间上SL_r(C)-Higgs模空间的轨道型分层框架：分层具辛与Poisson结构，垂直辛叶由轨道型层描述，且Hitchin-Kobayashi对应在层上保持超Kähler结构并具光滑依赖性，从而把模空间的解析、辛与超Kähler几何统一到分层视角下。

Abstract: We study the marked analytic moduli space of polystable degree-zero $\mathrm{SL}_r(\C)$-Higgs bundles over Teichmüller space. The connected components of the orbit-type loci defined by the full determinant-one stabilizers form a complex Whitney stratification. The projection to Teichmüller space is a holomorphic submersion on every stratum. Each stratum carries a canonical relative holomorphic symplectic form. These forms determine a vertical holomorphic Poisson bracket on the reduced structure sheaf, whose symplectic leaves are the connected orbit-type strata in the fibers. On each fiber, the Hitchin--Kobayashi correspondence is biholomorphic on every stratum and identifies its hyperkähler structure. The harmonic representatives and vertical hyperkähler tensors vary smoothly along the strata of the total space.

</details>


### [287] [Quasi-modularity of $q$-traces and integrals over Hilbert schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02049)
*Killian Hong-Minh,Sergey Mozgovoy*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限维二次空间及其超空间上玻色Fock空间中规范化q-迹的准模性，证明了一类由自由玻色场及其后代算子生成的q-迹具有准模性且权有界，并给出多个应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范化q-迹的准模性是顶点算子代数与模形式交叉领域的核心问题。已有结果（如Bloch–Okounkov定理）多针对特定情形，缺乏统一框架；同时Qin关于曲面Hilbert概型上tautological积分的准模性猜想有待证明。作者旨在建立一般性的准模性定理并统一已有结果。

Method: 在有限维二次空间及超空间对应的玻色Fock空间上，考察由自由玻色场及其后代算子构成的一类自然算子，分析其规范化q-迹，并结合权估计技术证明准模性，其中权的上界由插入算子的权之和控制。

Result: 证明了该类算子的规范化q-迹是准模的，且权被插入算子权之和所界定。作为推论：证明了Qin关于数值平凡典范类曲面Hilbert概型上tautological积分的准模性猜想；得到Heisenberg顶点算子代数中任意零模关联函数的准模性；并通过秩一特殊化给出Bloch–Okounkov准模性定理的新证明。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了玻色Fock空间上q-迹准模性的统一框架，将Bloch–Okounkov定理视为其秩一特例，并解决了Qin猜想，深化了顶点算子代数、Hilbert概型与模形式之间的联系。

Abstract: We study quasi-modularity of normalized $q$-traces on bosonic Fock spaces associated with finite-dimensional quadratic spaces and superspaces. For a natural class of operators obtained from free-boson fields and their descendants, we prove that their normalized $q$-traces are quasi-modular, with weight bounded by the sum of the weights of the insertions. As applications, we prove Qin's quasi-modularity conjecture for tautological integrals on Hilbert schemes of points of a surface with numerically trivial canonical class, obtain quasi-modularity of arbitrary zero-mode correlation functions in the Heisenberg vertex operator algebra, and give a new proof of the Bloch--Okounkov quasi-modularity theorem as a rank-one specialization of our general result.

</details>


### [288] [Supermoduli spaces are non-projected in every genus $g\geq 3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00490)
*Mauricio Corrêa,Ron Donagi,Simone Noja*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了对任意亏格 $g\geq 3$，光滑无 punctured 超 Riemann 曲面模 superstack 的每个 parity 分支，其主 obstruction 在代数与全纯意义下均非零，因而两分支皆非 projected 且非 split。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究光滑无 punctured 超 Riemann 曲面模 superstack 的 parity 分支的几何结构，判断其是否能退化为（retract 到）其约化模 stack（spin 曲线模 stack），即是否具备 algebraic/holomorphic retraction。这类 split/projected 性质是超几何中区分超结构与普通结构的核心问题。

Method: 计算模 superstack 各 parity 分支的主 obstruction（primary obstruction），并证明该 obstruction 在代数范畴与全纯范畴下均非零。此 obstruction 的存在性直接给出非 projected 性。

Result: 对每个 $g\geq 3$，两个 parity 分支的主 obstruction 均非零（代数与全纯皆然）。

Conclusion: 两 parity 分支均为 non-projected，特别地均为 non-split：不存在到其约化 spin 曲线模 stack 的代数或全纯 retraction。即超 Riemann 曲面模空间不能平凡地分解为普通 spin 曲线模空间与其奇部分。

Abstract: We prove that, for every genus $g\geq 3$, the primary obstruction of each parity component of the moduli superstack of smooth unpunctured super Riemann surfaces is nonzero, both algebraically and holomorphically. Consequently, both components are non-projected and, in particular, non-split: neither admits an algebraic or holomorphic retraction onto its reduced moduli stack of spin curves.

</details>


### [289] [Automorphisms of hypersurfaces in polarized varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00709)
*Ana Quedo*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 提出用截面环的分次Betti数判定一般除子自同构可延拓的统一判据，将Matsumura-Monsky结果推广到更广的极化簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Matsumura-Monsky定理只处理射影空间超曲面的自同构延拓问题，缺少对一般极化簇(F,H)的通用判据，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设F为射影簇，H为正规生成线丛，X∈|H^{⊗d}|为一般射影簇；以截面环R(F,H)的分次Betti数为工具，给出X的每个自同构可延拓为F的自同构的充分条件。

Result: 建立了以R(F,H)分次Betti数表述的统一判据，并在Picard数为1的有理齐性簇、反典范极化的光滑Fano三维簇，以及部分超Kähler与Calabi-Yau簇上验证了该判据的适用性。

Conclusion: 该判据把Matsumura-Monsky关于射影空间超曲面的结论推广到更广泛的极化簇类，为研究一般除子的自同构延拓提供了可计算的充分条件。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a projective variety, let $H$ be a normally generated line bundle on $F$, and let $X\in |H^{\otimes d}|$ be a general projective variety. We establish a uniform criterion, formulated in terms of the graded Betti numbers of the section ring $R(F,H)$, that provides sufficient conditions for every automorphism of $X$ to extend to an automorphism of $F$. This extends the classical results of Matsumura-Monsky on automorphisms of hypersurfaces in projective spaces to a broader class of polarized varieties $(F,H)$, including rational homogeneous varieties of Picard number one, smooth Fano threefolds with their anticanonical polarization, and examples of hyperkähler and Calabi-Yau varieties.

</details>


### [290] [Verdier-Type Category, Geometric Serre's conditions and perversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01146)
*Shang Xu*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文把塞尔对凝聚层的条件推广到带$t$-结构与对偶的三角范畴（Verdier型范畴），研究其与反常层（perversity）的关系，并证明Auslander-Reiten猜想的一个几何版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塞尔条件（如$S_n$、$R_n$、正规性）在交换代数与凝聚层理论中刻画了奇点与模的正则性，但缺乏在三角范畴、尤其是带对偶的$t$-范畴中的系统推广。作者希望建立统一框架，以便在构造层等几何情境下讨论类似的正则性条件，并与反常层理论建立联系。

Method: 1）将塞尔条件公理化推广到带$t$-结构与对偶的三角范畴，定义"Verdier型范畴"及其相关塞尔条件；2）给出此类范畴的构造性例子并计算对应的塞尔条件；3）聚焦构造层，把条件称为"几何塞尔条件"，分析其与$t$-结构、perversity、截断与对偶函子的相容性；4）通过一系列几何与代数例子说明理论。

Result: 建立了Verdier型范畴中塞尔条件的理论框架，给出构造层上几何塞尔条件的完整刻画，并揭示其与perversity分层及对偶的相互作用；在此基础上证明了Auslander-Reiten猜想的一个几何版本。

Conclusion: 塞尔条件可在带$t$-结构与对偶的三角范畴中自然推广，构造层上的几何塞尔条件与perversity密切关联，并为Auslander-Reiten猜想提供了几何上的支持性证据。

Abstract: We generalize Serre's conditions for coherent sheaves to triangulated categories equipped with a $t$-structure and duality, which we call Verdier-type categories. We present examples of such categories and their associated Serre conditions, then focus on constructible sheaves, where we refer to these conditions as geometric Serre conditions. We investigate their relationship with perversity and illustrate the theory through a range of examples. Finally, we prove a geometric version of the Auslander-Reiten conjecture.

</details>


### [291] [An elliptic Calabi-Yau threefold of Mordell-Weil rank twelve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01276)
*Dominik Burek*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 构造了一个光滑投影 Calabi–Yau 三维簇，其平坦椭圆纤维化的 Mordell–Weil 秩为十二，Hodge 数为 (h^{1,1}, h^{2,1}) = (25,1)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高 Mordell–Weil 秩的椭圆纤维化 Calabi–Yau 三维簇，这类例子稀有且与弦论对偶、模性等密切相关；本文旨在给出秩十二的新构造。

Method: 从固定椭圆曲线与 level-four 模族之积所对应的 Kummer 曲面出发构造三维簇；该三维簇双有理等价于 P(O_{P^1}(3)^{⊕2} ⊕ O_{P^1}(4) ⊕ O_{P^1}) 中的反规范四次超曲面；给出十二个独立截面，并构造相应 Weierstrass 模型的投影 crepant 消解。

Result: 得到一个光滑投影 Calabi–Yau 三维簇，具有平坦椭圆纤维化，Mordell–Weil 秩为十二，Hodge 数 (25,1)；显式给出十二个独立截面，并构造了 Weierstrass 模型的投影 crepant 消解。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了高 Mordell–Weil 秩（十二）椭圆纤维化 Calabi–Yau 三维簇的具体几何构造，连接 Kummer 曲面与反规范四次模型。

Abstract: We construct a smooth projective Calabi--Yau threefold with a flat elliptic fibration of Mordell--Weil rank twelve and Hodge numbers $(h^{1,1},h^{2,1})=(25,1)$. The construction starts from the Kummer surfaces associated with the products of a fixed elliptic curve and the level-four modular family. The threefold is birational to an anticanonical quartic in $\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(3)^{\oplus2}\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(4)\oplus\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1})$. We give twelve independent sections and construct a projective crepant resolution of the associated Weierstrass model.

</details>


### [292] [Abeloid varieties over higher rank valued fields: Raynaud uniformization and Néron models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01354)
*Simon Kaib,Annette Werner*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 将Raynaud一致化推广到任意秩赋值环上的adelic空间，并证明半阿贝尔簇与abeloid簇的Néron模型存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Raynaud一致化与Néron模型理论通常在秩一非阿基米德域上建立，本文旨在推广到任意秩赋值环的高秩设定，并探讨高秩Raynaud扩张与形式Néron模型之间的联系。

Method: 在$\operatorname{Spa}(K,K^+)$（$K^+$为任意秩赋值环）上研究abeloid簇，推广Raynaud一致化；在代数与形式情形下构造合适的Néron模型，并结合有限可分基变换处理。

Result: 证明了：在分数域代数闭的赋值环上，半阿贝尔簇存在代数(lft-)Néron模型；在剩余域代数闭的完备秩一赋值环上，经有限可分基变换后亦存在；进而得到$\operatorname{Spa}(K,K^+)$上abeloid簇经有限可分基变换后的形式带标记Néron模型。

Conclusion: 高秩Raynaud一致化与形式Néron模型存在性得到统一处理，并揭示了两者之间的内在联系。
