Abstract: We study abeloid varieties over the adic space $\operatorname{Spa}(K, K^+)$, where $K$ is a complete non-Archimedean field and $K^+$ is a valuation ring of arbitrary rank. We extend the well-known Raynaud uniformization to this setting. We also show the existence of suitable Néron models in algebraic and formal situations over higher rank valuation rings: We prove the existence of algebraic (lft-)Néron models for semi-abelian varieties over valuation rings with algebraically closed fraction fields, as well as, up to finite separable base change, over complete rank one valuation rings with algebraically closed residue field. Building on this result, we show the existence of formal marked Néron models for abeloid varieties over $\operatorname{Spa}(K, K^+)$ after finite separable base change. We also investigate the connection between the higher rank Raynaud extension and the formal Néron model.

</details>


### [293] [Stability of representation cohomology sheaves on moduli spaces of bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01481)
*Jianping Wang,Xueqing Wen*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了 $\SU_C(n,L)$ 的切层是斜率稳定的，并对主丛模空间的切层/相关上同调层给出了更一般的稳定性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲线模空间上向量丛模空间的稳定性，推广到一般主丛模空间，且不假设模空间光滑。

Method: 对任意光滑复射影曲线及行列式，利用与表示相伴的上同调层替代切层，对连通约化群主丛模空间证明一般稳定性定理；几乎单群情形由伴随表示推出切层稳定性。

Result: $\SU_C(n,L)$ 的切层对 $g\geq2,n\geq2$ 及任意 $L$ 斜率稳定；$g\geq3$ 时主丛模空间相应上同调层稳定，几乎单群给出切层稳定。

Conclusion: 模空间可奇异，稳定性对任意丰沛 Cartier 极化成立。

Abstract: We prove that the tangent sheaf of $\SU_C(n,L)$ is slope stable for every smooth complex projective curve $C$ of genus $g\geq2$, every $n\geq2$, and every determinant $L$. For $g\geq3$, we prove a more general stability theorem on moduli spaces of principal bundles for connected reductive groups, using cohomology sheaves associated with representations in place of tangent sheaves. The adjoint representation gives tangent sheaf stability for the case of almost simple groups. The moduli spaces may be singular, and stability holds with respect to every ample Cartier polarization.

</details>


### [294] [Conifold factorization in topological recursion and Gromov--Witten theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01557)
*Juping Chen,Linji Chen,Bohan Fang,Zhengyu Zong*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了谱曲线在正则节点退化附近、允许对数谱坐标时的拓扑递归因子化定理：闭亏格 g≥2 的配分函数可分解为归一化配分函数、普适高斯真空与连通图求和的指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑递归在节点（conifold）退化附近的行为具有重要几何与物理意义；已有结果（如 conifold gap）多依赖特定模型或非对数坐标，缺少统一、可计算的因子化框架。

Method: 在谱曲线的正则节点退化附近建立拓扑递归的因子化定理，推广到对数谱坐标；将闭亏格 g≥2 配分函数分解为归一化配分函数、普适高斯真空以及连通图求和指数；用 remodeling 方法将高斯颈张量表述为参数化 P^1 的相对 Gromov–Witten 不变量。

Result: 图求和在消失周期上具有严格正阶，从而恢复 conifold gap；消失周期零次项等于归一化的自由能；正幂次系数可写成有限图公式。固定亏格时，正则级数在周期与节点轨迹参数上联合收敛。对局部 P^2、局部 F_0（conifold frame，背景分别为 C^3、resolved conifold）得到 Gromov–Witten 因子化；局部 F_1 对应 P^1 上 O(1)⊕O(-3) 背景。

Conclusion: 该因子化定理以有限图公式统一描述节点退化附近的拓扑递归，恢复并推广 conifold gap，并通过 remodeling 给出局部 P^2、F_0、F_1 的 Gromov–Witten 因子化，为相关计数不变量提供了可计算框架。

Abstract: We prove a factorization theorem for ordinary topological recursion near a regular nodal degeneration of a spectral curve, allowing logarithmic spectral coordinates. The partition function for closed genera $g\ge2$ factors into the partition function of the normalization, a universal Gaussian vacuum, and the exponential of a connected graph sum. This graph sum has strictly positive order in the vanishing period, and thus the factorization recovers the conifold gap, identifies the term of degree zero in the vanishing period with the free energy of the normalization, and gives finite graph formulas for the coefficients of positive powers of the period. At fixed genus, the regular series converges jointly in the period and the parameters along the nodal locus. The Gaussian neck tensors can be expressed in terms of relative Gromov--Witten invariants of a parametrized $\mathbb P^1$. As examples, we use remodeling to obtain Gromov--Witten factorizations for local $\mathbb P^2$ and local $\mathbb F_0$ in the conifold frame, with backgrounds $\mathbb C^3$ and the resolved conifold, respectively. The analogous local $\mathbb F_1$ factorization has background $\mathcal O(1)\oplus\mathcal O(-3)$ over $\mathbb P^1$.

</details>


### [295] [An explicit form of the standard conjecture of Hodge type for Hermitian varieties, with $p$-adic applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01731)
*Shushi Harashita*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文给出Hermitian簇中间上同调上相交形式正定性的初等证明，并额外得到关于格Λ的p进控制和Hodge型标准猜想的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dummigan、Dummigan–Tiep与Shimada已证明：偶数维有限域上非奇异Hermitian簇的极大全迷向线性子空间类张成中间上同调，且本原部分的相交形式正定，符合Hodge型标准猜想的预测。但原证明依赖有限酉群的表示论，作者希望给出不依赖表示论的初等证明，并进一步控制格Λ的p进性质。

Method: 仅使用Hermitian形式对应的对偶极图（dual polar graph），避免有限酉群的表示论。通过该图分析极大全迷向子空间类之间的相交数，从而证明本原部分上的相交形式是某个欧氏内积的显式正倍数。进一步利用该方法对格Λ进行p进分析。

Result: 证明本原部分相交形式为欧氏内积的显式正倍数，从而正定。若X定义在F_{q^2}上且维数为2m，则这些子空间相交矩阵的每个p进初等因子整除q^m。因此q^m倍任意余维m的代数循环都数值等价于这些类的整系数线性组合。

Conclusion: 作为副产品，Hodge型标准猜想对可被Hermitian簇有限覆盖的簇成立；该p进界可沿次数与p互素的有限态射下降。新证明同时简化了正定性论证并给出了p进控制。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a nonsingular Hermitian variety of even dimension over a finite field, and let $Λ$ be the lattice spanned by the classes of its maximal totally isotropic linear subspaces defined over the base field. By work of Dummigan, Dummigan--Tiep and Shimada, these classes span the middle cohomology, and the intersection form on the primitive part is definite, as predicted by the standard conjecture of Hodge type. We give an elementary proof, which avoids the representation theory of finite unitary groups and uses only the dual polar graph attached to the Hermitian form. It shows that the intersection form on the primitive part is an explicit positive multiple of a Euclidean inner product.
  The advantage of the new proof is that it also controls $Λ$ $p$-adically. If $X$ is defined over $\F_{q^2}$ and has dimension $2m$, every $p$-adic elementary divisor of the intersection matrix of these subspaces divides $q^m$. Consequently $q^m$ times every algebraic cycle of codimension $m$ is numerically equivalent to an integral combination of them. As a by-product, the standard conjecture of Hodge type holds for varieties finitely covered by Hermitian varieties, and the $p$-adic bound descends along finite morphisms of degree prime to $p$.

</details>


### [296] [Dihedral reflections and an infinite series of irrational Seshadri constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01783)
*Grzegorz Malara,Łukasz Merta,Justyna Szpond,Marcin Zieliński*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文将Laface-Ugaglia在九点爆破上构造的无理Seshadri常数机制推广到所有奇数n≥5，统一证明了P^1×P^1上特定线束在2n个一般点的Seshadri常数等于√((n-4)/n)，并在n≥7时给出P^2爆破上显式丰沛除子及平衡反射原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laface和Ugaglia通过二面体轨道、de Jonquières变换序列与沿(-4)-曲线（平衡法丛）的反射论证，在P^2九个很一般点爆破上构造了无理单点Seshadri常数。作者希望说明这一机制并非偶然，而是可统一推广到每个奇数n≥5，从而系统性地产生无理Seshadri常数并提炼其背后的抽象原理。

Method: 结合P^1×P^1上的二面体自由轨道、de Jonquières变换序列，以及沿具有平衡法丛的(-2a)-有理曲线的反射论证；抽象出平衡反射原理：当特殊纤维上某有理曲线的法丛为O(-a)^{⊕2}时，可沿该曲线反射nef类，并证明反射类在很一般纤维上仍为nef。

Result: 对n=2k+1，证明 ε(O_{P^1×P^1}(n-4,1);p_1,…,p_{2n})=√((n-4)/n) 在2n个很一般点处成立，且在固定2n阶二面体群的自由轨道上即成立。对每个奇数n≥7，经一次二次变换得到显式丰沛除子 L_n=(3n-4)H-nE_1-(n-2)(E_2+…+E_5)-4(E_6+…+E_{k+5})-2(E_{k+6}+E_{k+7})，满足L_n^2=4n(n-4)且 ε(L_n;x)=√(L_n^2)=2√(n(n-4))。

Conclusion: Laface-Ugaglia的无理Seshadri常数构造机制可统一推广到所有奇数n≥5：在P^1×P^1上给出显式的Seshadri常数公式，并对n≥7在P^2的k+7=(n+13)/2个很一般点爆破上构造出丰沛除子L_n，其单点Seshadri常数为2√(n(n-4))。此外提炼出平衡反射原理，为同类构造提供了一般性工具。

Abstract: Laface and Ugaglia recently constructed an irrational one-point Seshadri constant on the blow-up of $\mathbb{P}^2$ at nine very general points by combining a dihedral orbit on $\mathbb{P}^1\times\mathbb{P}^1$, a sequence of de Jonquières transformations, and a reflection argument along a $(-4)$-curve with balanced normal bundle. We show that the same mechanism extends uniformly to every odd integer $n\geq 5$. For $n=2k+1$ we prove $$ \varepsilon\bigl(\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1\times\mathbb{P}^1}(n-4,1);p_1,\ldots,p_{2n}\bigr)=\sqrt{\frac{n-4}{n}} $$ at $2n$ very general points, and already at a very general free orbit of a fixed dihedral group of order $2n$. For every odd $n\geq 7$ this produces an explicit ample line bundle on the blow-up of $\mathbb{P}^2$ at $k+7=(n+13)/2$ very general points whose one-point Seshadri constant at a very general point equals $$ 2\sqrt{n(n-4)}. $$ More precisely, after one quadratic transformation we obtain the ample divisor $$ L_n=(3n-4)H-nE_1-(n-2)(E_2+\cdots+E_5)-4(E_6+\cdots+E_{k+5})-2(E_{k+6}+E_{k+7}), $$ with $L_n^2=4n(n-4)$ and $\varepsilon(L_n;x)=\sqrt{L_n^2}$. We also isolate an abstract balanced-reflection principle underlying the construction: a nef class on a special fiber can be reflected across a rational curve of square $-2a$ whenever the curve has normal bundle $\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}^1}(-a)^{\oplus 2}$ in the total space, and the reflected class is nef on very general fibers.

</details>


### [297] [Euclidean Distance Optimization Within the Grassmannian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02018)
*Hannah Friedman,Serkan Hoşten,Andrea Rosana*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究Grassmannian子簇上的最近点优化问题，定义Euclidean distance (ED) degree与Grassmann distance (GD) degree，证明整个Grassmannian的ED判别式等于数据投影矩阵特征多项式的判别式，并给出若干几何有意义子簇的ED与GD次数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grassmannian子簇上的欧氏距离优化问题的临界点计数是一个重要的代数复杂度度量，但此前缺乏对ED判别式（临界点数改变的数据点集合）的整体刻画，也缺乏对具体有几何意义子簇的ED与GD次数计算。

Method: 从代数几何与代数优化视角出发，将数据点视为投影矩阵，利用矩阵特征多项式的判别式刻画整个Grassmannian的ED判别式；针对乘积型子Grassmannian、拟阵实现簇、Schubert簇等具体子簇，推导ED degree与GD degree的计数公式。

Result: 证明整个Grassmannian的ED判别式恰为数据（作为投影矩阵）特征多项式的判别式；给出乘积型小Grassmannian、拟阵实现簇、Schubert簇的ED degree与GD degree的计算公式。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Grassmannian子簇距离优化的代数判别式与次数理论，把临界点计数与矩阵特征多项式判别式联系起来，并为几类经典子簇提供了显式次数公式。

Abstract: Given a subvariety of the Grassmannian and a data point, we seek to find a point on the subvariety minimizing the Euclidean distance to the data point. The number of complex critical points of this optimization problem is the Euclidean distance (ED) degree. We show that the ED discriminant of the whole Grassmannian, that is, the set of data points with a number of critical points different than the ED degree, is the discriminant of the characteristic polynomial of the data as a projection matrix. Another closely connected algebraic complexity measure for a subvariety in the Grassmannian is the Grassmann distance (GD) degree, which is the number of complex critical points of the distance optimization problem when the data point itself is in the Grassmannian. We give formulae for ED and GD degrees of geometrically meaningful subvarieties of the Grassmannian, namely, products of smaller Grassmannians, matroid realization varieties, and Schubert varieties.

</details>


### [298] [The Nash manifold of four-point configurations modulo similarity subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02087)
*Bruce Olberding,Elaine A. Walker*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造并研究实平面内四点构型在相似变换群的Nash子群作用下的Nash模空间，证明四边形空间$\mathcal{Q}$是8维Nash流形且轨道空间$\mathcal{Q}/G$也是Nash流形；定义几何不变量"aspect"（取值于$[-1,1]$），并证明在$(-1,0)$与$(0,1)$上$\mathcal{Q}/G$的部分Nash微分同胚于区间与固定纤维的乘积，从而将非例外模问题化归为两个模型纤维的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四点构型模空间在退化情形会丢失极限直线数据，而使用"四边形"这一更精细的概念可保留退化时的直线信息；同时希望以Nash（半代数/实解析）范畴的框架来严格建立模空间的流形结构，并寻找可将模问题分解的几何不变量。

Method: 以quadrangle取代四点构型，构造实平面中四点构型在相似变换群Nash子群作用下的空间；证明quadrangle空间$\mathcal{Q}$为8维Nash流形，并对任意Nash子群$G$证明轨道空间$\mathcal{Q}/G$为Nash流形；引入取值于$[-1,1]$的几何不变量aspect，并在两个开区间上对轨道空间作乘积分解。

Result: 证明了$\mathcal{Q}$是8维Nash流形，且对相似群的任意Nash子群$G$，$\mathcal{Q}/G$是Nash流形；在$(-1,0)$和$(0,1)$上，$\mathcal{Q}/G$的相应部分Nash微分同胚于该区间与某一固定纤维的乘积。

Conclusion: 非例外的模问题可简化为对两个模型纤维的分析，且这两个纤维在四边形本身的意义下具有自然的几何解释。

Abstract: We construct and study the Nash manifold of four-point configurations in the real plane modulo the action of a Nash subgroup of the group of similarity transformations. We do so by using the finer notion of a quadrangle in place of that of a four-point configuration, since this retains limiting line data in degenerations. We prove that the space ${\mathcal Q}$ of quadrangles is an 8-dimensional Nash manifold and that, for every Nash subgroup $G$ of the similarity group, the orbit space ${\mathcal Q}/G$ is a Nash manifold. We define a geometric invariant, called aspect, with values in $[-1,1]$, and prove that, over each of the intervals $(-1,0)$ and $(0,1)$, the corresponding part of ${\mathcal Q}/G$ is Nash diffeomorphic to the product of the interval with a fixed fiber. Thus the nonexceptional part of the moduli problem reduces to the analysis of two model fibers, each having a natural geometric interpretation in terms of the quadrangles themselves.

</details>


### [299] [Higher-Page Jacobian and Albanese Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02109)
*Dan Popovici,Luis Ugarte*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文用Hodge理论方法，对两类紧复流形（page-(r-1)-∂∂̄流形与E_r-sGG流形）构造E_r-Jacobian环面、E_r-Albanese环面及E_r-Albanese映射，并发展相应Hodge理论，进而给出结构定理、代数维数恒等式，以及关于6维球面复结构（假设或新近声称存在）的上同调与度量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Albanese映射与Jacobian环面依赖于∂∂̄引理等Hodge理论条件，适用范围受限；作者希望将这一理论推广到更一般的非Kähler复流形类（page-(r-1)-∂∂̄与E_r-sGG），以便在更广情形下研究复结构、代数维数与度量性质，特别是处理6维球面这类可能存在复结构的情形。

Method: 采用Hodge理论方法：针对page-(r-1)-∂∂̄流形与E_r-sGG流形（前者包含于后者）构造E_r-Jacobian环面、E_r-Albanese环面与E_r-Albanese映射；并为E_r-sGG流形发展一套Hodge理论，作为构造与应用的框架。

Result: 成功构造了E_r-Jacobian环面、E_r-Albanese环面与E_r-Albanese映射，并建立了E_r-sGG流形的Hodge理论；应用这些结果得到结构定理与用E_r-Albanese映射、环面表达的代数维数恒等式；对6维球面，在假设复结构情形及新近声称存在的复结构情形下，得到上同调与度量定理，例如后一情形中S^6上不存在强Gauduchon度量。

Conclusion: 本文推广了Albanese理论至page-(r-1)-∂∂̄与E_r-sGG复流形，并借助新发展的Hodge理论获得结构定理与代数维数恒等式；对S^6复结构问题的应用表明，若文献中声称的复结构存在，则S^6上不存在强Gauduchon度量，为排除此类复结构提供了新的上同调与度量判据。

Abstract: We construct, through Hodge-theoretical methods, what we call the $E_r$-Jacobian torus, the $E_r$-Albanese torus and the $E_r$-Albanese map of any compact complex manifold that is either {\it page-(r-1)-$\partial\bar\partial$} or {\it $E_r$-sGG}. These two classes of manifolds, the former of which is contained in the latter, have been introduced recently by both authors jointly with J. Stelzig, respectively by the first-named author. A Hodge theory is also developed for the latter class of manifolds. We then apply our results to give structure theorems and an identity of algebraic dimensions in terms of the $E_r$-Albanese map and torus. Other applications result in cohomological and metrical theorems for the $6$-dimensional sphere when it is equipped either with a hypothetical complex structure or with the complex structures very recently claimed to exist in the literature. For example, we show that in the latter case no {\it strongly Gauduchon} metric exists on $S^6$.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [Detecting Einstein metrics on products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00066)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 \(S^5\times S^2\)、\(S^5\times S^3\)、\(S^5\times S^4\)、\(S^7\times S^2\) 上构造无穷多个两两非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量，且除有限个例外均非乘积或经典两和 Böhm 度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在突破经典两和 Böhm 度量与 Riemannian 乘积的限制，在乘积流形上获得大量新的 Einstein 度量，需克服闭路条件中高度振荡的 shooting 映射难以直接追踪的困难。

Method: 从某一奇异轨道处两个非塌缩半径很小的解出发，放大该区域使 Einstein 方程趋近 Ricci 平坦问题，其解以衰减振荡趋向锥；从 Ricci 平坦极限中提取两个响应量：一个确定共同振荡相位，去除相位后第二个闭路条件简化为标量符号变化，再用一致内外匹配论证将极限符号变化转移到精确 Einstein 方程。

Result: 度量形如 \(dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}\)（\(p,q\ge2,\ 2p+q\le8\)）；随初始尺度趋于零，相位可任意多圈旋转，闭路条件因而被无穷多次满足，构造出无穷多个两两非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量。

Conclusion: 通过 Ricci 平坦极限中的相位与符号分析及一致匹配，可在上述四类乘积流形上系统性地产生无穷多个本质不同的 Einstein 度量，拓展了已知 Einstein 度量的构造范围。

Abstract: On \[ S^5\times S^2,\qquad S^5\times S^3,\qquad S^5\times S^4,\qquad S^7\times S^2, \] we construct infinitely many pairwise non-isometric real-analytic Einstein metrics. After discarding finitely many members, none is isometric or homothetic to a Riemannian product or to a classical two-summand Böhm metric. The metrics have the form \[ dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}, \qquad p,q\ge2,\qquad 2p+q\le8. \]
  The construction starts with solutions for which the two non-collapsing radii at one singular orbit are very small. After magnifying this region, the Einstein equations approach a Ricci-flat problem whose solutions tend to a cone by a decaying oscillation. The main point is to determine exactly which part of this oscillation can still be seen by the closing conditions at the other end of the manifold. Rather than following the full, increasingly oscillatory shooting map, we extract two response quantities from the Ricci-flat limit. One determines the common oscillatory phase; after that phase has been removed, the second closing condition is reduced to a scalar sign change. A uniform inner--outer matching argument transfers this limiting sign change to the exact Einstein equations. As the initial scale tends to zero, the phase makes arbitrarily many turns, and the closing conditions are therefore met infinitely many times.

</details>


### [301] [Gradient Ricci Solitons on PTR-Submanifolds of Complex Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00077)
*Jae Won Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究复空间形式中PTR子流形上的梯度Ricci孤立子，建立Gauss–孤立子方程重构切向Kähler算子平方，给出谱编码、λ上界、刚性及不存在性等结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究复空间形式中部分全实（PTR）子流形上梯度Ricci孤立子的结构与谱性质，将外在几何量与内在孤立子数据联系，并确定曲率、极小性与相对零化条件对孤立子常数与刚性的影响。

Method: 在非零环境全纯截曲率下推导Gauss–孤立子方程，用Hessian自同态与外在Ricci算子重构切向Kähler算子平方P²；结合第二基本形式刻画PTR切向谱；对紧致极小PTR孤立子估计λ；证明紧致平凡性判据、无迹各向异性恒等式、平行数据刚性与紧致实超曲面不存在性；用平坦多级Gaussian模型与非平坦Segre CR乘积作一致性检验。

Result: PTR切向谱由内在孤立子数据与第二基本形式共同编码；正环境曲率下紧致极小PTR孤立子满足λ≤c(n-1)/4，并在相对零化条件下可严格化；获得紧致平凡性判据、精确的无迹各向异性恒等式、平行数据刚性结果，以及紧致实超曲面的不存在性定理；两个显式模型验证了重构公式并识别其极限情形。

Conclusion: Gauss–孤立子方程为PTR孤立子提供了统一重构框架，将切向谱与外在几何耦合；所得λ界、刚性、不存在性与显式模型共同刻画了复空间形式中PTR孤立子的限制条件与极限行为。

Abstract: We study gradient Ricci solitons on partially totally real (PTR) submanifolds of complex space forms. In nonzero ambient holomorphic sectional curvature, the Gauss--soliton equation reconstructs the square $P^2$ of the tangential Kähler operator from the Hessian endomorphism and an extrinsic Ricci operator. Consequently, the PTR tangency spectrum is encoded by intrinsic soliton data together with the second fundamental form. For compact minimal PTR solitons with positive ambient curvature we obtain $λ\le c(n-1)/4$, with a strict version under a relative-nullity condition. We also prove compact triviality criteria, an exact trace-free anisotropy identity, a parallel-data rigidity result, and a nonexistence theorem for compact real hypersurfaces. A flat multi-level Gaussian model and a nonflat Segre $CR$-product provide explicit consistency checks and identify the limiting cases of the reconstruction formulas.

</details>


### [302] [A Quantitative Reeb-Form Obstruction for Real Hypersurfaces of Strict Nearly Kähler Six-Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00078)
*Jae Won Lee,Chul Woo Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在六维严格近Kähler流形的五维定向实超曲面上，证明Reeb形式的导数(dη)_p在每一点非零，从而排除任何包含dη=0的局部诱导近切触结构，并给出Yano半对称度量联络的Ricci张量非对称性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究六维严格近Kähler流形中超曲面的诱导Reeb结构性质。此前关于此类超曲面的诱导近切触结构分类常涉及dη=0的条件，需要判定该条件在此几何设置下是否可能成立，从而澄清相关近切触类的存在性。

Method: 方法为局部论证：利用超曲面恒等式∇_Xξ=φAX−G(X,N)与六维严格近Kähler代数。假设(dη)_p=0，导出φA+Aφ=2G_N，并说明其迫使Hopf条件。随后在接触超平面D_p=kerη_p上分析：φA+Aφ与φ可交换，而G_N与φ反交换且满足G_N^2=−αI_{D_p}(α>0)，从而得出矛盾。

Result: 证明了逐点障碍(dη)_p≠0对每个p∈M成立；由此排除任何定义条件包含dη=0的局部诱导近切触类。作为联络论推论，对与ξ关联的标准Yano半对称度量联络∇̂_XY=∇_XY+η(Y)X−g(X,Y)ξ，在五维情形有Skew Ric^{∇̂}=−(3/2)dη，因而Ric^{∇̂}处处非对称。并在标准齐性严格近Kähler空间上给出例证。

Conclusion: 六维严格近Kähler流形的五维实超曲面上诱导Reeb形式必处处非退化（dη≠0），不存在dη=0的局部诱导近切触结构；此障碍进一步导致Yano半对称度量联络的Ricci张量无处对称。

Abstract: Let $M^5$ be an oriented real hypersurface of a six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler manifold $(\widetilde M^6,\widetilde g,J)$, and let $ξ=-JN$ and $η=g(\,\cdot\,,ξ)$ denote the induced Reeb field and Reeb one-form. We prove the pointwise obstruction \[ (dη)_p\neq0\qquad\text{for every }p\in M. \] The proof is local and uses only the hypersurface identity \[ \nabla_Xξ=φAX-G(X,N) \] together with the six-dimensional strict nearly Kähler algebra. If $(dη)_p=0$, then \[ φA+Aφ=2G_N,\qquad G_NX=G(X,N), \] which forces the Hopf condition. On the contact hyperplane $\mathcal D_p=\kerη_p$, the endomorphism $φA+Aφ$ then commutes with $φ$, whereas $G_N$ anti-commutes with $φ$ and satisfies \[ G_N^2=-αI_{\mathcal D_p},\qquad α>0. \] This contradiction proves the obstruction. As a connection-theoretic consequence, for the standard Yano semi-symmetric metric connection associated with $ξ$, \[ \widehat\nabla_XY=\nabla_XY+η(Y)X-g(X,Y)ξ, \] one has in dimension five \[ \operatorname{Skew}\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}=-\frac32\,dη, \] and hence $\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}$ is nowhere symmetric. The obstruction also rules out any local induced almost contact class whose defining conditions include $dη=0$. We illustrate the result on the standard homogeneous strict nearly Kähler spaces.

</details>


### [303] [A counterexample to Yau's bounded-mean-curvature embedding question](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00153)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了Yau关于有界Ricci曲率与正单射半径流形等距嵌入问题的反例：在ℝ⁴上构造满足有界Ricci曲率、正单射半径但全曲率无界的完备光滑度量，从而任何C²等距浸入欧氏空间均必有平均曲率无界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau曾提问：每个具有有界Ricci曲率和正单射半径的完备黎曼流形是否都能以有界平均曲率等距嵌入到某个欧氏空间中。该问题将内蕴曲率控制与外在嵌入的几何量（平均曲率）联系起来，具有重要理论意义。本文旨在通过构造反例来否定这一猜想。

Method: 在ℝ⁴上通过拼接互不相交的曲率尖峰构造光滑完备度量，使各尖峰的Ricci缩并保持一致有界；结合短测地曲率估计与Jacobi方程证明单射半径有一致正下界；再利用Gauss方程论证全曲率无界将迫使任何C²等距浸入的平均曲率无界。该构造可推广到所有维数≥4，且在四维时Ricci曲率可趋于无穷远处为零。

Result: 构造出ℝ⁴上的光滑完备度量，满足Ricci曲率有界、单射半径为正，但全曲率无界；由此证明该度量到有限维欧氏空间的任何C²等距浸入均具有无界平均曲率。构造在维数≥4均成立，四维情形下Ricci曲率还可选为在无穷远处趋于零。

Conclusion: 该反例否定了Yau的猜想：有界Ricci曲率与正单射半径不足以保证存在有界平均曲率的等距嵌入。结果表明需要更强的内蕴曲率控制（如全曲率有界）才能约束外在平均曲率。

Abstract: Yau asked whether every complete Riemannian manifold with bounded Ricci curvature and positive injectivity radius admits an isometric embedding into some Euclidean space with bounded mean curvature. We construct a counterexample: a smooth complete metric on $\mathbb{R}^4$ with bounded Ricci curvature, positive injectivity radius, and unbounded full curvature. The Gauss equation then forces every $C^2$ isometric immersion of this metric into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space to have unbounded mean curvature. The metric is assembled from disjoint curvature peaks whose Ricci contractions stay uniformly bounded. A short-geodesic curvature estimate and the Jacobi equation give the uniform injectivity-radius bound. The construction works in every dimension at least four, and in dimension four the Ricci curvature may be chosen to tend to zero at infinity.

</details>


### [304] [Minimality and Nonlinear Spectral Rigidity of Hypersurfaces in Real Space Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00214)
*Zihao Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究欧氏、球面与双曲空间中超曲面的非线性平均曲率谱方程，证明当谱参数σ≥nc时解极小，σ<nc时在梯度点处有严格平均曲率界，并给出脐性模型的尖锐性及若干局部刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一处理三类常曲率空间中的非线性平均曲率型谱方程，推广并证明Chen猜想（欧氏余维一）及常负曲率下的双调和极小性，摆脱完备性与全纯性假设，并刻画谱极小范围与模型刚性。

Method: 对容许解析响应F与环境截面曲率c，采用统一的实解析延拓与焦点迹（focal-trace）论证；结合脐性显式模型构造；对紧非极小测地球与等半径Clifford超曲面使用定量局部刚性分析。

Result: 证明σ≥nc时极小性，σ<nc时梯度点处严格平均曲率界；该类包含所有幂F(t)=t^{p-1}(p>1)及满足显式宽度条件的正混合；由此推出Chen猜想与负常曲率双调和极小性；脐性模型显示无条件谱极小范围的尖锐性；并证明三类几何中紧非极小测地球及球面中真等半径Clifford超曲面在环境等距意义下的定量局部刚性。

Conclusion: 建立了一个跨三种常曲率空间的统一谱极小性与刚性框架，给出尖锐范围并强化了经典猜想，同时为超曲面谱方程研究提供了可推广的实解析延拓方法。

Abstract: We study nonlinear mean-curvature spectral equations for hypersurfaces in Euclidean, spherical and hyperbolic space. For an admissible analytic response $F$ and ambient sectional curvature $c$, we prove minimality when the spectral parameter satisfies $σ\ge nc$, and strict mean-curvature bounds at gradient points when $σ<nc$. The class includes every power $F(t)=t^{p-1}$, $p>1$, and positive mixtures satisfying an explicit width condition. In particular, the result proves Chen's conjecture in Euclidean codimension one and biharmonic minimality in constant negative curvature, without completeness or holonomicity. A common real-analytic continuation and focal-trace argument treats all three geometries. Explicit umbilical models show that the unconditional spectral minimality range is sharp. Independently, we prove quantitative local rigidity, modulo ambient isometries, of compact nonminimal geodesic spheres in all three geometries and proper equal-radius Clifford hypersurfaces in spheres, for smooth laws with $F,F'>0$ near the model amplitude.

</details>


### [305] [Minimal equatorial fillings of low index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00247)
*Jacob Bernstein,Daniel Ketover*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中极小赤道填充的几何与拓扑，在最低可能 Morse 指标下给出限制条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极小曲面填充问题中，填充的拓扑与几何受边界与指标约束，最低 Morse 指标情形是理解该类极小填充结构的基础。

Method: 利用极小曲面理论中的 Morse 指标分析与稳定性方法，考察 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中赤道边界对应的极小填充。

Result: 得到最低可能 Morse 指标的极小赤道填充在几何与拓扑上的限制。

Conclusion: 最低指标条件对 $\mathbb{S}^3$ 中极小赤道填充的几何与拓扑形成强约束。

Abstract: We obtain restrictions on the geometry and topology of minimal equatorial fillings in $\mathbb{S}^3$ of the lowest possible Morse index.

</details>


### [306] [An exact hypergeometric Weierstrass representation for Smyth's free-boundary minimal disk in the regular tetrahedron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00434)
*Steven Finch*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出正则四面体内Smyth所证三张嵌入极小圆盘的显式Weierstrass数据，其Gauss映射为角(π/4, π/2, πβ/2)的Schwarz三角函数，无辅助参数且无自由参数，并证明数据非代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smyth(1984)证明任意四面体T包含三张与边界正交的嵌入极小圆盘，但仅在一般存在性层面；对于正则四面体这一高度对称的特例，其对应的极小曲面显式解析数据（Weierstrass表示）此前未知，值得精确求解。

Method: 将问题化为斜四边形Q上的Plateau问题的共轭曲面；对正则四面体，利用对称性导出Weierstrass数据，其中Gauss映射取角为(π/4, π/2, πβ/2)的圆弧三角Schwarz三角函数，β=arccos(-1/3)/π；通过超几何方程及单值群分析确定参数。

Result: 超几何参数关于β线性，不存在辅助参数与自由参数；给出κ的Γ函数闭式、g′的Wronskian闭式、精确总曲率2π(2β-1)、数据的对称平方结构；证明因β/2无理，投影单值群稠密故数据不可为代数。数值上表示精度达28位，Area/ℓ²=0.2172341554075574483760009126…，验证了Brakke 2013年Surface Evolver计算。

Conclusion: 正则四面体内的Smyth极小圆盘具有完全显式、刚性且非代数的Weierstrass数据，总曲率由β精确给出，高精度数值与先前计算一致，深化了对该经典极小曲面族的解析理解。

Abstract: Smyth (1984) proved that every tetrahedron $T\subset\mathbb{R}^3$ contains three embedded minimal disks meeting $\partial T$ orthogonally, obtained as conjugate surfaces of Plateau solutions in a skew quadrilateral $Q$ whose edges are $c_iν_i$, where $\varphi_1,\varphi_2,\varphi_3,\varphi_4$ are faces of $T$, $c_i=\operatorname{Area}(\varphi_i)$ and $ν_i$ are outward normal vectors. For the $\textit{regular}$ tetrahedron we give the Weierstrass data explicitly. The Gauss map is a Schwarz triangle function for a circular-arc triangle with angles $(π/4,\ π/2,\ πβ/2)$, where $β=\arccos(-1/3)/π$; the hypergeometric parameters are linear in $β$ and there is $\textit{no accessory parameter and no free parameter}$. We record a closed form for $κ$ in $Γ$-values, a Wronskian closed form for $g'$, the exact total curvature $2π(2β-1)$, the symmetric-square structure of the data, and a rigidity statement: since $β/2$ is irrational, the projective monodromy is dense and the data $\textit{cannot}$ be algebraic. Numerically the representation extends to 28 digits -- $\operatorname{Area}/\ell^{2}=0.2172341554075574483760009126\ldots$ -- confirming a 2013 Surface Evolver computation of Ken Brakke.

</details>


### [307] [The Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00625)
*André Magalhães de Sá Gomes,Christian S. Rodrigues,Luiz A. B. San Martin*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明圆周的Wasserstein空间$W_2(S^1)$不是平坦的：其截面曲率由Fourier系数上的辛形式控制，非迷向平面平坦，n阶模平面曲率上界为$3n^2$（在均匀测度处取等），因而是无界的。这纠正了"底流形一维则Wasserstein空间平坦"的普遍预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实数轴与区间的Wasserstein空间是平坦的，人们据此普遍预期"底流形一维则Wasserstein空间平坦"。本文旨在检验并纠正这一错误预期，转而研究圆周$S^1$的Wasserstein空间$W_2(S^1)$的曲率几何。

Method: 在Lott所观察到的、由流形$M$的Laplacian特征函数诱导的$P^\infty(M)$切丛整体标架下进行计算。证明该标架是每个切空间的Schauder基，并在其中写出Otto度量和Levi-Civita联络。对圆周情形给出$P^\infty(S^1)$度量和联络系数的闭形式表示（密度的Fourier系数的有限组合），并直接从$W_2$定义验证曲率结果。

Result: 圆周Wasserstein空间的截面曲率由Fourier系数上的辛形式决定：平面平坦当且仅当其为该辛形式的迷向平面；在n阶Fourier模张成的平面上曲率上界为$3n^2$，等号仅在均匀测度处成立，故曲率无界。在Otto形式Riemann结构内算出的结果与直接从$W_2$定义得到的结论一致，并表明$P_2(S^1)$中均匀测度的任何邻域都不是CAT(0)。

Conclusion: 圆周Wasserstein空间非平坦，其截面曲率无界且由辛形式刻画，纠正了"一维底流形对应平坦Wasserstein空间"的普遍说法。所建立的特征函数标架适用于更一般流形，并可应用于动力学计算：将扩张圆映射在其不变测度处的push-forward作用导数读作Fourier频率上的加权移位。

Abstract: We show that the Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat, in contrast with the flatness of the Wasserstein spaces of the real line and of an interval. Its sectional curvature is governed by a symplectic form on the Fourier coefficients, so that a plane is flat exactly when it is isotropic for that form, and on the plane spanned by the $n$-th Fourier modes the curvature is bounded above by $3n^2$, with equality exactly at the uniform measure. It is therefore unbounded. We compute it inside the formal Riemannian structure of Otto and confirm it directly from the definition of $W_2$, which also shows that no neighbourhood of the uniform measure in $P_2(S^1)$ is CAT(0). The result corrects the widely stated expectation that a Wasserstein space is flat whenever its base manifold is one-dimensional.
  The computation is carried out in the global frame of the tangent bundle of $P^\infty(M)$ induced by the eigenfunctions of the Laplacian of $M$, whose existence was observed by Lott. We prove that it is a Schauder basis of every tangent space and write Otto's metric and its Levi-Civita connection in it. On the circle this gives the metric and connection coefficients of $P^\infty(S^1)$ in closed form, as finite combinations of the Fourier coefficients of the density. A final section puts the frame to work on a computation from dynamics, reading the derivative of the push-forward action of an expanding circle map at its invariant measure as a weighted shift on the Fourier frequencies.

</details>


### [308] [The Heat Kernel of the Hodge Laplacian on an Invariant Subspace of 1-forms on the Hyperbolic Space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00653)
*Petros Siasos*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了实双曲空间 $\mathbb{H}^n$ 上 Hodge Laplacian 限制在 1-形式不变子空间上的热核显式公式，并建立跨维递推关系、一致估计与长时间渐近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1-形式上的热核（相对于标量情形）缺乏显式表达与精确估计，而该对象在解析、几何分析及拓扑/指标问题中具有基础意义。作者希望将经典的标量热核界推广到实双曲空间上的 1-形式。

Method: 利用 warped product（扭曲积）分解，将问题化归为特定子空间上的分量算子；对奇、偶维分别求解，得到闭式热核表达式；并建立 Millson 型跨维递推关系。

Result: 得到了奇偶维数下 1-形式热核的精确闭式公式；证明连接不同维数热核的 Millson 型递推关系；基于显式表示导出尖锐一致估计与长时间渐近行为。

Conclusion: 该工作把经典标量热核界成功推广到实双曲空间 1-形式，并为相关谱分析与几何应用提供可计算的显式工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct explicit formulas for the heat kernel of the Hodge Laplacian restricted to an invariant subspace of $1$-forms on the real hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$. Using warped product decompositions, the problem is reduced to component operators on specific subspaces, yielding precise closed-form expressions for both odd and even dimensions. We further establish Millson-type recurrence relations that connect heat kernels across differing dimensions. Based on these explicit representations, we derive sharp uniform estimates and long-time asymptotic behavior, extending classical scalar heat kernel bounds to $1$-forms on real hyperbolic spaces.

</details>


### [309] [Strict Log-Concavity of First Dirichlet Eigenfunctions under Symmetry in $\mathbb{CP}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00716)
*Jia-Lin Hsu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了在 $\mathbb{CP}^n$（$n=2,3,4$）中，边界凸的光滑区域上，关于标准 $U(1)\times U(n-1)$ 作用不变的 Dirichlet 第一正特征函数是严格对数凹的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数凹性（log-concavity）是特征函数的重要几何凸性性质，在欧氏空间已有经典结果（如 Brascamp–Lieb 理论），但在复射影空间等非负曲率流形上尚缺乏对应结论。本文旨在将这一性质推广到复射影空间中的凸区域，以理解曲率与特征函数凸性的关系。

Method: 针对 $\mathbb{CP}^n$ 中边界凸（convex boundary）的光滑区域，考虑其 Dirichlet 第一特征函数；在该函数满足标准 $U(1)\times U(n-1)$ 对称（不变）的假设下，利用对称性将问题约化为低维分析，并结合对数凹性的微分判据进行证明，覆盖维数 $n=2,3,4$。

Result: 证明了在所设条件下（边界凸、$U(1)\times U(n-1)$ 不变），第一 Dirichlet 正特征函数对 $n=2,3,4$ 是严格对数凹的。

Conclusion: 结果建立了复射影空间中对称凸区域上第一特征函数的严格对数凹性，将经典的欧氏对数凹性结果部分推广到正曲率复流形，同时维度限制（$n\le4$）提示高维情形仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: We prove that the positive first Dirichlet eigenfunction of every smooth domain with convex boundary in $\mathbb{CP}^n$ invariant under the standard $U(1)\times U(n-1)$-action is strictly log-concave for $n=2,3,4$.

</details>


### [310] [On the Bryant conjecture for minimal hypersurfaces in $S^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00886)
*Chao Qian,Ya Tao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了S^4中具有常数量曲率和三个不同主曲率的局部极小超曲面局部等距于极小Cartan等参超曲面，肯定回答了Bryant猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Bryant猜想：刻画S^4中常数量曲率、每点三个不同主曲率的局部极小超曲面是否为极小Cartan等参超曲面。

Method: 证明对每个固定S_0>3，满足|A|^2≡S_0的归一化局部图解的第四阶射流集合\mathscr{G}_{S_0}是紧的，其中A为形状算子。

Result: 建立了\mathscr{G}_{S_0}（S_0>3固定）的紧性，从而得到该类超曲面的刚性结论。

Conclusion: 该局部极小超曲面必须局部等距于S^4中的极小Cartan等参超曲面，Bryant猜想成立。

Abstract: We prove that a local minimal hypersurface $M^3$ in the unit sphere $S^4$ with constant scalar curvature and three distinct principal curvatures at every point must be locally congruent to the minimal Cartan isoparametric hypersurface in $S^4$, which provides a positive answer to the Bryant conjecture \cite{ChangLocal}. The key ingredient is to prove that, for each fixed \(S_0>3\), the set $\mathscr{G}_{S_0}$ of fourth-order jets of normalized local graph solutions satisfying \(|A|^2\equiv S_0\) is compact, where \(A\) denotes the shape operator.

</details>


### [311] [Monge-Ampère degenerations and conifold contractions in Fujiki class $\mathcal{C}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00942)
*Riley Guyett,Ryan Mc Gowan*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在小消解情形下研究Calabi-Yau簇上度量的退化与收敛性，建立了例外轨迹之外的统一估计，并在三维时给出了Gromov-Hausdorff收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望推广Song关于Candelas-de la Ossa猜想的工作，理解具有普通双重点与小型消解（small crepant resolution）的Calabi-Yau簇上Ricci-平坦度量在退化区域的行为。

Method: 设Y为带普通双重点的Q-Gorenstein正规射影Calabi-Yau簇，π: X→Y为小消解且X属于Fujiki类C。通过分析给定度量在例外轨迹附近的退化行为，在远离例外轨迹的紧子集上建立一致估计（uniform estimates）。

Result: 在例外轨迹之外建立了度量的统一估计；当Y为三维时，证明这些度量在Gromov-Hausdorff意义下收敛到Y正则轨迹上度量的完备化（metric completion）。

Conclusion: 结果推广了Song关于Candelas-de la Ossa猜想的工作，刻画了小消解Calabi-Yau簇在例外轨迹附近的度量退化与极限空间结构。

Abstract: Given a $\mathbb{Q}$-Gorenstein normal projective Calabi-Yau variety $Y$ with ordinary double points as singularities and a small crepant resolution $π\colon X\to Y$ with $X$ in the Fujiki class $\mathcal{C}$, we establish uniform estimates away from the exceptional locus where the given metrics may degenerate. When $Y$ is a threefold, we prove that these metrics also converge in a Gromov-Hausdorff sense to the metric completion of $Y$ on the regular locus. In particular, our results extend the work of Song on a conjecture of Candelas and de la Ossa.

</details>


### [312] [Parabolic Monge-Ampère equations on a family of Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01202)
*Jiyuan Han*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了抛物型Monge-Ampère方程的一致$L^\infty$估计，并将其应用于穿孔子盘上一族极化Calabi-Yau流形上的Kähler-Ricci流，得到了沿着流的Skoda估计、直径界和Gromov-Hausdorff预紧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究穿孔子盘上一族极化Calabi-Yau流形上的Kähler-Ricci流，希望获得沿流的均匀估计以及Gromov-Hausdorff紧性，为Li-Tosatti的直径估计提供替代证明。

Method: 证明抛物型Monge-Ampère方程在关于初始与给定测度的一致Skoda估计下的一致$L^\infty$估计；将该估计应用于Kähler-Ricci流，并通过插值论证得到沿流的均匀Skoda估计。

Result: 得到抛物型Monge-Ampère方程的一致$L^\infty$估计；沿Kähler-Ricci流的均匀Skoda估计；该族流的一致直径界与Gromov-Hausdorff预紧性；并由此给出Li-Tosatti直径估计的替代证明。

Conclusion: 该工作将一致Skoda估计与抛物型Monge-Ampère方程的正则性联系起来，为Calabi-Yau流形上Kähler-Ricci流的均匀几何估计与紧性提供了新途径。

Abstract: We prove a uniform $L^\infty$-estimate for the parabolic Monge--Ampère equation under uniform Skoda estimates with respect to the initial and prescribed measures. We apply this estimate to Kähler--Ricci flows on a family of polarized Calabi--Yau manifolds over the punctured disk. An interpolation argument gives uniform Skoda estimates along the flows. We also establish uniform diameter bounds and Gromov--Hausdorff precompactness for this family of flows. In particular, we obtain an alternative proof of the diameter estimate by Li-Tosatti.

</details>


### [313] [Algebraic limits of Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections on Kähler surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01221)
*Keshu Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文用几何方法统一建立Kähler曲面上有界能量Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络的Hörmander $L^2$估计，将光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛实现为解析平坦族内的代数收敛，并给出可光滑族的连续性与固定拓扑下$\mu$-半稳定层的有限性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hörmander $L^2$估计、Uhlenbeck收敛与代数收敛之间缺乏统一框架；同时模空间问题需要连续性、有限性等基础性结果，以刻画半稳定层的几何与分析联系。

Method: 发展一套几何方式，对Kähler曲面上有界能量的Hermitian-Yang-Mills联络一致地执行Hörmander $L^2$估计；并借助解析平坦族，把光滑HYM联络的Uhlenbeck收敛刻画为其中的代数收敛。

Result: 得到光滑HYM联络Uhlenbeck收敛的代数实现；证明可光滑族的连续性定理，以及固定拓扑下$\mu$-半稳定层的有限性结果。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了分析估计与代数收敛视角，为模空间问题提供潜在应用。

Abstract: We develop a geometric way to uniformly carry out Hörmander's $L^2$-estimates for Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections with bounded energy on Kähler surfaces. We then show the Uhlenbeck convergence of smooth HYM connections on Kähler surfaces can be realized as algebraic convergence inside an analytic flat family. As applications, we prove a continuity theorem for smoothable families and a boundedness result for $μ$-semistable sheaves with fixed topology. We also discuss potential applications for the moduli problem.

</details>


### [314] [Riemannian Structures on Quotients in Elastic Diffeology via Q-Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01346)
*Yusuke Shiobara*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究了Q-chart（Blohmann弹性沿其下降的子浸没）上的黎曼结构下降问题，证明了弹性微分几何的整套黎曼机器沿Q-chart双向保持，并应用于无理环面完全刻画其黎曼几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Miyamoto引入了Q-chart并证明Blohmann弹性可沿其下降，但弹性微分几何中的黎曼结构（各类联络、度量、测地线等）是否也能沿Q-chart下降尚不清楚。作者希望将这一框架系统化并推广到商空间情形。

Method: 利用迭代切丛T^nX及其纤维积T_kX是Y上对应丛沿φ的拉回这一关键观察，建立Y上结构X上Ψ(φ)-不变结构之间的双射，并验证无挠性、有效性、平坦性、满性、Levi-Civita性质及测地线方程在双向保持。对微分上离散群主作用的商，整体提升测地线并证明弹性空间类在该商下封闭以及完备测地流下降。

Result: 证明黎曼结构沿Q-chart双向保持并由Ψ(φ)-不变性刻画；商情形下可整体提升测地线、完备测地流下降；完全确定无理环面Tor_α的黎曼几何：每个黎曼度量为ϑ²的正常数倍，垂直联络构成单参数平坦有效联络族，其中唯一成员为所有度量的Levi-Civita联络，该联络测地流完备但诱导伪距离恒为零。

Conclusion: Q-chart是弹性黎曼几何下降的有效框架，其上的黎曼结构由Ψ(φ)-不变性完全刻画；商与无理环面的例子显示弹性框架能揭示度量、联络与伪距离之间不同于经典黎曼几何的现象。

Abstract: Following Miyamoto, we consider Q-charts, a class of subductions $φ\colon X\to Y$ of diffeological spaces along which Blohmann's elasticity descends. We show that the Riemannian apparatus of elastic diffeology---vertical, horizontal and full connections, Riemannian metrics, Levi-Civita connections and geodesics---also descends along Q-charts. More precisely, structures on $Y$ correspond bijectively to structures on $X$ that are invariant under the pseudogroup $Ψ(φ)$ of fibre-preserving transitions, and torsion-freeness, effectivity, flatness, fullness, the Levi-Civita property and the geodesic equation are preserved in both directions. The key input is that the iterated tangent bundles $T^nX$ and their fibre products $T_kX$ are the pullbacks along $φ$ of the corresponding bundles over $Y$.
  For quotients by principal actions of diffeologically discrete groups, we lift geodesics globally, prove that the class of elastic spaces on which $\R$ is a curve object is closed under such quotients, and show that complete geodesic flows descend. As an application, we determine the Riemannian geometry of the irrational tori $\Tor_α=\R/(\Z+α\Z)$ completely. Let $\vartheta$ be the one-form on $\Tor_α$ induced by $dt$ on $\R$. Every Riemannian metric is a positive constant multiple of $\vartheta^2$, and the vertical connections form a one-parameter family of flat effective connections, a single member of which is the unique Levi-Civita connection of every metric. This connection has a complete geodesic flow, yet the induced pseudo-distance vanishes identically.

</details>


### [315] [Rigidity of volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in high dimensional Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01392)
*Sanghun Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文对$(n+1)$维黎曼流形中极小化自由边界超曲面建立体积估计，并在等号情形给出刚性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自由边界极小超曲面的定量性质（体积/面积上界）与其达到上界时的流形结构，是极小曲面理论与刚性问题交叉中的自然问题。

Method: 对稳定极小自由边界超曲面，利用其稳定性（第二变分非负）导出以流形标量曲率和边界$\partial M$第二基本形式控制的体积估计；进而分析估计中的等号情形。

Result: 得到了用$M$的标量曲率与$\partial M$的第二基本形式表达的稳定极小自由边界超曲面的体积估计。

Conclusion: 在体积估计取等号的情形下，证明了对$M$的相应刚性结论，即此类流形必为特定标准模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a volume estimate and an associated rigidity result for volume-minimizing free boundary hypersurfaces in an $(n+1)$-dimensional Riemannian manifold $M$. First, we derive a volume estimate for stable minimal free boundary hypersurfaces in terms of the scalar curvature of $M$ and the second fundamental form of the boundary $\partial M$. We then prove rigidity results of $M$ in the corresponding equality cases.

</details>


### [316] [The sphere is the only closed surface satisfying the fixed-width Archimedean property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01550)
*Mijia Lai*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在$\mathbb R^3$中的连通光滑闭嵌入曲面里，阿基米德"球带面积等于$2\pi h$"的等面积性质，只需对某一个固定宽度成立，即可唯一刻画单位球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿基米德发现单位球面被两平行平面截出的球带面积只依赖两平面间距$h$（等于$2\pi h$），与位置无关。本文探究该经典性质是否可作为曲面的特征刻画，并希望以尽可能弱的条件（单个固定宽度而非所有宽度）唯一确定单位球面。

Method: 在$\mathbb R^3$中考察连通光滑闭嵌入曲面；对某一固定宽度$h$，假设该曲面被任意相距$h$的平行平面所夹的球带区域面积恒为$2\pi h$，通过几何/分析手段（如面积公式、支撑函数与曲率分析）证明该曲面必为单位球面。

Result: 证明了对单个固定宽度成立阿基米德等面积性质，就足以在连通光滑闭嵌入曲面中特征化单位球面。

Conclusion: 阿基米德的球带面积性质具有强刻画性：无需对所有宽度成立，仅固定一个宽度即可唯一确定单位球面。

Abstract: Among Archimedes' many celebrated discoveries is a striking fact: the region of the unit sphere between two parallel planes, both meeting the sphere and separated by a distance $h$, has area $2πh$. We prove that, among connected smooth closed embedded surfaces in $\mathbb R^3$, this property characterizes the unit sphere for a single fixed width.

</details>


### [317] [Solvable Algebraic Generalized Ricci Solitons in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01608)
*Lorenzo Mucciante*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明可解李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的新结构结果，讨论低维例子，并完成三维及四维可解幺模李代数上的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解可解李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的结构性质与存在性，并在低维情形给出完整分类。

Method: 从结构定理入手，证明非平凡可解代数广义Ricci孤立子必为扩张型；对阿贝尔李代数的一维扩张给出存在性必要条件；随后按等距与尺度进行分类。

Result: 证明所有非平凡解必为扩张型，给出阿贝尔李代数一维扩张上存在的必要条件，并完成三维李代数及四维可解幺模李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子的分类。

Conclusion: 可解李代数上代数广义Ricci孤立子具有刚性结构，低维分类为其存在性与几何性质提供完整刻画。

Abstract: We prove new structural results for algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on solvable Lie algebras and we discuss the existence of examples in low dimensions. We establish that all non-trivial solvable algebraic generalized Ricci solitons must be expanding and give necessary conditions for the existence of such solitons on one-dimensional extensions of abelian Lie algebras. Finally, we classify algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on three-dimensional Lie algebras, and algebraic generalized Ricci solitons on four-dimensional solvable unimodular Lie algebras, up to isometry and scaling.

</details>


### [318] [Finite Time Singularities of the Kähler Ricci Flow on Compact Kähler Surfaces are of Type I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01643)
*Yeyun Xu,Linfeng Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在紧Kähler曲面上，对有限时间体积塌缩的Kähler–Ricci流建立了第一类曲率界，无需对称性假设，也不要求初始Kähler类为有理类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体积塌缩情形下曲率可能爆破，第一类曲率界的建立对理解奇点形成与流的结构至关重要；既有结果往往依赖对称性假设或初始Kähler类为有理类的限制，本文旨在移除这些假设。

Method: 证明结合局部辛拓扑与梯度Kähler–Ricci收缩孤子（shrinkers）的分类，并借助Bamler的紧性与结构理论获取所需的收缩极限。

Result: 在紧Kähler曲面上，对有限时间体积塌缩的Kähler–Ricci流得到了第一类曲率界，且不施加对称性假设，初始Kähler类无需为有理类。

Conclusion: 该结果表明在紧Kähler曲面体积塌缩的Kähler–Ricci流中曲率保持第一类控制，推广了既有结果，并为奇点分析与收缩孤子分类、辛拓扑方法的结合提供了新思路。

Abstract: We establish a Type I curvature bound for finite-time volume-collapsing Kähler--Ricci flows on compact Kähler surfaces. No symmetry assumption is imposed, and the initial Kähler class need not be rational. The proof combines local symplectic topology with the classification of gradient Kähler--Ricci shrinking solitons. Bamler's compactness and structure theory provides the shrinking limits used in the argument.

</details>


### [319] [Classification of quadratically pinched translators in higher codimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01713)
*Debora Impera,Michele Rimoldi,Francesco Ruatta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究任意余维数下平均曲率流完全平移孤子，在第二基本形式二次夹紧条件下证明Andrews-Baker阈值的余维数约化定理，并进一步得到刚性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进低维情形下由古代平均曲率流余维数约化结果所给出的夹紧范围，并在无熵假设下刻画平移孤子的刚性。

Method: 将平移孤子视为加权极小子流形，结合漂移拉普拉斯算子的Simons型恒等式、精细梯度估计及Omori-Yau极大值原理，采用纯椭圆方法证明。

Result: 若完全n维连通平移孤子满足 |B|^2 ≤ (4/(3n)-ε0)|H|^2，则或为仿射n-平面，或处处|H|>0且包含于(n+1)维仿射子空间；在更强夹紧 |B|^2 ≤ (c_n-ε0)|H|^2（c_n=min{4/(3n),1/(n-2)}, n≥3；n=2时c_n=2/3）下，平移孤子或为仿射平面，或为包含于(n+1)维仿射子空间的bowl孤子。

Conclusion: 在Andrews-Baker阈值处建立了平移孤子的余维数约化与刚性定理，且无需熵假设。

Abstract: We study complete translating solitons for the mean curvature flow in arbitrary codimension under quadratic pinching of the second fundamental form. We prove a codimension reduction theorem at the Andrews-Baker threshold: if a complete $n$-dimensional connected translator isometrically immersed in $(\mathbb{R}^{n+p},\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle)$ satisfies \[ |B|^2\leq \left(\frac{4}{3n}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)|H|^2 \] for some $\varepsilon_{0}>0$, then either it is an affine $n$-plane or $|H|>0$ everywhere and the translator is contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. In particular, this improves in low dimensions the pinching range previously obtained from codimension reduction results for ancient mean curvature flows. Our proof is purely elliptic: viewing translators as weighted minimal submanifolds, we combine Simons-type identities for the drift Laplacian with refined gradient estimates and an Omori-Yau maximum principle. As a consequence, under the stronger pinching condition \[ |B|^2\leq (c_n-\varepsilon_{0})|H|^2,\qquad c_n=\min\left\{\frac{4}{3n},\frac{1}{n-2}\right\} \] for $n\geq3$, with $c_n=2/3$ when $n=2$, we obtain a rigidity theorem: the translator is either an affine plane or a bowl soliton contained in an $(n+1)$-dimensional affine subspace. No entropy assumption is required.

</details>


### [320] [Hessian manifolds of positive constant Hessian sectional curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01737)
*Hakobi Sakamoto*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在正曲率情形下分类了常Hessian截面曲率的标准Hessian流形模型，方法是在具阿贝尔Lie群Hamilton作用的Kähler流形商流形上引入Hessian结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hessian几何被视为Kähler几何的实类比，其Hessian截面曲率对应Kähler几何中的全纯截面曲率。Furuhata与Kurose（2013）已分类非正曲率情形下的常Hessian截面曲率标准模型，但正曲率情形尚未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在配备适当阿贝尔Lie群Hamilton作用的Kähler流形之商流形上引入Hessian结构，并将标准模型实现为这类商流形。

Result: 完成了正曲率情形下标准模型的分类。

Conclusion: 通过Kähler流形商流形上的Hessian结构构造，给出了常Hessian截面曲率正曲率情形标准模型的完整分类，补全了Furuhata–Kurose的结论。

Abstract: Hessian structure on a manifold is a geometric structure consisting of a pair $(\nabla,g)$ of an affine connection $\nabla$ and a Riemannian metric $g$ satisfying certain compatibility conditions. It is also known as a dually flat structure in information geometry. Hessian geometry is considered to be a real analogue of Kähler geometry; in particular, Hessian sectional curvature corresponds to holomorphic sectional curvature in Kähler geometry. The standard models of Hessian manifolds with constant Hessian sectional curvature were classified by Furuhata and Kurose (2013) in the nonpositive curvature case, whereas the positive curvature case is open. In this paper, we classify the standard models in the positive curvature case. For this end, we introduce Hessian structure on quotient manifolds of Kähler manifolds equipped with suitable Hamiltonian actions of abelian Lie groups and realizing the standard models as such quotient manifolds.

</details>


### [321] [Quasi-contact metric structures from Sasaki-Einstein metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02073)
*Beniamino Cappelletti-Montano,Antonio De Nicola*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文首次构造了维数≥5的奇数维上非接触度量的拟接触度量结构，并在S^5上给出齐性例子，同时导出Killing特征向量场情形下的Ricci曲率判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟接触度量结构是接触度量结构的自然推广，但此前尚不清楚是否存在真正非接触的拟接触度量结构，本文旨在回答这一存在性问题并刻画其性质。

Method: 在维数≥5的奇数维上构造非接触的拟接触度量结构；在5维时通过形变Sasaki-Einstein SU(2)-结构得到含Killing特征向量场的单参数族；对一般维数考察具Killing特征向量场的拟接触度量结构，并推导其成为K-接触的Ricci曲率判据。

Result: 在每个维数≥5的奇数维上构造出非接触的拟接触度量结构；在5维得到单参数族例子，并在S^5上给出紧致齐性例子；得到K-接触性的Ricci曲率判据。

Conclusion: 拟接触度量结构确实比接触度量结构更一般，二者存在实质差异；Ricci曲率可作为判定其是否退化为K-接触的有效准则。

Abstract: We construct the first examples of quasi-contact metric structures that are not contact metric in every odd dimension greater than or equal to $5$. In dimension $5$, we construct a one-parameter family of such examples with a Killing characteristic vector field by deforming Sasaki-Einstein $\mathrm{SU}(2)$-structures. This yields compact, homogeneous examples on $S^5$. In arbitrary dimensions, we investigate quasi-contact metric structures with a Killing characteristic vector field and derive a Ricci curvature criterion for the structure to be K-contact.

</details>


### [322] [The Morse index of prismatic free boundary minimal surfaces in the unit ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02078)
*Hung Tran*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了球体中具有棱柱群对称性的亏格为零、含b个边界分支的嵌入自由边界极小曲面的Morse指数，证明其指数为2b、零度为3，并将指数形式的负空间作为对称群的表示加以刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前除赤道圆盘与临界悬链面外，几乎没有球体中自由边界极小曲面的Morse指数被精确确定。作者希望精确计算一大类具有棱柱对称性的FBMS的Morse指数与零度，并借助对称性分析其指标的表示结构，从而扩展对该类极小曲面稳定性的理解。

Method: 核心方法是等变比较（equivariant comparison）：利用有限群表示论，在面积与能量之间建立比较关系，并借助圆域（circle domains）按对称类型对Teichmüller方向进行计数。结合棱柱群的作用、边界分支的循环置换与赤道面反射等对称性假设展开分析。

Result: 证明该类曲面的Morse指数为2b，零度为3；并确定了指数形式的负空间在对称群下的表示。所涵盖的例子包括Karpukhin–Kusner–McGrath–Stern的b-noids，以及大b情形下Folha–Pacard–Zolotareva的亏格零曲面。此外还给出了具有旋转或多面体对称性曲面的指数下界。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了除赤道圆盘和临界悬链面之外首批Morse指数被精确确定的球体FBMS，其指数为2b、零度为3；等变比较方法结合有限群表示论为处理此类对称极小曲面问题提供了新工具，并对更一般对称情形的指数给出下界。

Abstract: For integer $b\ge3$, let $\Sig\subset\Ball$ be an embedded free boundary minimal surface (FBMS) of genus zero with $b$ boundary components. Suppose that $\Sig$ is invariant under the prismatic group of order $4b$, the rotation of order $b$ permutes the boundary components cyclically, and the reflection in the equatorial plane preserves each of them. We show that the Morse index of $\Sig$ is $2b$ and its nullity is three; moreover, we determine the negative space of the index form as a representation of the symmetry group. Examples include the $b$-noids of Karpukhin, Kusner, McGrath, and Stern and, for large $b$, the genus-zero surfaces of Folha, Pacard, and Zolotareva. To our knowledge, apart from the equatorial disc and the critical catenoid, these are the first FBMS in the ball whose Morse indices are known precisely. There are also lower bounds for the index of surfaces with rotational or polyhedral symmetry. The main new ingredient is an equivariant comparison, using the representation theory of finite groups, between area and energy, in which the Teichmüller directions are counted by symmetry type using circle domains.

</details>


### [323] [Uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02164)
*Ronan J. Conlon,Alix Deruelle*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子（shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton）的孤子向量场在双全纯映射推前意义下唯一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立子向量场是Kähler-Ricci孤子的核心结构，但其选取是否唯一此前尚不明确。研究其唯一性有助于厘清孤子的内在几何性质，并为进一步的分类工作提供基础。

Method: 通过对完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子上的孤子向量场进行分析，讨论其与双全纯映射（biholomorphism）推前之间的关系，从而建立唯一性结论。

Result: 得到结论：完备收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的孤子向量场在双全纯映射推前的意义下是唯一的。

Conclusion: 该唯一性结果表明孤子向量场在几何上是本质确定的，为收缩梯度Kähler-Ricci孤子的结构研究与分类提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: We establish the uniqueness of the soliton vector field of a complete shrinking gradient Kähler-Ricci soliton up to pushforward by biholomorphism.

</details>


### [324] [Ahlfors--Weill Extension, Asymptotically Conformal Curves and Epstein--Poincaré Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02171)
*Ming Hong Tee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个局部拟反射，借助修正Ahlfors经典估计，证明由渐近共形曲线围成的区域其双曲度量与拟双曲度量渐近相似；并结合Epstein曲面的几何重释，得到互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近任意接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ahlfors–Weill构造将Schwarzian范数小于1/2的单叶函数显式延拓为Riemann球面上的拟共形同胚，并可通过Epstein曲面几何理解；对渐近共形曲线，单值化映射可延拓到更大圆盘，且可用Loewner理论验证其拟共形性。作者希望构造相应的局部拟反射，用以研究渐近共形曲线所围区域的度量与曲面性质。

Method: 构造一个具有此类延拓的局部拟反射；利用该局部拟反射修正Ahlfors的经典估计；再结合Epstein曲面对延拓的几何重释进行分析。

Result: 证明由渐近共形曲线围成的区域具有渐近相似的双曲度量与拟双曲度量；并证明互补区域的Epstein-Poincaré曲面在曲线附近任意接近。

Conclusion: 局部拟反射为渐近共形曲线区域的度量比较与Epstein曲面逼近提供了统一工具，深化了对Ahlfors–Weill延拓几何意义的理解。

Abstract: The Ahlfors--Weill construction explicitly extends a univalent function $f$ with Schwarzian norm less than $1/2$ to a quasiconformal homeomorphism of the Riemann sphere, an extension that can be understood geometrically through Epstein surfaces. For asymptotically conformal curves, the uniformization map can be extended to a larger disk, and Loewner theory can be used to verify that this extension is quasiconformal. In this paper, we construct a local quasireflection with such an extension. Using this local quasireflection, we modify classical estimates of Ahlfors and show that domains bounded by asymptotically conformal curves have asymptotically similar hyperbolic and quasihyperbolic metrics. Furthermore, by combining these estimates with a geometric reinterpretation of the extension through Epstein surfaces, we show that the Epstein-Poincaré surfaces of the complementary domains are arbitrarily close to one another near the curve.

</details>


<div id='math.SG'></div>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [Biran decomposition and relative symplectic cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01334)
*Hong-kwon Jo,Jungsoo Kang*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文计算了Biran分解中圆子丛的相对辛上同调，并据此证明超临界半径圆丛是重子集，从而在哈密顿微分同胚群中得到无限维拟平坦结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究单调整辛流形中辛超平面截面补集的Liouville骨架与圆丛的辛拓扑性质，尤其是相对辛上同调及其在辛刚性中的应用。

Method: 利用Biran分解定理将M分解为D上的辛圆盘丛与M\D的骨架；计算该圆盘丛的圆子丛相对辛上同调（系数为Z），并用以D的量子上同调表示；分析其相对于临界半径的依赖性。

Result: 对一大类(M,D)，证明超临界半径的相对辛上同调在Z上不消失；因此超临界半径圆丛是重子集，导出关于Hofer度量和Z上谱度量的哈密顿微分同胚群中的无限维拟平坦；若deg D=k>1，则M\D的骨架在Z/kZ上是SH-full，因而在M中不可位移。

Conclusion: 相对辛上同调为研究Biran分解中的圆丛与骨架提供了有效工具，揭示了重子集与辛刚性现象，并建立了量子上同调与辛位移性质之间的联系。

Abstract: Let $(M,ω)$ be a closed monotone integral symplectic manifold with monotonicity constant $τ>0$, and let $D\subset M$ be a symplectic hyperplane section of degree $0<k<τ$. By Biran's decomposition theorem, $M$ is the union of a symplectic disk bundle over $D$ and the skeleton of the complement $M\setminus D$ equipped with its Liouville structure. In this paper, we compute the relative symplectic cohomology of circle subbundles of this disk bundle over $\mathbb{Z}$ in terms of the quantum cohomology of $D$. This relative symplectic cohomology depends on the radius of the circle bundle relative to a certain critical radius. For a broad class of pairs $(M,D)$, we prove that the relative symplectic cohomology over $\mathbb{Z}$ does not vanish for supercritical radii. Therefore, circle bundles of supercritical radii are heavy subsets, which yields infinite dimensional quasi-flats in the Hamiltonian diffeomorphism group with respect to both the Hofer metric and the spectral metric over $\mathbb{Z}$. We also establish that if the degree $k$ of $D$ is greater than one, the skeleton of $M\setminus D$ is SH-full over $\mathbb{Z}/k\mathbb{Z}$, and hence non-displaceable in $M$ by any symplectomorphism.

</details>


### [326] [Generalized Marsden-Weinstein symplectic structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01401)
*Boris Khesin,Klas Modin,Cornelia Vizman*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文在奇异1-形式密度上定义辛结构，并在增广三元组上定义预辛结构，作为不可压缩涡膜Marsden–Weinstein结构的可压缩类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将不可压缩流体中涡膜上的Marsden–Weinstein辛结构推广到可压缩情形，并统一理解奇异涡量与相关辛结构。

Method: 在奇异1-形式密度上构造辛结构，在增广三元组上构造预辛结构；将增广三元组的等价类视为可压缩流体的特殊涡量，即全微分同胚群李代数对偶的奇异元素；并与体积保持微分同胚群的奇异涡量相关联。

Result: 所构造的结构处于两个既有框架之间：辛流形上加权各向同性子流形的Weinstein辛结构，以及涡膜上的Marsden–Weinstein辛结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为可压缩涡量提供辛/预辛框架，并统一了若干经典辛结构，揭示其相互关系与中间地位。

Abstract: We define a symplectic structure on singular 1-form densities and a presymplectic structure on augmented triples, a compressible analog of the Marsden--Weinstein symplectic structure on vortex membranes in the incompressible case. Equivalence classes of augmented triples can be regarded as special vorticities in compressible fluids, i.e., singular elements of the dual to the Lie algebra of the group of all diffeomorphisms of a manifold, while Marsden--Weinstein symplectic structure is related to singular vorticities for the group of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms. This structure on singular 1-form densities occupies an intermediate position between two other settings, which we revisit, the Weinstein symplectic structure on weighted isotropic submanifolds of symplectic manifolds and the Marsden--Weinstein symplectic structure on vortex membranes.

</details>


### [327] [Cluster structures from Legendrian double twist knots: a comparative approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02086)
*James Hughes,Jiajie Ma*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了同一光滑纽扣型不同Legendre纽结代表相关的簇代数，对包含所有可定向拉格朗日填充扭结的双扭结族，在m-分次增广簇上构造簇结构，并证明同一光滑纽结型的不同Legendre代表簇结构等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Legendre纽结的簇代数结构与其光滑纽结型、以及拉格朗日填充存在性之间的关系，刻画哪些Legendre代表具有Maslov-m精确拉格朗日填充。

Method: 对一族双扭结（含所有可定向拉格朗日填充扭结）的Legendre代表的m-分次增广簇构造簇结构，并比较同一光滑纽结型不同代表的簇结构；结合Maslov-m精确拉格朗日填充的存在性判定。

Result: 同一光滑纽结型的不同Legendre代表具有等价的簇结构；刻画了具有Maslov-m精确拉格朗日填充的代表，并为每个代表构造Catalan数目的此类填充；证明任何Maslov-0可填充代表都Legendre同构于正编织的(-1)-框架闭包；给出一个缆索扭结的两个Legendre代表，其簇结构等价且为无限型。

Conclusion: Legendre纽结相关的簇结构在光滑纽结型层面具有等价性，且Maslov-m填充的存在性与正编织闭合表示密切相关；缆索扭结亦可出现无限型等价簇结构。

Abstract: We undertake a systematic comparison of cluster algebras associated to Legendrian knots of the same smooth knot type. We describe a cluster structure on the m-graded augmentation variety of Legendrian representatives of a family of double twist knots that includes all orientably Lagrangian fillable twist knots. For different Legendrian representatives of the same smooth knot type, we show that these cluster structures are equivalent. Along the way, we characterize the representatives that admit Maslov-m exact Lagrangian fillings, construct a Catalan number of such fillings for each representative, and show that any Maslov-0 fillable representative is Legendrian isotopic to the (-1)-framed closure of a positive braid. Finally, we give two Legendrian representatives of a cabled twist knot that yield equivalent cluster structures of infinite type.

</details>
